- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine`: `HardFloat { product, sum }` (the floating-point DSP block's measured multiply
  and add), `Streaming::with_hard_float` (refused with `codegen_sva::fp_hard::HardError` when a wrapper cannot serve the
  block), `hard_float_verilog` (an engine's `logos_fmul_p`/`logos_fadd_p` replaced by `fp_hard::hard_verilog`'s
  wrappers, refused when the core reaches either) and `hard_float_model` (the `fourteennm_fp_mac` stand-in a simulation
  takes, `sim/logos_fp_mac_model.v` in the AFU tree).
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::vector`: `logos_rne_p` and `RNE_P_LATENCY`, the nearest integer in two stages.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::Streaming::with_float_rows` (`float_rows`, default `vector_side::FM_ROWS`): the
  token-rows the vector unit's side holds a float matrix's inputs for at once, 16 lanes a row, the weights read once a
  group.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::gdn`: `logos_gdn_read_w`, `logos_gdn_flush_w` (W sixteens of elements a clock, groups
  of 4W rows) in `gdn_stream_verilog`; `logos_gdn_side` takes `W` and reads the next head's query and log and folds it
  while a head's rows come back (a token the log does not flush); `rtl::engine::Streaming::{recurrence_width,
  with_recurrence_width}`. `rtl::attend::{attend_int8_p_verilog, attend_side_verilog}` (`logos_attend_int8_p`,
  `logos_attend_side`); `rtl::engine::{Streaming::{attention, with_attention}, AttendJob}`; the `d5005_afu` example's
  `--recurrence-width <w>` and `--attention`, and `scripts/d5005/build-afu.sh`'s.
- `spatial::decode_engine::machine::StoreLayout::greedy` and `StoreLayout::greedy` (the field): the logits slot a beat
  a row, `image` laying each row's `numerics::argmax` and that logit's bits in lanes 0 and 1. `ofs::write_greedy_program`
  (the head's stream entry greedy; an error when the head's matrix is not streamed); `ofs::EngineProgram::{greedy,
  store_beats}`, `engine.txt`'s `store <beats>` and `greedy 1`; the build header's `LOGOS_D5005_ROWS` and
  `LOGOS_D5005_STORE_BEATS`. `rtl::streaming::StreamEntry::greedy` (line 0's seventh word), `greedy_verilog`
  (`logos_greedy`), `GREEDY_LATENCY`; `logos_streaming_matvec_<set>` takes `greedy`; `rtl::engine::HybridJob::GREEDY`,
  `HybridJob::ENTRY` 224. The `d5005_afu` example's `--greedy` lays an engine build's store greedy and writes a greedy
  hybrid program; so does `scripts/d5005/build-afu.sh --greedy`.
- **`spatial::decode_engine::rtl::column`**: `ColumnSet` (a name, its formats, its activation words),
  `columns_verilog`/`column_verilog` (`logos_column_core_<set>`, `logos_column_<set>`, `logos_column_array_<set>`,
  `logos_column_feed_<set>`, `logos_matvec_pass`), `column_passes`, `column_clocks`, `column_array_clocks`,
  `COLUMN_VALUE_LATENCY`. **`rtl::streaming`**: `streaming_verilog` (`logos_streaming_matvec_<set>`), `TileRun` and
  `tile_run_verilog`, `act_stream_p_verilog` (`logos_act_code_p`, `logos_act_stream_p`), `ACT_CODE_LATENCY`.
  `rtl::matvec`'s `logos_matvec_partials`; `rtl::lane`'s `logos_lane_value_p` (`WORDS`) and `LANE_VALUE_LATENCY`;
  `rtl::fp32`'s pipelined `logos_fmul_p` and `logos_fadd_p`, `FMUL_LATENCY`, `FADD_LATENCY`; `rtl::ram`'s
  registered-read `logos_bram_w<W>` (`bram_name`, `bram_verilog`). **`image`**: `ImageLayout::stripe`,
  `TensorEntry::streamed`, `Placement::{block_bytes, block_major}`, `Image::locate_block`,
  `ImageError::{EmptyStripe, StreamedKernel}`; **`machine`**: `Placed::streamed`.
- The engine in the user clock and the hybrid engine: **`rtl::engine`**'s `EngineParams::streaming` and `Streaming`,
  `HybridJob`, `ENGINE_MIN_DIVIDE`, `bridge_verilog` (`logos_engine_core_ce`, `logos_engine_bridge`, `logos_engine_fast`
  under a `logos_engine` of the reference ports and the user clock's), `simulation_twin` (the engine with its core's
  clock gated to the user cycles the core acts in, which the AFU tree carries as `sim/logos_engine_gated.v` for
  `run-cosim.sh`); **`rtl::ram`**'s `logos_ram_ce_w<W>`
  (`ram_ce_name`, `ram_ce_verilog`); **`rtl::streaming`**'s `StreamEntry` and `STREAM_ENTRY_LINES`; **`rtl::column`**'s
  `ColumnSet::{hybrid, signature}`; **`rtl::ram`**'s `logos_bram_re_w<W>` (`bram_re_name`, `bram_re_verilog`);
  **`physical`**'s `Compaction` (`of`, `removed`, `place`, `find`, `bytes`, `space`, `space_bytes`) and
  `PhysicalMap::hybrid`; **`image`**'s `streamed_image_of`; **`ofs`**'s `AfuConfig::hybrid`, `write_hybrid_program`,
  `EngineProgram::streaming` and the shell's fast channel ports, the compute clock's `fedge` and `fmid`.
- The Gated-DeltaNet units for the user clock: **`rtl::gdn`**'s `gdn_stream_verilog` (`logos_gdn_read_p`, a lazily
  flushed head's read-out a beat a clock on the pipelined units, rows gathered four at a time and sent beat-major so
  each lane's sum returns to its add as the row's next term enters; `logos_gdn_flush_p`, the rows' replay of every
  logged update, stored once; `logos_gdn_fold_p`, the query folded back through the log newest token first, each token's
  decay through the pipelined exponential); **`rtl::vector`**'s `logos_exp_p` and `EXP_LATENCY`, `exp` an operand a
  clock, and `logos_delay`; **`rtl::fp32`**'s `logos_tree16_p` and `TREE16_LATENCY`; **`numerics`**' `tree`, the lanes'
  pairwise fold, public; `logos_gdn_side`, a token row's lazily flushed block after the core has logged the token: each
  value head's query and log into the fold and the flush, every row through the read-out and, when the log is full, the
  flush, the read-outs into the store and the flushed rows back as whole lines, over memory of logical lines read in
  order. The flush takes its log as the fold does (keys, values, decay exponents through its own exponential, gates).
- The hybrid engine's recurrence unit: **`rtl::engine`**'s `Streaming::{recurrence, with_recurrence}` and `GdnJob`, the
  core handing a lazily flushed memory's token-row to `logos_gdn_side` in `logos_engine_fast` once its writes have left
  and keeping no lazy row engine, log append or count of its own, the bridge crossing the card port's map;
  `logos_gdn_side` reads the log's count, appends the row's entry from the store (each field's lanes past its elements
  zeroed) and writes the count back itself, waiting for its writes to be written (`written`) before it reads the log;
  **`physical`**'s `fast_port_verilog` (`logos_fast_port`: whole logical lines read and written where the map puts them,
  a channel's consecutive lines one descriptor, the reads returned in the order asked, the writes counted until
  written); **`ofs`**'s `hybrid_shell_verilog` and the shell's fast writes (a write FIFO and a done FIFO a channel); the
  `d5005_afu` example's `--recurrence`.
- The hybrid engine's vector unit's side: **`rtl::vector`**'s `logos_recip_p`, `logos_rsqrt_p`, `logos_ln_p` and
  `logos_i2f_p` (`RECIP_LATENCY`, `RSQRT_LATENCY`, `LN_LATENCY`, `I2F_LATENCY`), `recip`, `rsqrt`, `ln` and the integer
  conversion for the user clock, an operand a clock, and `logos_exp_p`'s integer step on `logos_i2f_p` (`EXP_LATENCY`
  42); **`rtl::simd`**'s `simd_p_verilog`, `logos_lane_func_p` (`LANE_FUNC_P_LATENCY`) and `logos_simd_p`, the vector
  unit for the user clock; **`rtl::vector_side`** (`vector_side_verilog`, `logos_vector_side`); **`rtl::engine`**'s
  `Streaming::{vectors, with_vectors}` and `VectorSideJob`, the core handing each vector job over and keeping none of its
  vector-job states; the `d5005_afu` example's `--vectors`. **`rtl::fp32`**'s `logos_f32_to_f16_p`
  (`F32_TO_F16_LATENCY`), the binary16 rounding for the user clock in two clocks, which `logos_gdn_flush_p`'s stored
  halves, `logos_gdn_side`'s log entries and `logos_lane_func_p`'s half results take.
- **`rtl::simd`**'s function units in four stages: `logos_lane_func`'s `STAGED`, `LANE_FUNC_STAGES`, and `logos_simd`
  issuing a `Map`'s slices into the stages one row after another, each row written when its last slice's values are out
  and a `ScalarMap` when its value is.
- **`spatial::decode_engine::physical`**: `PhysicalMap` (`d5005`, `of`, `flat`; `locate`, `runs`, `ring_row`,
  `memory`, `log`, `cache`, `channel_bytes`), everything past the weight image dealt round the card's channels in
  granules of the engine plan's state chunk, and `Run`, one stretch of a channel; `card_port_verilog`, the
  `logos_card_port` that serves the engine's logical lines where the map puts them.
- `spatial::decode_engine::image::{image_of, machine_of}`: the weight image laid out from the machine's layout, and the
  machine's layout from a header ledger.
- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs`: SECDED lines (`LocalMemoryLine::Data512Secded`; the code `secded_line`, `secded_read`
  and `SecdedRead`, `secded_check_byte`, `SECDED_WORDS`, `SECDED_CHECKS`, and its Verilog `secded_verilog`); the engine
  build (`AfuConfig::engine`, its ops `Op::{EngineConfig, EngineTable, EngineRun, StoreRead}`, and
  `write_engine_program` with `EngineProgram`); a running command's progress records (`STATUS_RUNNING`, the shell's
  `HB`); a compute clock divided from the user clock (`AfuConfig::compute_divide`,
  `COMPUTE_CLOCK_VERILOG`, `ENGINE_USER_CLOCK_MHZ`, `ENGINE_COMPUTE_DIVIDE`). Among the emitted files the physical
  backend `sw/logos_hal_pci.c` (the card's PCI function driven directly on a host with no IOMMU), the Verilator
  backend `sim/logos_hal_dpi.c`, and on the engine build `sw/logos_step.c`.
- **`spatial::decode_engine::rtl::ram`**: `ram_verilog(widths)` (`logos_ram_w<W>`: a memory of `W`-bit words, its read
  address latched on the falling edge, its writes on the rising edge under a byte mask) and `ram_name`.
  `rtl::engine::census`, the instance statements under a module counted through the hierarchy; `EngineParams::union`,
  the one engine that runs every checkpoint of a set; `EngineParams::function_units` and `FUNCTION_UNITS`, the
  function units a row of the vector unit shares across its lanes; `logos_act_stream` in `rtl::act_quant`, the engine's
  one activation quantizer; `logos_engine`'s last port `mem_wr_ready`. `machine::{state_bytes, cache_row_bytes}`.
  `spatial::decode::GgufHeader` is a `WeightSource`: a header ledger lays a checkpoint out without its weights.
- The `d5005_afu` example builds the engine build for a set of header ledgers (`--engine`, its compute divide
  `--divide`) and writes a checkpoint's step program (`--program`).
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::simd`: `logos_simd_p` takes `PASSES` and the ports `group`, `group_rows` and
  `rd_s_pass`: an instruction over a group of passes, pass `p`'s registers from row `p·group_rows` and its scalars its
  own, a dot's or a sum's passes in subgroups of the add's latency and one. A unit of one pass is the unit as it was.
- `spatial::decode_engine::machine::VectorJob::grouped` (the job word's bit 54) and `vector::Op::{reads,
  writes_scalar}`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::{Streaming::{vector_passes, with_vector_passes}, VECTOR_PASSES_MAX}`:
  `logos_vector_side`'s `PASSES` (1 to 32), a grouped job's passes loaded each into rows of its own, run as one and stored one after another; the
  `d5005_afu` example's `--vector-passes <n>`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::Streaming::{recurrence_flushes, with_recurrence_flushes}`: each head lane's
  flushing head dealt over that many flush units by rows (`logos_gdn_flush_w`'s `ROWS`, `logos_gdn_lane`'s and
  `logos_gdn_lanes`' `FLUSHES`); the `d5005_afu` example's `--recurrence-flushes <f>`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::machine::{Fold, fold, Machine::folded}`: a matrix job's `fold` (`Put`, `Add`, `SwiGlu`; its
  word's `[31:30]`), and a step's residual adds and gated products folded into the matrix jobs that alone feed them,
  every value as it was. `rtl::engine::EngineParams::folds`, `capability::Gap::Folds`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::Streaming::{folds, with_folds, signature}` and
  `rtl::streaming::{streaming_verilog_folding, fold_lane_verilog, FOLD_LATENCY, StreamEntry::fold}`: the streaming unit
  folding a job's sums into its output slot over 1 to 16 lanes of `logos_fold_lane_p`, its stream entry's fold beside
  the greedy flag, the unit named `+folds`; the `d5005_afu` example's `--folds <lanes>` (a build) and `--folds` (a
  program).
- `spatial::decode_engine::machine::{StoreLayout::widest, STORE_ORDERS}`: the most beats a point of a step holds, and
  the orders `StoreLayout::of` tries past the widest first to lay its row that short.
- `spatial::serving::StateFlush::Trailing { tokens }`: a Gated-DeltaNet memory replayed a window behind, a `1/tokens`
  share of every head's rows a token; priced in `PassShape::of` (a window more log read and held, each token's share
  read and written back).
- `spatial::decode_engine::model::{Replay, TRAILING_TOKENS}`: the lazy flush's schedule a token (`Replay::at`, the rows
  that read through the newer tokens, the rows replayed and by how many tokens, the tokens dropped) and the log's
  longest (`Replay::capacity`); `EngineNumerics::all` gains the trailing points.
- `spatial::decode_engine::numerics::gdn_fold_trailing`: one newest-first walk giving the fold over a log's newer
  tokens and over the whole log.
- The machine's lazy log is a ring of `Replay::capacity` slots, the count line holding its oldest slot (four bytes
  after the count).
- `rtl::engine::EngineParams::delta` is `(width, StateFlush)` (was the log's token count); `rtl::engine::{logged,
  recurrence_log, behind}`; `EngineParams::streaming_signature` (the header's `LOGOS_D5005_STREAMING`, `+behind<T>` for a
  recurrence flushed a window behind); `capability::Gap::Recurrence` names the flush.
- `rtl::gdn::{lanes_trailing_verilog, flush_m_verilog}`: the head lanes of a trailing flush (`logos_gdn_lane_t`, the
  coordinator's ring at `WINDOW`) and `logos_gdn_flush_m`; `logos_gdn_fold_m` gains `TRAILING`, its `older` input and its
  newer folds (`ynew_beat`, `cnew_beat`).
- The `d5005_afu` example's `--recurrence-behind <t>` (a build) and `--behind <t>` (a program), and `build-afu.sh
  --recurrence-behind <t>`.
- `rtl::engine::Streaming::{recurrence_credit, with_recurrence_credit}` and `rtl::gdn::{LANE_CREDIT, LANE_CREDIT_MAX}`:
  the lines the recurrence's coordinator and head lanes ask ahead of those they have taken (`OUTSTANDING`, now 9 bits
  wide on `logos_gdn_side`, `logos_gdn_lanes`, `logos_gdn_lane` and `logos_gdn_lane_t`). The `d5005_afu` example's
  `--recurrence-credit <lines>` and `build-afu.sh --recurrence-credit <lines>`.
- `logos_gdn_lane` and `logos_gdn_lane_t` keep each head's folds in one of two buffers, so a head's rows are asked while
  the head before is still on its way through the read-out.
- `rtl::gdn::rows_verilog` (`logos_gdn_rows`): a recurrence job's rows as descriptors from copies of the rows and slots
  tables.
- `logos_gdn_lanes` takes a job (`start`, `rows`) and its rows as descriptors (`row_valid`, `row_ready`); each lane holds
  two copies of the log; `logos_gdn_lane`/`logos_gdn_lane_t` take a row by `start` while `room`, and say `done`.
- `logos_gdn_flush_m` holds three banks (`log_bank`, `row_bank` and `bank` two bits, `free` three), and
  `logos_gdn_lane_t` counts its flush banks mod 3 apart from its two fold banks.
- The `d5005_afu` example's `--function-units <n>` (`EngineParams::function_units`) and `build-afu.sh --function-units
  <n>`; the example's build form refuses a flag its usage does not name.
- `spatial::decode_engine::vector::Op::{MulAdd, MulAddScalar}` (opcode 15, `rtl::simd::{encode, decode}`), run by
  `logos_simd` and `logos_simd_p`. `vector::conv` takes no scratch register: `conv(ring, x, taps, acc, out)`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs`: the step program's profile mode (`LOGOS_PROFILE`, a job list), each job of the prompt's
  last position run alone and its cycles printed.
- **`web_host::first_paint` and `web_host::assemble_preview_painted`.** `first_paint` is the in-process render of a page's first paint that `largo build` writes into every document it ships, moved here from the CLI unchanged: `prerender_golden_lock` holds `largo build`'s document for every page the repository ships, byte for byte. `assemble_preview_painted` is `assemble_preview` with that first paint already in the frame's body, marked `data-logos-prerendered` for the page's mount to adopt, so a preview shows its page the moment the document parses. The front page's mini Studio builds its live preview with it; `assemble_preview` is unchanged. `crates/logicaffeine_tests/unit/logicaffeine_compile/web_host/first_paint_tests.rs`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs::{job_listing, job_listing_of}`: every program carries its job listing (`jobs.txt`, each
  job at the word the engine's job table opens it at), the list a card profile runs a step by; `d5005_afu
  --job-listing <program dir>` reads one back from a program's job table alone.

- The fabric capacity budget counts distributed RAM a LUT copy PER READ PORT, not once: a pool a gather's lanes read through a port a lane (Board A's 8-lane decode reads the exp table's page through eight) costs its ⌈bits/64⌉ LUTs that many times, so a LUT-RAM-bound design is refused rather than left to fail in place-and-route. `DeclaredMemory::read_ports` (a pool's `nread`, a tensor memory's one).
- **`spatial::serving`**: the price of a batched, speculative decode pass on a card that holds its token-rows and
  streams its weights — `PassShape::of(header, serving)`, `cost`, `cost_split`, `best`; `Serving` (batch, `ContextMix`,
  paged KV, `AttentionMath`, KV and state bytes, activation words and `ActScaling`, rotated KV, `Draft`, `StateFlush`,
  host memory); `Card` (memory, on-chip, array, vector and block-scale lane rates as cited `Fact`s, and `Streams`: the
  memory's share on a long read, on a state rewritten in place and on a read with its row appended, each priced on its
  own bytes, `PassShape::rewrite_bytes`); `ServingBind`; `array_passes`, `lane_cycles`.
- **`spatial::d5005`**: the Intel PAC D5005 as a decode `Chip` and a serving `Card` — `EnginePlan::{standard,
  full_beat}` (the array's DSP blocks, soft multiply-adds, block-scale lanes at `LANE_MIX`, the vector units, `Beat`,
  `state_chunk_lines`), `streams` read from `DDR_STREAMS` (the DDR4 ledger's clocks, `BeatStreams`, `StreamClocks`),
  `ladder`, `roofline`, `CONTEXT_SWEEP`, `mixed`; the example `d5005_roofline` writes
  `benchmarks/silicon/d5005/roofline.json`.
- **`spatial::decode_engine`**: `numerics` (f32, f16, per-32 and per-block activation scaling `quantize`,
  `quantize_blocks`, `ActScaling`, `CodedRow`, `dot`, `exact_sum`, the transcendentals, `hadamard`, `rope`, `conv`,
  `absmax`, `argmax`, `int8_codes`, `cache_row`, `softmax`, `attend_vector`, `quantize_head`, `int8_score`,
  `weighted_int8`, `attend_int8`, `Logged`, `gdn_fold`, `gdn_read`, `gdn_flush`),
  `model::EngineModel` (the qwen35 forward over a batch at every `EngineNumerics` knob point), `lane` (the block-scale
  lane over the array's excess sums), `vector` (the vector unit's instruction set — its rotary, arg-max, magnitude,
  int8-code and f16 instructions among them — and its micro-programs),
  `rtl::{fp32, act_quant, weights, array, lane, matvec, attend, vector, gdn, simd}` (the units in Verilog;
  `rtl::weights::units`, the decoders' software twin; `rtl::simd::encode`, the vector unit's instruction word).
- **`spatial::decode_engine::program`**: `Program`, `Command`, `Slot`, `lower(spec, numerics)`, `Program::tensors` (the
  weight stream's order), `Program::ropes` and `Program::dataflow`; the sequencer's 256-bit words, `Program::encode`
  and `Program::decode` (`Word`); `EngineModel::program` and `EngineNumerics::real_points`.
- **`spatial::decode_engine::machine`**: `Layout::of` (`Placed`, `BlockState`, `LogFields`, `LINE`, `LANES`),
  `lower(program, layout, eps)` (`Job`, `VectorJob`, `Order`, `Load`, `Put`, `Source`, `Sink`, `CachePart`, `Reg`, `VREGS`,
  `SREGS`), the sequencer's words `encode` and `decode`, `StoreLayout`, `Machine::{new, sequence, release, position, step,
  step_traced, memory}` (`Row`, `Store`, `Trace`); `model::runs`, the knob points and architectures the engine runs;
  `program::Slot::{ALL, code, of_code}`.
- **`spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine`**: `engine_verilog` (`logos_engine`: the sequencer, the vector unit, the
  array's columns, the recurrence and attention units over the store and a line port onto the card's memory, the host
  port) over `EngineParams`, `tables` (`Tables`, the tables the host writes for a step), and `compose`, Verilog sources
  as one with every module once; `rtl::simd::decode`, the vector unit's instruction words read back;
  `vector::Op::Scale`, an exact power-of-two scale.
- **`spatial::decode_engine::twin`**: `demands(spec, header, program, step)` (`Demand`, `Step`, `SequenceRows`),
  `schedule(demands, card, window)` (`Timeline`, `Span`, `Bound`), `predicted` (`Predicted`, `PredictedRow`),
  `serving_of`, `steady_steps`; the example `d5005_twin` writes `benchmarks/silicon/d5005/predicted.json`.
- `model::spec::SpecSource` and `ModelSpec::from_source`: a spec read from anything that describes a checkpoint, the
  GGUF one way among them; `spatial::decode::GgufHeader::{source, spec}` (`HeaderSource`) read a header ledger as one.
- `model::quant::GgmlType::{Iq4Nl, Iq4Xs}` and `KVALUES_IQ4NL`: IQ4_NL and IQ4_XS blocks, dequantized as ggml does.
- **`spatial::decode_engine::ofs`**: the D5005 accelerator's OFS build tree. `afu_sources(&AfuConfig)` writes the PIM boundary, the shell and its dual-clock FIFOs (`logos_dcfifo_mlab`, `logos_dcfifo_m20k`), the stream engines, the registers (`REGISTERS`: the OFS feature header and AFU ID at words 0 to 4, then the doorbell, arguments and status record) and the bring-up core's commands (`Op`), the AFU JSON, the FIFOs' timing constraints, the clock-domain map (`CLOCK_DOMAINS_FILE`) and the C header; `LocalMemoryLine` carries the banks' 512 data bits or 576 with the ECC lanes; `AfuConfig::d5005` and `AFU_UUID`. The tree's `sw/` holds the host interface (`logos_hal.h`), its OPAE backend and the bring-up receipt; its `sim/` holds the receipt's VPI backend, the co-simulation top and the memory channels' model (`cosim_user_clock_ps`). Example `d5005_afu` writes the tree.
- **`codegen_sva::ddr4_jedec`**: a DDR4 channel clocked from its controller's configuration and held to JESD79-4 —
  `Ddr4Grade` (`D5005_EMIF`, read from the D5005 shell's EMIF configuration) and `Ddr4Grade::at`; `Ddr4Timing` (the
  clocked rules, `read_to_write`, `write_to_read`, `write_to_precharge`, `lines`, `controller_ck`, `peak_bytes_per_s`);
  `locate` (the controller's CS_R_B_C_BG address order); `Ddr4Cmd`, `Ddr4Timed`; `BusChecker` and `check_bus` (every
  bank, bank-group and bus rule, tFAW, tRFC and the refresh account, returning `ddr2_jedec::Violation`); `Scheduler`
  (`line`, `line_at` for a line taken at its arrival clock, each returning its column clock), `schedule` and `measure`
  (the in-order controller model, row commands `LOOKAHEAD` lines ahead of the data, its commands released in clock
  order so a stream of any length is checked in bounded memory); `stream_lines`; `verilog_model`
  (`logos_ddr4_channel_model`, the controller model behind an Avalon-MM slave for simulation, its log of every line and
  command, `LOGGED_KINDS`).
- **`spatial::decode_engine::{image, ddr}`**: `image::weight_image(&TensorTable, order, &ImageLayout)` (tiles of whole
  rows dealt to the channels least loaded first; `Image::{locate, fill, write_channels, tile_table, first_bad_tile, row}`;
  `TensorTable::{from_rows, of_gguf, of_header, read_order}`; `ImageLayout::d5005(Beat)`; `crc32c`; `image_ledger`);
  `ddr::ddr_ledger` (a decode pass's streams priced on one D5005 channel; `stream_bytes`). The examples
  `d5005_weight_image` and `d5005_ddr` write `benchmarks/silicon/d5005/weight_image.json` and `ddr4_efficiency.json`;
  `d5005_weight_image -- --write <gguf> <dir> [data64|full72]` writes one checkpoint's image as the card's loader takes
  it and reads it back (every tile's check word, every tensor's first and last rows against the checkpoint).
- **`spatial::decode_engine::rtl::stream`**: the image stream engine — `verilog(&ImageLayout)` (`logos_image_stream`:
  tile descriptors in, the shell's channel read and its lines, 64-byte beats out tagged with tensor, first row and
  offset, the tile's check-word verdict on its last beat), `descriptors(&Image, channel)`, `beats` (its software twin),
  `crc32c_update`, `crc_masks` (the CRC equations), `TileDescriptor`, `Beat`, `BEAT_BYTES`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs`: `ChannelModel` (`AfuConfig::channel_model`: the co-simulation's channels a memory that
  waits at random, or DDR4-timed), `Op::{HostToMemPacked, MemToHostPacked}`, `sw/logos_core.h` (the core's commands),
  `sw/logos_load.c` (the image loader), `sim/logos_ddr4_channel_model.v`; the VPI backend runs any program
  (`logos_program_main`, its arguments the `+arg=` plusargs).
- `BuiltinId::OrderedInfinitesimal` (wire id appended), a field maker for `resolve_division`, and the standpoint argument of the nonstandard predicates. The supertask engine writes a supertask nested in each pass as its map (`supertask::clock::compose_nested`): one whose pass is a polynomial map of its variables and of the names the passes change, read over the monomials that close under it (`linearized`), so its power is computed once ahead of the outer end state; the same closure gives a polynomial body its exact closed form. `supertask::stage::ahead` counts a binding the pass leaves behind as changing, so nothing that reads it runs ahead of it.
- **A fabric refuses a program too big for its part before a line of RTL is printed.** `SpatialTarget::part` names the programmable part a fabric is printed onto (set on the xc7 presets, read from `spatial::part::PartResources`); `spatial::lower::fabric::budget::{fabric_usage, fabric_memories, check_capacity}` count exactly the memories `print_site` and `print_pool` declare (a tensor's rows at its row pitch, through the one `tensor_words` the printer and the hex images read), each resource a LOWER bound so a refusal is sound — RAMB18s from the block-styled bits over a RAMB18's 18,432, LUT-RAM LUTs from the distributed bits over 64, flip-flops the registers-styled bits, DSP slices a MAC an array cell. `SpatialLowerError::OverCapacity` names the part, every resource it overruns with its need and have, and the memories that need the most. A 768×1024 int8 weight is refused on the Artix-7 100T (342 > 270 RAMB18) and held on the 200T; the tiny decode step needs no block RAM on either, as the routed Board A image used none. The storage choice `memory_decl` makes is lifted into a `Storage` enum the printer and the budget share, byte-identical to before.
- **`spatial::part::PartResources`**: what a programmable part holds — its logic cells, flip-flops, DSP multipliers and every kind of on-chip memory (LUT-RAM, block RAM, UltraRAM) — as facts with sources, for `xc7a100t`, `xc7a200t`, `xczu7ev` and the Stratix 10 SX 2800 (`sx2800`, counted in ALMs, with M20K and MLAB blocks). The one table a fabric lowering spends as a budget and a decode pricer reads as capacity; each figure quotes its datasheet (DS180/UG473/UG474/UG479 for the 7-series parts, DS891/UG573/UG574/UG579 for the Zynq UltraScale+, the Stratix 10 device overview and product table for the SX 2800), and `MemoryKind::is_block`, `block_bytes` and `block_read_bytes_per_cycle` read the dedicated-memory totals.
- **`spatial::fpga_decode`**: the ZCU104 (Zynq UltraScale+ XCZU7EV) as a decode `Chip`. `zcu104_points` names the three PL operating points that bound a batch-1 decode on the board — four 128-bit AXI HP ports at 100 MHz (the FPT'19 ZCU102 measurement), three at 300 MHz, and the DDR4-2400 peak — `zcu104_at` builds the chip at each (its SRAM the part's block RAMs and UltraRAMs read at the PL clock, its DDR the board's 2 GB, its int8 rate two MACs a DSP), and `zcu104_sizing` prices every checkpoint under `models/` at each point. Added to `Chip::catalog`.
- `codegen_sva::systolic::{bist_tile_weights, emit_systolic_bist_wrapper, emit_systolic_os_bist_wrapper}`: the self-test wrappers the pipelined tiles are signed off in, and the weights they bake, moved from the `emit_systolic_pipelined_tile` example into the library. The output-stationary wrapper folds its accumulators row by row through registered row words and two registered half-folds.
- **`BuiltinId::ExactPower`** (`exactPower`, the extension's, on every tier): `**` to an exact exponent; `resolve_division` writes it for a `**` whose exponent is an exact field value, unless the base is a float written as one or the program defines a function of that name.
- **The comparative-synthesis bench's harness** (`codegen_sva::qor_harness`): each kernel's candidates — LOGOS, HLS (Bambu, Calyx) and IP (Gemmini, Vortex, Spatial) — described by protocol (registered, start/done, pipelined, go/done, enable/done, load/go/read, memory-mapped), materialised inside one harness whose LFSR steps only when a candidate takes an operation and whose MISR folds only its results, so every candidate of a kernel folds the golden signature the Rust oracle predicts; the out-of-context fold wrapper each is synthesised in. K1's LOGOS candidates include the decision compiled for standard cells (`k1_logos_cells`, three stages from its ports; `k1_logos_hoisted`, one clock) and K6's the SIMT step on ports (`k6_logos_ports`).
- **A DDR2 traffic gate.** `codegen_sva::ddr2_jedec` clocks a speed grade's datasheet minimums (`Ddr2Grade::at`) and holds a bus trace to JESD79-2 (`check_bus`: bank state, tRCD/tRP/tRAS/tRC/tRRD/tFAW, CAS spacing, turnarounds, auto-precharge, refresh debt up to `REFRESH_POSTPONE_MAX`); `codegen_sva::ddr2_traffic` streams 64-bit words through each K7 controller to a model of the Nexys A7's part and judges the log: every word read back, every request matched to its column command, latencies and spans.
- **`ddr2_dfi_ctrl`** (`codegen_sva::ddr2_dfi`): a DDR2 controller on LiteDRAM's native port and DFI at 1:2 — one in-order queue whose rows open ahead in other banks, a row kept open only while a waiting request reads it, column commands on phase 0 and row and refresh commands on phase 1, every rule a down-counter baked from the clocked grade, refresh postponed while requests wait and repaid when idle.
- **`codegen_sva::mul_tree`**: a word multiply as an aligned tree of adds over its partial products, each add only as wide as the bits it can change, registered after the levels a caller names; no `*`.
- **Pipelined LOGOS candidates.** `fabric_pipe::decision_ops` lowers a decision fabric into the pipeline's op vocabulary (a compare as the overflow-corrected sign of a subtract, an equality as an OR tree into the sign, a select as a masked merge), each op emitted once, cut at `DECISION_CHAIN_BUDGET` — one carry chain to a stage — for a fabric with carry chains; for standard cells, `select_hoist::hoist_selects` cofactors a decision's selects out of the arithmetic that reads them (Shannon expansion on canonical predicates, hash-consed, its tree ordered latest predicate first, exact at every datapath width: K1's decision from six carry chains in series to two), and `fabric_pipe` reads a pipeline's operands from its ports (`Inputs::Ports`), folds a sum into every sum that reads it (`SumSharing::Fold`), resolves a compressor tree with one add synthesis implements (`FinalAdd::Native`) and cuts a decision at `DECISION_CELLS_BUDGET`; `keccak_rtl::emit_ntt_butterfly_pipelined_verilog` cuts the Montgomery butterfly at any `NttCuts` (the product an aligned add tree, the reduction subtracting only high halves); `keccak_rtl::emit_keccak_core_half_verilog` registers each Keccak round after theta; `gpu_simt_rtl::LaneArith::Streamed` shares one pipelined multiply among the SIMT lanes, each lane's product issued as its operand loads; `simt_ports::emit_simt_ports_verilog` takes the SIMT engine's `a·x + b` step on ports — every operand at once, each lane's product an aligned add tree, a step every clock.
- `flow_config::ClosureSettings::comparative`: a block measured beside others completes a setup miss, records its synthesis findings, repairs a last antenna pin with a diode and is synthesised for delay (`COMPARATIVE_SYNTH_STRATEGY`), with `COMPARATIVE_HOLD_MARGIN_NS`.
- The output-stationary systolic array streams tiles (`emit_systolic_os_streaming_verilog`, an accumulator-clear port), de-skewing only the columns a tile shorter than the array is wide would miss (`os_streaming_deskew`: none for a square array); the Plasticine report lists a routed-network failure as uncalibrated with what happened; tensor recognition and the Spatial lowering carry the twins of Spatial's DotProduct, OuterProduct, Kmeans, GDA and BlackScholes apps.
- **Register-stage pipelining** (`codegen_sva::fabric_pipe`): a residual circuit lowered to carry-save form (multiplies and sums as 3:2 compressor trees, the last two words resolved by a Kogge-Stone add, at any width), cut into register stages under a depth budget at the fewest live register bits (a min-cut closure), and emitted with its cycle model. `fabric_rtl::emit_loop_fabric_verilog_pipelined` keeps a counted loop's terms a stage ahead in carry-save accumulators behind a parallel-prefix counter; `emit_csa_multiplier_verilog` is the compressor-tree multiplier the FPU's significand product instantiates.
- `semantics::collections::IntegerPlace::{ALL, code, of_code}`.
- `analysis::types::TypeEnv::infer_expr_with`, typing a read of a record's field through a resolver.
- `analysis::types::LogosType::{number_join, holds_rational}`.
- A list literal of integers and rationals is a list of rationals, and a run or a copy of a list of the exact extension's values is one of its values (`analysis::tower`).
- The supertask engine counts a loop whose guard compares terms linear in a counter it steps (`supertask::linear`, the search's linear reading shared) with `firstExit`, and takes a counter stepped by a constant for a clock when the guard reads a value the exact extension reaches. `BuiltinId::FirstExit`.
- **Real numbers a function of the program defines** (`BuiltinId::DefinedReal`, `definedReal`). `analysis::defined_real` refuses, before the program runs, a call of any other form than the phrase writes and a function that takes another shape, answers another type, does more than answer or reads a variable of the program. `semantics::defined` reads the function in a context of its own — a child interpreter over the same program, a fresh machine over the same bytecode — under a `RunScope` the engine opens. The Rust tier defines it through `LogosValue::defined_real`; the C backends, RISC-V and linked WebAssembly through `logos_rt_value_defined_real`, calling the program's closure back through a reader each emits only for a program that defines a real (`__logos_defines_N`, `lc_defines`, `logos_tower_define`'s table of answer shapes, the module's `logos_program_defines`). `BuiltinId::answers_exact` is the rule every bytecode backend's tower analysis reads. The C tree-shaking keeps a function a defined real names. A value handed to a task is checked as it crosses on the Rust tier (`codegen::tower::crossing_to_a_task`) and on RISC-V (`logos_tower_crossing`), as the VM's spawn materializes it.
- **A supertask inside a supertask** (`supertask::stage`): the statements of a pass that touch nothing the passes carry, write only locals of the pass, and only compute run once ahead of the outer end state, so the outer strategies resolve what is left.
- **An int8 model's integer steps** (`tensor_recognize::qwen::{IntRequantSpec, IntClampSpec, ReluSpec,
  LutAttentionSpec}`, recognized by `recognize_int_requant`, `recognize_int_clamp`, `recognize_relu` and
  `recognize_lut_attention`): `spatial::ir::TensorBody::{Shr, Min, Max, FloorDiv}`, and the evaluator computes an
  all-integer body in checked `i64`, refusing an overflow by name. `tensor_graph::vjp` refuses each as a step function.
- **`spatial::int8_model`**: the tiny transformer's decode step as a LOGOS program (`Int8Model::decode_step_lg`, its
  tensor-parallel form `decode_step_tp_lg`), and `Int8Model::step_reads`, what its host reads.
- **`spatial::value_range`**: the interval every integer tensor of a program holds, over the whole choreography; the
  fabric sizes its words and datapaths from it.
- **The XC7 fabric targets** (`SpatialTarget::{xc7a100t_fabric, xc7a200t_fabric}`, `Roofs::xc7_fabric`) and the facts a
  fabric is printed from: `SiteSpec::{local_mem_read_latency, mem_banks, vector_lanes, int_compute, multiplier}`.
- **The fabric engine** (`spatial::lower::fabric::engine`): one engine a class of sites, its op table registered on the
  next state, banked memories, a vector unit beside the matrix array whose gathers read through a port a lane (a page
  read through several ports in a pool of its own, so a port copies only it), rows that are not whole words kept at a
  pitch of whole words (in tensors and in a matmul's B pages; `host_word` and the hex images follow the pitch),
  pipelined Generic bodies issued on a modulo schedule, a sequential floor divider; `fabric_cycle_model` prices the
  same micro-ops.
- **`spatial::lower::fabric_board`**: a fabric program as a Nexys board image (`board_image`, `decode_board_verilog`,
  `chip_board_verilog`), its host protocol, the on-chip decode loop (`generate_cycles`) and the PLL clock
  (`CoreClock::fastest_within`).
- **`spatial::lower::cuda::general`**: a spatial program the persistent kernel cannot hold, printed as a general
  program, and one that fits one block flattened from its choreography (`general::flat`).
- **`spatial::passes::forward`** and `pipeline::Results`: a result only the next statement reads is forwarded where the
  caller declares the program's results (`compile_program_to_spatial_returning`, `compile_program_to_chips_returning`).
- `compile_program_to_chips`: a tensor-parallel program as one program a chip, with the tensors that cross.
- `codegen_sva::systolic::{WeightStart, emit_systolic_pipelined_loadable_verilog_starting}`: a loaded PE array's
  weights start at what they power up with or at zero.
- `spatial::lower::fabric_board::{program_board_image, ImageRole}`: a fabric program's board image, every file
  synthesis reads, its memories holding its fills and a tensor supplied at run time starting at zero; the Nexys
  Video's FT245 bridge is embedded (`FT245_UART_BRIDGE_V`). `CoreClock::{UNCERTAINTY_NS, for_route}`: the clock
  contract. `spatial::eval::Inputs::from_fills`; `Int8Model::{prompt_next_tokens, reference_cuda}`.
- `codegen_sva::flow_config::ClosureSettings::{grt_overflow_iters, grt_adjustment}`: global routing's overflow
  iterations and the share of each gcell's capacity it holds back, emitted only when chosen;
  `CaravelProject::{macro_grt_overflow_iters, macro_grt_adjustment_permille}` and their `CP_GRT_*` shell variables;
  `emit_flow_config --grt-overflow-iters N --grt-adjustment A`, each refusing what is not one.
- `codegen_sva::psram`: `psram_cache` takes its read and write hits as registered inputs (`rhit`, `whit`) and exports
  the state they are looked up in (`tags`, `vals`, `filled`, `fidx`, `ftag`); `emit_psram_cache_hit_function`,
  `emit_psram_cache_hit_wires` and `psram_cache_hit_ports` give an engine or a bench its half. The off-chip engine
  registers each hit beside the address it sets (`wpe_offchip`, the template's tasks and `ci_set`).
- The supertask engine's cycle search gives up on a state past 2^16 bits (from 2^20). A step's work grows with the square of the state it multiplies, so the bound keeps the last step cheap as well as the states the search keeps: a state that grows quadratically (`x ← x + k·x²` over the hyperreals) is refused in about a second on every tier, where it took minutes.
- `resolve_division`: the program's infinitesimal and a standard part make an exact context bottom-up, and an exact operand inside the exponent of `**` makes its quotients exact. `RuntimeValue` implements `TowerValue::exact_rational` and `ValueAlgebra::power`.
- **The whole-program engine is staged for a short clock.** Its float conversions (`fp_conv`, integer to float and floor, five stages), its compare and popcount lane and its integer multiplier (`MUL_CYCLES` = 4; a compressor tree on the Caravel build, which elaborates no variable multiply) are cut so no stage holds more than one carry chain, leading-one search, barrel shift or compressor tree, and the cycle model charges the multiply's stages.
- **`wpe_caravel::WPE_CARAVEL` runs at 21 ns (47.62 MHz), from 48 ns**, the honest period (`signoff_blocks::honest_period_ns`) of the engine's slow corner measured on its routed 18.5 ns harden. The PSRAM edge takes two engine cycles per SCLK half, so the 42 ns half period still covers a read's 21.17 ns round trip through the harness and the board; its bursts are cut for 40 ns and the UART divisor is 413 at 115200 baud. The macro is built at the chip clock until its harden records a period.
- **The engine runs on `user_clock2` (`wpe_caravel::WPE_CLOCK`).** `pdk_target::HarnessClock` names the two clocks the harness gives a user macro, and `HarnessTiming` carries the core clock's signoff period (25 ns, from Caravel's `caravel.sdc`) and the ports the harness times against it. `CaravelProject::clock` is the clock a project's logic runs on: `check` refuses one that drives the core clock past its signoff period (`CaravelProjectUnplaceable::CoreClockPastSignoff`), `wrapper_clocks_ns` gives the clocks its wrapper is timed at, and the scripts' view carries `CP_CLOCK_PORT`, `CV_CORE_CLOCK_PERIOD_NS` and `CW_*`. `flow_config::vehicle_macro_sdc(pdk, clock)` times a macro on `user_clock2` with `wb_clk_i` a clock of its own at the core period, asynchronous to the macro's, and the bus's delays moved onto it; `emit_flow_config --vehicle-macro-sdc <clock port>`. The macro's engine takes `wb_rst_i`, or logic-analyzer probe 0 while the management SoC drives it, through a two-flop synchronizer; its bring-up bench drives both clocks, and its read-window STA times the engine's clock.
- **The SECDED codec is shallow and the memory subsystem registers it.** `scratchpad_rtl::emit_secded_verilog` writes each check bit and the overall parity as one reduction over its data bits, and the decoder's uncorrectable verdict (an even-parity error, or an odd one whose syndrome names no codeword position: `Decode::DoubleError`) in parallel with the correction. `infinichip_rtl` registers the encoder's data word and the decoder's verdict, one edge each way. Re-signed at what every corner meets under delay-oriented synthesis: `infinichip` 8.1 ns, `gemm_mac_realbist` 9.4 ns.
- **A registered block is synthesised under the strategy its period was measured with.** `signoff_blocks::SignoffBlock::synth_strategy` holds it beside the period (`registered_synth_strategy`, `resolve_synth_strategy`), and `emit_flow_config` refuses another.
- **Re-signed at what every corner meets under delay-oriented synthesis:** `dram_executor` 5.0 ns, `dram_executor_wb` 4.8 ns, `decision_hft_bist` 4.4 ns, `cgra_sumto` 6.4 ns.
- `passes::fuse_generic` folds a map into the map it follows when their accesses agree on the iteration space.
- `NexysBoard::extra_sources` gives each file's text, and `board_image` includes them.
- `Op::MagicDivU` carries `wrap`: the oracle proved its dividend below twice its divisor (`Opt::ModWrap`), so its remainder is the dividend less the divisor at most once. The VM compiler sets it from `OracleFacts::expr_int_range`, and compiles a remainder of a value below its divisor as the value; the VM and the C walker compute a `wrap` remainder as a compare and a subtract, and the JIT, RISC-V, WebAssembly and the fabric keep the magic sequence. The rule moved to `optimize::modwrap`, where the VM compiler and both AST emitters read it.
- `postgres.lg`'s `pgCancelInFlight` waits for the postmaster to close the cancel connection before closing its own end, as libpq's `PQcancel` does, so a statement sent after a cancel returns is never the one it cancels.
- The lane's staged per-head matmul takes a tile of rows that fits a block's default 48 KB of shared memory beside the kernel's own sums (`kernels::AXPY_SUMS`): a head of more rows than (48 KB less those sums) holds took a kilobyte past it in Q4_K and IQ4_XS, and its launch failed (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **The decode pricer charged a tied head and a rotation table to the wrong place.** A checkpoint with no `output.weight` ties its head to the embedding table; `price` billed that read at the weights' memory tier rather than the table's, and a llama-family `rope_freqs` table was read in the embedding stage but held under no residency class. A stage now carries its `Stage::weight_class`, so a tied head reads the table from whichever tier holds it; the rotation table is held with the weights and read once, by the first attention block (a hybrid stack's is not its first block), and a rotation table no attention block rotates is refused by name. The only committed figure that moves is `benchmarks/silicon/decode/decode_chip.json`'s two Llama-3.1 rows, by ≤4.4e-6 relative now that the table is held.
- A device tier's check for the exact extension (`compile::exact_refusal`: the Silicon ISA, Tenstorrent, analog, Plasticine, RDU, CUDA, the soft core and the fabric) compiled the program through the `## Require` gate, so `## Require fastdiv` failed `largo emit isa` on a program fastdiv serves: the check's own compile has no range oracle and fastdiv cannot fire in it. The check now only asks whether the extension reaches the program; the tier's compile reports a program that does not compile, located, and checks its `## Require`.
- `supertask::scope`: before a strategy reads a clock loop's pass, a `Let` of a name the guard or an earlier statement used is renamed apart with every later mention, and the renaming is checked against `optimize::mentions`. A pass the renaming cannot keep apart, or one that binds its clock again, resolves to a failure with the reason.
- `concurrency::marshal` refuses a provisional answer encoded inside another.
- The fabric printer adds a tile address's runtime index: a gather read the table's first row whatever its index.
- `codegen_sva::fpu.v`'s incrementers and `wpe_soc_template.v`'s overflow test have no wide single-bit select:
  `cin` enters the prefix scan as its bit 0, and the overflow reads `{hi, sign}` with `&` and `|` reductions.
- `tensor_graph::vjp::backward(forward, output, cotangent, wrt)` differentiates a tensor program in reverse mode and writes the backward pass as ordinary LOGOS: tensor sugar where the language has the form (`matmul`, `transpose`, `plus`, `hadamard`, `scaled by`), row-major loops over declared tensors where it does not. It has rules for matmul, elementwise add and multiply, scaling by a number, transpose, head slices (the first or last columns of every group), joins (whole rows or across heads), per-head matmul, softmax, RMSNorm (weighted, weightless, over a fused residual, per head), LayerNorm, L2 norm (per row or per head) — each norm at the epsilon its program gives — sigmoid, SiLU, GELU, SwiGLU, row and rows gathers, rope, attention (full, over a cache up to a position, grouped-query, a causal block of queries; the softmax recomputed less each row's largest score, as the forward computes it) and row writes. `argmax` is refused as piecewise constant; causal convolution, decay, gated delta and mixture-of-experts are refused by name until their rules are written; rope tables and integer indices are never differentiated, and asking for them is refused.
- The C backend carries an exact integer through a task's argument and through a waiting call's argument and result. `lint_to_cell`/`lint_of_cell` hold a value within 62 bits as 2v+1 and any other as its collector-held big integer; launched and stepped functions type their parameters from the exact-slot analysis, a launch's arguments are stores into its task's parameters, and a stepped `Return` is coerced to the function's result type.
- **A function bytecode C runs on machine words has a word entry.** `codegen_c::from_program` makes the body of every function whose integers it runs on machine words until one outgrows a word its word entry, `fn_N_w`, and moves the part that runs held exact into a function of its own, `fn_N_x`. A caller on machine words hands the word entry its integers untagged, and it answers in two registers, a machine word or the exact value it escaped to, marked; when a value outgrows a word it hands `fn_N_x` only the registers live there, so its own frame keeps nothing for the exact half. `fn_N`, the entry for tagged words, enters one or the other. Takeuchi's function runs in 1.07× the AST C emitter's time, where it took 1.68× (`c_bytecode_perf_lock` holds it under 1.30×), and every way a value crosses the entries runs as on the VM (`c_bytecode_word_entry_lock`).
- Mutable heap parameters remain rooted until their implicit return, including across allocating loops. The exact power-of-two modulo fallback releases its owned remainder after conversion.
- Integer-valued C bytecode maps use one exact representation across native and overflow paths, preserving wide values through stores, reads and memoized recursion. Count-accumulator proofs include call purity, loop nesting, positive inclusive starts and exact-bound exclusions. WASM comparisons with Nothing bypass integer narrowing. Optimizer fact consumers and exact-integer host interfaces are integrated with their providers.
- `ClosureSettings::pdn_vpitch_um` sets the vertical power-strap pitch, emitted as `FP_PDN_VPITCH` only when it is chosen. A cell midway between two straps loses supply along its met1 rail, and only the pitch shortens that rail. `emit_flow_config` takes it as `--pdn-vpitch`, adds `--grt-antenna-iters` and `--grt-antenna-margin`, and refuses a malformed `--eco-iters` instead of keeping the default.
- `ClosureSettings::hold_slack_margin_ns` sets the placement and global-route hold-repair margin (`PL_`/`GLB_RESIZER_HOLD_SLACK_MARGIN`), emitted only when it is chosen. The repair judges hold on estimated parasitics and signoff on extracted ones; on `systolic_pipe16_realbist` routing doubled the clock skew and signoff read −0.04 ns past OpenLane's 0.05 ns margin. `emit_flow_config` takes it as `--hold-slack-margin` and refuses it beside `--vehicle-macro`.
- `full_asm` lowers `text_bytes` to chip opcode 237, gated on a Text operand. The soft-core model builds the UTF-8 `Seq of Int` with the same range encoder as `soft_core.v`'s `TextBytes` FSM, and on every Unicode scalar value that encoder equals `char::encode_utf8`.
- C text-byte views belong to the declaration and lexical scope that emitted them. Repeated names across functions and shadowing owned sequences retain their own storage representation while proven local views remain borrowed.
- Standalone WebAssembly reclaims temporary collection, record, closure and word-list storage at safepoints. Batch functions root live handles across calls and loop backedges, preserving caller values, iterator snapshots and returned children; released host callbacks are rejected.
- Fabric lowering reserves every fixed list’s proven capacity plus its header in straight-line and CFG paths. Function opcode inspection and CFG compilation retain the type registry that supplies the shared capacity proof.
- `spatial::work::work_of_plan`, the closed form the plan search ranks its candidates by, counts what the compiler emits on every target. It priced every tile at the operand's width, so a result written wider than its operands (fp32 under bf16 in exact numerics, int32 or int64 under int8) was counted short: the fabric's bytes at 22–30% of what its choreography moves, the RTX 3060's and the RDUs' at 50–86%, every exact-numerics Tensix plan's at 75%. It split a site's streams across two movement engines on targets that have one, charged each multicast burst as a four-byte control word, and counted a multicast at the busiest site as one transaction per tile. It now reads the tile extents, the widths (`work::GemmWidths`: each operand at its tensor's width, the result at the output's, a multicast at its buffer's), the fidelity, the sites and the engines per site off the template and its target, `work_of_plan(template, plan, fused, target)`, so no caller hands it a width or an engine count the emitter does not use; `pipeline::build_candidate` is the one definition of the program a candidate builds. The plans chosen at every preset and shape the suites plan are unchanged.
- Each matrix engine states its sub-block API (`target::BlockApi`, the `block_api` of `ComputeCap::MatmulTile`): Tensix's `matmul_block` makes a call per inner step and its unpacker holds one operand along the sub-block's shorter side, re-loading the other for each held tile (`llk_unpack_AB_matmul`); the sky130 fabric's systolic array makes one call over the whole inner block, paying its fill once, and is fed each operand panel once; the GPU's `wmma` loop takes a step per inner step, each warp loading its own fragments; an engine with none runs a tile pair per call. `SpatialTarget::{matmul_calls, operand_loads}` are the one definition the choreography walk and the closed form read, `work::Work::engine_operand_tiles` counts the loads, and `Objective::Balanced` breaks a tie on calls by them. The fabric's descriptor said a tile per call, and every sub-block API was then counted as Tensix's with no loads at all, so its planner kept 1 × 1, then long, then shallow sub-blocks; its GEMM plans now take the fewest cycles of any candidate its cycle twin times (32 × 32 × 16: 74,830 cycles before the descriptor named its API, 14,125 now; 64 × 64 × 32 under rounding numerics 586,583 → 97,341). On Tensix four plans under rounding numerics take a full-height sub-block column (Wormhole 2048 × 512 × 1024, 1536 × 768 × 768 and 2048 × 1024 × 512, Blackhole 2048 × 1024 × 512: 4 × 2 → 8 × 1, 2 × 3 → 6 × 1, 2 × 4 → 8 × 1), and ttsim measures each at the same MVMULs and NOC traffic in 14–32% fewer Tensix instructions and fewer device cycles; `benchmarks/silicon/tenstorrent_gemm.json` records the four as the compiler's choice. `Objective::key` is a `work::RankKey`, seven exact counts compared in order with none packed into a shared place.
- **WebAssembly lowers `NewEmptyListFixed`** as the empty list it is observably.
- **AST C holds exact every integer the other tiers do.** Which of a body's variables and results are exact is the shared decision (`analysis::exactness::body_exactness`, the rules bytecode C and WebAssembly ask of the bytecode): a sum fed twice by itself (`x + x`), a sum cycle through several variables (`t = a + b; a = b; b = t`), a product through the function's own result (`n * fact(n - 1)`) and a program binding a function multiplies are exact where AST C printed a wrapped word; a bitwise combination carries its operands' exactness and a mask or remainder by a constant carries none. Floor division of an exact integer is exact (`logos_lint_floordiv`); an exact integer handed to a Real parameter widens to the nearest double on both C tiers; an exact index past a word fails with the reference tier's sentence (`lint_index_i64`).
- **The CGRA extractors read a counted loop.** `Repeat for v from a to b` compiles to a counted loop (a header comparing the counter with its bound, the counter's unit step ending the body) rather than a range iterator; `codegen_sva::fabric_rtl`'s single and nested loop extractors recognise that header and step, so every counted-loop fabric is the circuit it was.
- **Bytecode C settles global kinds around its signatures**, and a declared `Int` global an exact integer is stored into is exact, as a parameter is: a product kept in a program binding a function reads printed a wrapped word.
- **Bytecode C swaps an exact integer as itself and refuses a wide index in the VM's words.** `Swap a and b` exchanges the tagged words and the walker's kinds where it narrowed the exact side first and gave the narrowed kind back to the register now holding the other side's word (the run stopped with "integer value out of range"); a side the shared decision holds exact (`vm::exactness::Exactness::outs`) takes a machine word it receives tagged. An exact index past a word fails with the sentence the VM fails a list read, a Text read or a list store with (`lc_xindex`).
- **Bytecode C empties a list's buffer in place only for a list of the same kind.** A new list reused the buffer its register last held alone, whatever that held: a `Seq of Bool` followed in one register by a `Seq of Int` wrote eight bytes per element into a buffer of one, corrupting the heap. A register's buffer is reused only where every handle the region writes to it, on machine words and held exact, is that one kind of sequence; and the owned flags of a swap and of a fixed-capacity list are written only in a region that tracks them, where a region without one did not compile (`c_bytecode_lock::a_new_sequence_never_takes_the_buffer_of_another_kind`).
- **A function that only inspects a recursive value borrows it** (`codegen::lent`: `fn check(tree: &Tree)`, arm bindings borrowed on the same terms, a greatest fixpoint over the call graph); walking a tree copies nothing on the Rust tier (`enum_param_borrow_lock`). On the VM an `Inspect` arm binding shares the enum value instead of copying the subtree, so a walk allocates the same per node at any depth (`vm_enum_walk_lock`).
- An exact GEMM on Tensix splits its reduction across K blocks and finishes it in the output's own page. Where every partial sum is provably an integer fp32 holds exactly (`NumericsClaim::Exact`), adding a block's sum is the straight K-ascending accumulation bit for bit, so the packer adds each block into local memory in place, the intermediate shares the output's memory (`Buffer::alias_of`; `target::PackerAccumulate::IntoTheOutput`, two indices of one tt-metal `CircularBufferConfig`), and the last block accumulates into the page the writer stores: no reload of the fp32 partial Wormhole and Blackhole cannot unpack. An item reserves its output pages before any partial lands in their memory. Exact plans used to hold K in one block and outgrow the mesh; every exact GEMM from 1536³ to 2048³ now reads each operand once, and under ttsim a 2048 × 2048 × 1024 GEMM moves 33.6 MB in 5,966 device cycles where it moved 151 MB in 22,895, PCC 1.0. Data of unknown range keeps its order. `tile::finishes_in_place`; `tile::candidates_where`, so a calibration run that pins an arrangement gets that arrangement's best plan, wrapped where its blocked plans cannot fit; `size_buffers::footprint_bytes_for_plan(program, plan, target)`. `place` sizes a spill buffer, and an output it shares, to the sub-blocks each boundary site of a ragged grid walks, so the packer's page comes back to the first every block. The IR evaluator gives aliased buffers one memory and faults a write into a page an alias still holds (`eval::ChoreoFault::AliasClobber`).
- `spatial::roofs::Roofs`: every `SpatialTarget` preset carries the rates its chip sustains — off-chip bandwidth, network-link width, matrix-engine operations per cycle — each a `Fact` in the unit its source states it in (Tenstorrent's card pages and ISA documentation, the tt-metal GEMM report, NVIDIA's RTX 3060 table and GA102 whitepaper, the RDU facts, the fabric's cycle twin and Sky130 signoff). A `MemRoof` counts its rate in bytes for a bus and in elements for an element-serial lane (the fabric's address lane carries one element per cycle whatever its width), and `work::Work::dram_tiles` counts the tiles behind the DRAM bytes, so a lane is paced by the elements it carries. `Roofs::per_cycle` / `per_second` convert through the clock and keep the weaker provenance. `PlasticineTarget::roofs` gives each Plasticine mesh its DRAM bandwidth, clock and fp32 peak under the provenance its record supports (Tungsten's bandwidth measured, on a read stream). `rdu_chip::Provenance` gains `Derived` and `Measured`, in trust order, and `RduChipFacts::{hbm_gbps, clock_ghz, roofs}` hold the RDU's modeled HBM rate and clock, which `PlasticineTarget::rdu` reads. `roofs::roofline` divides a program's work by its target's roofs — DRAM bytes by the shared memory, the busiest movement engine's bytes by one network link, the arithmetic by the matrix engine — and names the bound that binds, with the program's arithmetic intensity. `work::Objective::Roofline` ranks plans by that bound, the slowest resource first, with `Balanced`'s key as the exact tiebreak; it admits the ragged grids `Balanced` refuses for want of a clock, since it prices their spread. It is a named objective beside `Traffic`, `Transactions` and `Issue`: on Wormhole at 640³ and on Blackhole at 1024³ and 2048 × 1024 × 512 it chooses a ragged grid whose matrix bound is 25% below `Balanced`'s at equal DRAM bytes, which no simulator here can time.
- **The standard library is written in LOGOS: 35 new modules, 1,149 functions, auto-imported on first use.** Each module is ordinary LOGOS under `crates/logicaffeine_compile/assets/std/`, spliced into the program that calls it and compiled with it.
  - Text and data: `strings` (95 functions over the UTF-8 byte view: split/join/trim/replace, Two-Way `indexOf`, `wrapText`/`fillText`/`dedentText`, the rune codec `validUtf8`/`decodeRune`/`encodeRune`); `chars` (ASCII classification and case); `bytes` (44 byte-sequence operations); `bits` (`popcount`, `bitLength`, width-parametric rotates, reversals and zero counts, `packUintBE`/`packUintLE`); `regex` (literals, `.`, `*` and flat groups over UTF-8 bytes, 1-based, with Go-style submatch indices); `encoding` (hex, base64 std/URL/raw, base32/base32hex, base85/ascii85, uuencode, quoted-printable, `hexDump`); `convert` (`toBase`, `parseBool`, `formatUint`, the `append*` family); `json` (RFC 8259 `Json` with exact scaled decimals, lenient `jsonTryParse`, duplicate-refusing `jsonParseStrict`, canonical `jsonSerialize`, typed readers `jsonField`/`jsonString`/`jsonInt` answering `Result`); `unicode` (UCD general category, mirrored, combining, digit and east-asian-width tables).
  - Numbers: `math` (157 functions: trigonometric, hyperbolic, exp and log families, `erf`/`erfinv`, `gamma`/`lgamma`, IEEE `ulp`/`nextafter`/`frexp`, `powMod`/`modInverse`/`modSqrt`, Miller–Rabin `isProbablePrime`, arbitrary-precision `pi(digits)`/`eDigits`/`sqrtDigits`, complex `cExp` … `cAtanh` over `ComplexF`); `statistics`; `rng` (a seeded Lehmer generator, bit-identical on every tier, with ten distributions); `operator` (23 operator functions).
  - Collections: `iter` (58 sequence operations, including `mapSeq`/`filterSeq`/`reduceSeq`, bisect, set algebra and `scanSeq`); `collections` (Counter, `Deque`); `heap` (a binary min-heap, `heapSortSeq`, `nSmallest`); `sort`; `builder` (`StringBuilder`, `ByteReader`).
  - Linear algebra: `blas` (Levels 1–3 over row-major `Seq of Float`); `lapack` (53 routines: Cholesky, LU, QR, SVD, symmetric and general eigenproblems, Schur, `expm`/`logm`/`sqrtm`, least squares, pseudo-inverse; `solveLinear`/`minimizeQuadratic` back the `Solve … for x.` and `Minimize … over x.` sentences); `clapack` (complex BLAS/LAPACK over `ComplexF`).
  - System and media: `calendar` (ISO weekday names, `daysInMonth`, `timegm`); `compress` (CRC-32, Adler-32, zlib inflate of stored, fixed- and dynamic-Huffman blocks, a stored-block `zlibCompress`); `error` (`unwrapOr`); `media` (AIMD `adaptRate`, `elasticBuffer`, `layersFor`); `vision` (fixed-point `conv3x3`).
  - Server: `http.lg` (`Request`/`Response`/`HttpError`; replies `plain`, `html`, `json`, `notFound`, `unauthorized`, `forbidden`, `redirectTo`, `permanentRedirectTo`, `bytesResponse`; case-insensitive `headerOf` and `queryOf`; `withHeader` answers a `Result` and refuses an invalid name, a control byte, a hop-by-hop header and `content-type`; cookies are written `HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Secure` by default, and `isCookieSafe` refuses a name or value that would split the header); `static.lg` (`contentTypeFor`, the one MIME table `largo serve` and `largo build` read through `loader::content_type_for`; `fileResponse`; `staticUnder`, which refuses any `..` segment before touching disk); `tcp.lg` (twelve socket natives answering `Result`, with certificate-verifying `tcpConnectTls`/`tcpUpgradeTls`); `postgres.lg` (the PostgreSQL v3 protocol with cleartext, MD5 and SCRAM-SHA-256 authentication, `pgConnectSecure` (TLS or refusal), prepared and parameterized statements, NULL-honest `*AtOpt` getters, transactions, statement timeouts and `pgCancelRequest`, errors carrying their SQLSTATE); `session.lg` (HMAC-SHA-256 signed cookies through `signSession`/`verifySession`, CSRF tokens through `csrfToken`/`csrfValid`). `http_headers_lock`, `http_reply_and_cookies_lock`, `cookie_secure_lock`, `http_static_lock`, `mime_single_source_lock`, `tcp_tls_lock`, `postgres_aot_lock`, `session_lock`; `postgres_wire` and `postgres_protocol` pin every frame byte for byte.
  - Page: `web.lg` (fetch wrappers keyed by request id: `webGet`, `postJson`, `putJson`, `deleteAt`, `postJsonIdempotent`; the router helpers the compiler writes calls to: `matchRoute`, `routeParam`, `queryParam`, `bodyParam`; `urlEncode`/`urlDecode`, `htmlEscape`, `safeUrl` and multipart framing); `forms.lg` (validation complaints collected as a `Seq of FieldError`, `errorFor` answering `""` on a clean field, `checkRequired`/`checkMinLength`/`checkEmail` and their siblings); `ui.lg` (1-based pagination, `truncate`, `plural`, `initials`); `vdom.lg` (the retained-tree reconciler every page draws through, below). `forms_lock`, `ui_lock`, `lgx_stdlib_guide_lock`, `parity_web`.
  - `crypto.lg` adds SHA-2 (`sha256`, `hmacSha256`, `pbkdf2Sha256`, pinned to the FIPS 180-4, RFC 4231 and RFC 7677 vectors on the tree-walker, the VM and C by `crypto_sha2_lock`), ChaCha20 `encrypt`/`decrypt` (RFC 8439), 4-way Keccak (`keccakF4Logos`/`keccakSponge4` over `Lanes4Word64`) that expands the ML-DSA matrix once per keygen, sign or verify (`mldsa_aot_lock`), Ding RLWE key exchange and Rainbow signatures. `crypto_accel::{sha256, hmac_sha256, pbkdf2_sha256}` run `pbkdf2Sha256` natively on the tree-walker and the VM, byte-identical to the LOGOS body the compiled tiers run (`crypto_accel_lock`).
  - Contracts: every position a module takes or returns is 1-based (`stdlib_position_base_lock`); every `## To` name is unique across modules (`stdlib_no_name_collisions`); each compute module builds on AOT-Rust (`parity_stdlib_aot_rust`).
  - Parity: `benchmarks/parity/catalog/superset.json` holds the library to a deduplicated superset of the C, Python, Rust, Go and Java standard libraries; `benchmarks/parity/PARITY_LEDGER.md` reports 548 of 548 in-scope capabilities correct on every claimed tier and 38 of 520 performance-gated capabilities benchmarked faster (`parity_catalog_lock`, `parity_ledger_lock`).
- **Prelude splicing, pruning and provenance APIs.**
  - `loader::{apply_prelude_mapped, PreludeSegment}` splice the selected modules after the program's leading prose and record each segment, so a parse-time refusal inside spliced text maps to the author's line exactly once; `compile::{prelude_user_offset, prelude_user_span}` invert the splice (`checked_seam_span_lock`).
  - `loader::{prune_unreachable_prelude, prune_unreachable_prelude_from, prune_unreachable_prelude_mapped, prune_unreachable_prelude_mapped_from, prune_unreachable_prelude_mapped_with, keep_reachable_functions}` drop the library functions a program never reaches, and never a user function; the compiled tiers and deep check prune as `largo build` does (`rustc_check_prune_lock`).
  - `loader::{library_owned_functions, LibraryProvenance, is_library_function}` license a lowering keyed on a library name (memchr `indexOf`/`countSub`, AVX2 `trunc`, native `pbkdf2Sha256`) only when the callee is the library's own body (`user_name_shadows_stdlib_lock`).
- **Multi-file projects assemble into one program every tier runs.**
  - `analysis::assemble::{assemble_project, assemble_project_with_deps, check_scopes, AssembledProgram, Provenance, DefSite, DefKind, SourceMap, Segment, Located, ScopeInfo}` splice every reachable module into one source, post-order and safe on cycles and diamonds, keeping only the entry's `## Main`, with per-name provenance, a byte-range-to-file map and per-file scope. Imported functions reach the parser, the call graph and every backend; v0.10.1's `compile_project` carried only imported types.
  - Resolution (`loader::DepRoot`, `Loader::with_dep_roots`): a bare `Import Name.` tries `name.lg`/`name.md` beside the importer, then a directory module (`name/name.lg`, then `name/mod.lg`), then `src/` and the `Largo.toml` root, then declared dependency roots; a miss gets a did-you-mean. `Import G from "path".`, `Import G as Geo.`, `Import A and B.` and the selective `Import double [as dbl] from Util.` resolve.
  - A bare name two modules define differently is refused (identical bodies merge); `## To private f` and `A private T` stay inside their module; `Util::double(…)` and `Geometry::Point` resolve; an imported type is emitted once as `Alias__Type` with a `type Bare = …` alias; a parse error in an imported module is reported "in `<file>`: …".
  - `compile::{compile_project_with_deps, enforce_project_scope, enforce_project_scope_with_deps, ProjectScopeError}` build and scope-check on it.
  - `analysis::import_hygiene::{lint_imports, lint_imports_with_deps, ImportLint, ImportLintKind, LintSeverity}` report unused, duplicate and self imports as warnings.
  - `diagnostic::{RemappedError, remap_rustc_diagnostics}` and `compile::remap_project_errors` report a rustc failure on a project's generated crate at the LOGOS `file:line:col`, with the source line and a caret (`sourcemap_remap`).
  - Tests: `multimodule_functions`, `multimodule_encapsulation`, `multimodule_selective`, `multimodule_directory`, `project_module_lock`, `local_deps_lock`, `import_scan_lock`, `check_deep_multifile_lock`, `import_hygiene`.
- **Checker APIs.**
  - `analysis::known_calls::{check_unknown_calls, check_unknown_calls_with_exports, proven_module_exports}` and `TypeError::UnknownFunction`: every post-prelude compile seam refuses a call to a name defined nowhere at check time, in the runtime's words, `Unknown function: <name>` (`unknown_call_lock`).
  - `analysis::{check_program_located, LocatedTypeError}`: a type error names the function or construct the author wrote, not the lowered name (`type_error_location_lock`). `analysis::{infer_instantiations, Instantiations, CallInstance, Site, root_binding_types}` infer generic records and enums per instantiation.
  - `analysis::check::first_undeclared_type` finds the first type a program names that nothing supplies; every strict compile entry refuses it at the statement that names it (`undeclared_type_refusal_lock`).
  - `analysis::unify::{TypeScope, InferType::from_type_expr_in, InferType::from_field_type_in, InferType::describe, TypeTable::instantiate_with}`; `TypeScope::canonical` makes a bare type name and its namespaced twin one type.
  - `LogosType` and `InferType` gain the nominal DOM handle types `Element`, `Event` and `View`: each unifies only with itself, and the VM boundary, the AOT type maps, the optimizer's parameter tags and the interpreter's field defaults represent all three as the `i64` handle (`nominal_dom_types_lock`).
- **Monomorphization.** `monomorphize::{monomorphize, Options, Monomorphized, TypeInstance}` builds one instance per generic function and type-argument list and one registered type per applied generic; `Options::lower_option` lowers `some`/`none` to core's `Option`, and polymorphic recursion past 4,096 instances is refused. C (`codegen_c::codegen_monomorphic_program_c`) and WebAssembly (`ui_bridge::with_monomorphic_program`) compile generic programs through it; AOT-Rust keeps Rust generics, with a turbofish for phantom type parameters and the `where` bounds a body needs. `generics_all_tiers`, `c_enum_option_lock`, `aot_specialized_recursion_lock`.
- **`## Require <opt>` turns an optimization that did not fire into a compile error.** `optimize::install_decorators` merges a file's `## No` flags into the active configuration and opens a firing trace for each `## Require`; `optimize::DecoratorGate` fails the compile, naming the keyword, when a demanded optimization did not fire. AOT-Rust, `compile_project`, C, the ISA listing, WebAssembly and the verified path all compile through this one seam; `ui_bridge::interpret_unsatisfiable_require` refuses `## Require` on the interpreter path up front. `e2e_require_optimization`, `decorator_through_largo`.
- **New optimizer passes, each a registry row switched off with `## No <keyword>` or `LOGOS_OPT_OFF=<keyword>` and pinned with `## Tier`.**
  - On both pipelines: `sccp` (sparse conditional constant propagation over a ⊤/Const/⊥ lattice; proves a mutable's constancy at a point and prunes the branches `propagate` and `dce` miss; loop-carried values widen to ⊥), `intstrength` (integer induction strength reduction), `regpromote` (a loop's read-modify-write of `item IDX of C` at an invariant index stays in a scalar behind a zero-trip guard, refused under aliasing, `Break`/`Return` or a side-effecting condition) and `interchange` (`ijk` → `ikj` for an integer reduction nest, only where strictly more accesses become unit-stride).
  - The run path gains `memo`: a pure function of one hashable parameter that calls only itself, at least twice, is memoized through a module-level `Map`, so `fib(40)` completes on the optimizing VM.
  - On the AOT pipeline, where the proven-raw marks they leave lower to bare `i64`: `accumbound` (bounded-accumulator versioning, proved by an i128 interval inductive-invariant check), `specializeloop` (pure integer helpers inlined under the counter's proven range, dead sign branches folded) and `divmodfuse` (`q = a / b` bound once, `a % b` → `a - q*b`).
  - Under `comptime`, a pure function called with statically bounded arguments (at most 4,096 points) is replaced by a table computed at compile time; a point that would leave `i64` is never tabled.
  - Closed forms extend to `Σ i²`, `Σ i³`, affine `Σ (a·i + b)` (also through a one-parameter affine helper) and `Σ trunc(toReal(i))` (only for the canonical `toReal`/`trunc` bodies); geometric sums are refused.
  - `popcount` also rewrites the shift-and-mask popcount, bit-length and trailing-zeros loops to guarded `count_ones`/`leading_zeros`/`trailing_zeros`, exact for every input: `POPCNT` on AOT-Rust, `i64.popcnt` on WebAssembly, `__builtin_popcountll` on C.
  - The supercompiler tracks static `Seq` values in a program confined to literal-built sequences and constant-trip loops, folding `item i of A`, the loops and `**`.
  - Tests: `sccp_lock`, `int_strength_lock`, `reg_promote_lock`, `loop_interchange_lock`, `memo_run_lock`, `perf_pass_guard_elision_lock`, `perf_pass_linear_accum_lock`, `perf_pass_chain_accum_lock`, `comptime_tableize_lock`, `closed_form_poly_lock`, `closed_form_affine_lock`, `closed_form_trunc_toreal_lock`, `popcount_intrinsic_lock`, `tensor_supercompile_lock`.
- **Sixteen AOT-Rust code-generation optimizations, each a registry row with the same switches.**
  - Big integers: `bignumbyref` borrows a reused `LogosInt` operand into `LogosInt::{add, sub, mul}`; `bignuminplace` turns `Set acc to acc * <i64>` into `mul_assign_i64`.
  - Text: `textbytesview` (a read-only `text_bytes(s)` is `s.as_bytes()`), `textfrombytesbuilder` (a push-only byte `Seq` is a `Vec<u8>`), `radixdigitbuffer` (the itoa idiom writes one back-to-front `[u8; 64]`), `textslicereturn`/`byteslicereturn` (a trim-family function returns `&'a str`/`&'a [i64]`, admitted only where no caller owns the result), `stringaccumreuse`, `stringconcatreuse` and `stringrepeatloop` (`push_str`, buffer reuse, `chunk.repeat(n)`).
  - Integer loops: `bitintrinsics` (a native-width `rotateLeft`/`reverseBits`/`reverseBytes` is one Rust intrinsic), `asciiclassmask` (a byte-domain `Int -> Bool` predicate is a 256-entry table built at compile time), `strengthreducepow2` (`%` and `/` by `2^k` become `&` and `>>` on a proven non-negative operand), `branchlessaccumulate` (a guarded increment is an if-converted add), `wideaccumulator` (a bounded counting accumulator is an i128 add).
  - `simdreduce`: `Set acc to acc + trunc(<float expression of the counter>)` over a counted loop becomes a four-lane AVX2 `f64` kernel with a scalar tail; liftable helpers (the math.lg `sin`/`cos`/`atan`/`asin`/`acos`) inline, branches become `blendv`, each lane truncates over the full `i64` range, and the output is byte-identical to the scalar loop (`simd_reduce_lock`).
  - A self-recursive in-place function over `&mut [T]` (the quicksort shape) has its non-recursive work region outlined into an `#[inline(never)] fn <f>__part` that repeats the entry precondition; `## No borrow` turns it off.
  - Smaller levers: fixed buffers filled through a cursor (a sha256 schedule is a `[T; N]`), entry-guard headroom for an exclusive counter, raw heap-descent affine indices, loop bounds `narrow` resolves across scopes, and raw division by a nonzero literal; a `Parallel` sum declines a float accumulator (`correctness_seq_fills`, `aot_native_corpus`).
  - Tests: `phase_opt_trace` fires each on a witness; `text_from_bytes_builder_lock`, `radix_digit_buffer_lock`, `text_slice_return_lock`, `byte_slice_return_lock`, `concat_reuse_lock`, `repeat_loop_lock`, `chars_mask_lock`, `strength_reduce_pow2_lock`, `branchless_accumulate_lock`, `wide_accumulator_lock`, `perf_pass_bignum_inplace_lock`.
- **`vm::peephole::peephole(&mut CompiledProgram)`: an IR peephole for the backends that lower bytecode straight to a target.** Block-local copy propagation and dead-move elimination over an iterative-liveness fixpoint, run by `compile_to_riscv`, `compile_to_wasm` (and its observed and linked forms) and `codegen_sva::fabric_rtl::whole_program_fabric_from_source`, which have no downstream optimizing assembler; the `Set a to b. Set b to t.` swap idiom's five moves through temporaries become two. Four exhaustive per-op register classifiers: `reads` over-approximates, `kills` under-approximates, `clobbers = kills ∪ mutates` invalidates copies on both sides for value semantics, and `for_each_rewritable_read` excludes range windows, in-place mutation sites and the `TestArm`/`BindArm` scrutinee. `peephole_soundness_lock` holds it equal to the VM over the corpus and the benchmarks, idempotent, shrinking the swap idiom, under a 400-program fuzzer and aliasing torture; `riscv_aot_lock`, `wasm_aot_lock` and `fabric_coverage_lock` confirm it over the corpus.
- **The C tier is a general backend.** v0.10.1's C was a benchmark subset with no prelude, big integers, exact or value types, collection `Show` or bounds checks.
  - `compile_to_c` splices the auto-imported modules and keeps the functions the program reaches (`loader::keep_reachable_functions`), mangles names that collide with libm, monomorphizes, and resolves Rational `/` by type; a call to `gcd`, `binomial`, `mapSeq` or the `strings.lg` family emits its body (`c_prelude_probe`). It refuses by name a program whose reachable code touches the DOM, a Rust-only native kernel and a CRDT kind C does not lower (`dom_tier_refusal_lock`, `native_kernel_lock`, `crdt_backend_lock`).
  - The exact numeric tower: `LInt` (an `int64_t` fast path promoting to `LBig`) carries provable overflow, growing accumulators and every `**`/`pow`; `LDec` carries `Rational` and decimal, so `7 / 2` stays `7/2`; `LComplex` and `LMod` carry `complex(…)` and `modular(v, n)` (`c_numeric_tower_lock`, `c_bigint_power_lock`).
