A chunk's causal conv runs its rows at once (`lk_conv_rows`, then `lk_conv_carry` advancing the ring), and an `f32` projection spreads its rows over the grid's second dimension (`lk_gemv_f32`, `kernels::GEMV_F32_ROWS` a block), each output as before bit for bit. A 512-token prompt prefills at 10,980 tokens a second on Llama-3.2-1B (7,278 before), 1,858 on Qwen3-8B (1,145) and 6,030 on Qwen3.5-0.8B (2,219), whose gated-delta layers' gate projections had run 16 warps over every row (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **A wide chunk attends on the f16 tensor cores under the one-word numerics, and flash style under the two-word numerics.** Under the one-word numerics — llama.cpp's precision, whose attention rounds its operands to f16 — a wide chunk's attention with heads and values whole multiples of 16 at most 192 wide runs on `kernels::ATTENTION_MMA`'s `lk_attention_mma` (`Launch::attends_on_tensor_cores`): four warps take 64 query vectors, a warp 16 held in registers as `mma` fragments; each 64-key tile is staged as f16, its scores `m16n8k16` products against the keys (`ldmatrix`), each row's running maximum and sum taken across the four lanes of the row, and the weights — the scores' fragments rounded to f16 — weigh the values (`ldmatrix.trans`) from registers. Under the two-word numerics, heads and values to 256 wide run on `ATTENTION_FLASH`'s `lk_attention_flash` (`Launch::attends_flash`, `kernels::attn_flash_queries`, `attn_flash_threads`, `attn_flash_smem_bytes`): a block's queries staged once, each tile's keys 32 values of the head and its values 16 keys at a time by `cp.async` a stage ahead, a thread a 4 × 4 tile of scores and 4 queries' values, each query's maximum and sum across its 16 lanes. Wider heads keep `lk_attention_tiled`. A 512-token prompt's attention a layer drops from 838 µs to 217 on Qwen3-8B's shape and from 473 µs to 106 on Llama-3.2-1B's (one-word), and to 544 and 326 flash style (two-word) (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **A wide chunk's delta rule a chunk of 64 rows at a time.** Under the quantized numerics, a Gated DeltaNet layer over a chunk of more than sixteen rows, its heads at most 128 wide in whole runs of 32 (`kernels::delta_chunks_take`), walks its rule in the chunkwise form (`Launch::delta_in_chunks`, `kernels::DELTA_CHUNKS`), with `Δ = T·diag(β)·(V − diag(γ)·K·S₀ᵀ)` for the chunk's triangular `T = (I + A)⁻¹` of its keys under its decays and write strengths. `lk_delta_prep` takes, for every chunk and head at once, what the memory does not enter: the decayed keys and queries a value of the width at a time, `T·diag(β)` (`T` by 16 × 16 blocks — each diagonal block's inverse by forward substitution in registers, the blocks below a diagonal at a time) and the masked scores a column of the chunk's rows at a time, and each row's decay to the chunk's end — its keys and queries staged 32 values at a time, so it keeps the default shared memory (`DELTA_PREP_SMEM`). `lk_delta_state` walks a head's chunks in turn, a block of two warps 16 rows of its memory (`DELTA_STATE_ROWS`, `DELTA_STATE_THREADS`) held in shared memory a value of the width at a time and each warp's eight in registers; a lane takes one of the chunk's rows across the block's — the memory's products with the decayed keys and queries over the width, the corrections, the outputs — then each warp its rows of the memory the chunk leaves, each warp reading half the chunk's decayed keys and queries. `delta_chunk_scratch` sizes its scratch; `lk_lane_sums` moves to the prelude. The 16-row chunk and the decode step keep the rule a row at a time. Qwen3.5-0.8B's 512-token prompt spends 0.44 ms a layer on the rule, 1.8 a row at a time (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **A wide chunk's `f32` projections in tiles.** Under the quantized numerics, an `f32` projection over a chunk of more than sixteen rows, its rows whole `float4`s and its result its own buffer, multiplies as one `f32` GEMM of 64 × 64 tiles (`lk_gemm_f32`, `Launch::multiplies_in_tiles`), each tile's `k` cut into the parts that give the chip's blocks work (`Launch::GemvF32::split`, `kernels::gemm_f32_splits`), where the GEMV summed each row's dots in `f64` a warp a dot; the precise numerics keep the GEMV. Qwen3.5-0.8B's 36 gate projections of a 512-token prompt drop from 10.5 ms to 1.05 (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **A wide chunk's projections of one activation in one GEMM; its gated pair paired.** A wide chunk's tensor-core projections of one activation by matrices in one format — each into a buffer of its own or into a table's rows its width wide (a value projection into its cache), a whole number of tiles wide — launch as one GEMM over their outputs side by side (`Launch::GemmParts`, `GemmPart`, `lk_gemm_blk`'s `G` 4 over `lk_parts`, at most `kernels::GEMM_PARTS` a launch), chosen after the fusions by structure (`merged_projections`); each output is summed as its projection alone sums it, the last wave cut over the parts' tiles together. A gated pair whose gate and up share a format now pairs on the tensor cores too (`glu_pair`): one GEMM of the gate's and up's tiles side by side, the gated activation — the SwiGLU or `u·act(g)` — and its quantizer in the epilogue (`lk_gemm_blk`'s `G` 5), its last wave cut as the gate's own GEMM cuts it (`Launch::GemvGlu::tail`), so its hidden values are the pair's apart bit for bit. On Qwen3.5-0.8B each Gated DeltaNet layer's query, key, value and gate run as one GEMM, 334 µs where four took 428 (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **The Q8_0 block GEMM streams its matrix.** Under the one word, with `k` a whole number of 128-value stages, `lk_gemm_blk<8, 1>` takes a Q8_0 matrix's levels and scales into shared memory by `cp.async` beside the activation's (`lk_stream_stage`, `lk_cpn`), two stages in flight and a barrier a stage, and loads every `mma` fragment by `ldmatrix` (`lk_ldsm_s8`), where it fetched the matrix's words into registers a stage ahead and stored them back, three barriers a stage; each output is summed in the same order, bit for bit, in every mode — dense, cut, in parts, paired and grouped. A GEMM of 512 rows runs 1.19–1.32× faster (Qwen3-8B's 4096 × 4096 from 534 µs to 432). A 512-token prompt prefills at 15,236 tokens a second on Llama-3.2-1B (12,552 before the merges and the streamed GEMM), 16,204 on Gemma-3-1B (12,118, with its 256-wide heads on the tensor cores), 15,326 on Qwen3.5-0.8B (6,879, with the delta rule and the tiled `f32` projections) and 2,419 on Qwen3-8B (2,036) (`cuda_lane_lock`).
- **The two-word Q8_0 GEMM streamed in tiles of 64 rows, its words folded.** Under two words a Q8_0 matrix's dense tensor-core GEMM takes 64 activation rows a block (`kernels::gemm_blk_rows`, `Launch::gemm_rows`; `lk_gemm_blk<8, 2, 0, 64>`), so two streamed stages of its high and low words fit a block's shared memory and stream as the one word's do; and each 32-value block's high and low dots fold into one exact integer before one conversion and one `fma` (`s/128·d·(128·dot + dotₗ)`), where two separately rounded `fma`s summed them — the two-word lane's wide chunks' sums change, each as exact or more. A 512-row GEMM runs 1.05–1.43× faster (Qwen3.5-0.8B's 2048 × 1024 projections 1.28×, its 1024 × 3584 down 1.43×) (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **256-wide heads on the f16 tensor cores.** Under the one-word numerics, `lk_attention_mma` takes heads and values up to 256 wide (`Launch::attends_on_tensor_cores`), a tile 32 keys where they pass 192 (`kernels::attn_mma_keys`) so the values' sums fit a warp's registers; `attn_mma_smem_bytes` sizes the tile. Gemma-3-1B's attention of a 512-token prompt drops from 5.57 ms to 3.07 and Qwen3.5-0.8B's from 2.20 to 0.94 (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **A step's launches over four streams.** `lane::branches` spreads each form's launches over the step's own stream and three side streams (`branches::STREAMS`): a launch follows every earlier launch that writes memory it reads or writes, or reads memory it writes — memory by where it lives (`branches::regions`: a step-local array or a launch's scratch by its bytes in the arena, anything else by its buffer, an alias where its array lives, `arena::home`, the rule the arena places by). Each launch takes the stream whose last launch it follows, or one whose work it already follows, or the least recently used, and waits on another stream's launch by an event only where nothing it already follows orders the two (a vector clock a launch); the side streams fork from the step's own at its start and join it at its end, inside the step's CUDA graph, and `--profile` keeps every launch on the step's own stream. The arg-max that closes a step names the token and the position it moves on (`plan::Advance`), so every launch that reads them comes before it. `--stagger <seed>` holds each launch back by a stall the seed draws (`kernels::STALL`), so launches the streams leave unordered run in another order under each seed. Qwen3.5-0.8B's decode step runs at 302.8 tokens a second, 281.7 on one stream (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **A wide chunk attends on the int8 tensor cores in two words under the two-word numerics.** `lk_kv_two_words` takes every key and value row a chunk attends into two words once — `s·(h + l/128)` under the row's largest magnitude — the values a column a row, a run of 32 keys at a time, in the order the weights' fragments take them; `lk_attention_i8` walks tiles of 64 keys (32 past 128 wide) arriving by `cp.async`, the next tile's keys and values in flight while this one's are used. Every product of the stored words is taken: a score's high and cross dots fold into one exact integer and its low dot into another, and each weight times its value row's scale is taken into three words under its row's largest in the tile, so the attention computes, to `f32` rounding, the attention of its queries, keys and values taken into two words (`Launch::attends_in_two_words`, `kernels::{attn_i8_keys, attn_i8_smem_bytes, attn_i8_rows, attn_i8_scratch}`; its words and scales in the arena while it runs). Heads and values whole multiples of 32 at most 256 wide; the flash attention keeps the rest. A 512-row chunk attends in 176 µs on Llama-3.2-1B where the flash attention took 362, 306 on Qwen3-8B where it took 602, 285 on DeepSeek-V2-Lite's expanded heads (538), 268 on Qwen3.5-0.8B (405) and 165 on Gemma-3-1B (239), each output a quarter of the f16 attention's distance from the `f32` one or less; a 512-token prompt prefills under two words in 349 ms on Qwen3-8B, 360 before. The two-word quantizer is the prelude's `lk_two_scale`/`lk_two_words`, which `lk_quant_lane` calls. `lk_attention_tiled` orders its per-query writes after every lane's reads by `__syncwarp` (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **An `f32` GEMV's rows cut over a block's warps.** `lk_gemv_f32` takes `parts` warps an output row (`kernels::gemv_f32_parts`: the most, to eight, that keep 128 values a warp and no more than 16 warps a streaming multiprocessor), each summing one run of `k` in `f64`, the runs' sums added in order — each row's order the same in every form; `Launch::GemvF32::split` carries it outside the tiles. Qwen3.5's 16 gates over 1024 values take 3.7 µs a launch, 9.9 a warp a row, and its decode step runs at 309.0 tokens a second under one word, 302.2 before (`cuda_lane_lock`).
- **A model past the device's memory, its matrices in mapped host memory.** `lane::residence::plan` fixes what a lane's program allocates — its arena, the arrays it keeps on the device, every matrix in the order a step misses it least when read from the host (a table only gathered from, then the expert stacks, then the rest, each class from the decode step's last read back), and the spare its graphs take (`residence::{SPARE, SPARE_A_LAUNCH}`) — and the program decides how far down the order to go when it starts: on the device memory free once its context is up and its modules loaded, or the `--device-bytes` it is given, it moves as many matrices as must go to pinned host memory the device maps (`residence::place` is that decision), and refuses by name (exit 4) when the arrays that cannot move outrun the budget. Every array but the arena is carved from one device pool and every moved matrix from one mapped host pool, each at `residence::POOL_ALIGN`, where each matrix took an allocation of its own rounded to the driver's 2 MiB, so the bytes counted are the bytes the device gives up; the program prints them (`DEVICE_BYTES`, `HOST_BYTES`, `MOVED`) and stops (exit 5) when its allocations are not its plan. A kernel reads a mapped matrix through the same pointer, so where a matrix lives changes no value. Matrices that read the same checkpoint rows share one upload (`Plan::shares`): a tied head reads the embedding table's, where it took a copy of its own (270–320 MB on Llama 3.2 1B, Gemma 3 1B and Qwen3.5 0.8B). `CudaChip::mem_bytes`; `Lane::{plan, residence}`, the placement on the chip compiled for (`residence::CONTEXT_RESERVE` held back); `LaneError::Memory`. The program loads its modules when its context starts and allocates its prompt once, at the context's length (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **MXFP4 and Q3_K decoded in registers.** A row dot reads an MXFP4 sub-block's sixteen bytes as four words joined by funnel shifts (`lk_words`) and turns each word's nibbles into levels by byte permutes of the format's table (`lk_table16`, `lk_mxfp4_word`, the IQ4 formats' `lk_iq4_word` alike), where each lane looked its levels up one by one in a table in local memory; Q3_K picks its scale words by selects (`lk_pick4`) rather than an index. No kernel that reads a matrix's rows keeps a stack frame. gpt-oss-20b's experts read their rows at 285 GB/s, 203 before: it decodes at 112 tokens a second on the RTX 3060 under one word (92 before; llama.cpp 99.4), 109 under two, and prefills a 512-token prompt at 2,528 tokens a second (2,271 before; llama.cpp 2,515) (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **The reference's experts in one pass over the threads.** `Reference` runs a layer's chosen experts' gate and up products in one pass over its threads, then their down products in another (`products`), where each expert's product took the threads its own work warranted — three of them for one of DeepSeek-V2-Lite's — one expert after another; each row's sums are its own, so every logit is the same bit for bit.
- **An attention over one tensor as its keys and its values.** The tensor front end now lifts `attention of Q over K and K`, which weighs the keys as the values, with `K` as both operands (`tensor_recognize::qwen::recognize_attention`, `recognize_causal_attention`). The values are now read from the loop that writes the output, instead of being taken as the third tensor in program order (`cuda_attention_gqa_lock`).
- **Near calls in the reference.** `Reference::near_calls` lists each rounding of a quantized activation within `NEAR_ROUNDING` of a half-way point between two levels (`RoundingCall`, named by the activation's values, so one decision covers every projection that reads it, as the lane quantizes it once) and each routing choice within `NEAR_ROUTING` of a tie (`RoutingCall`), in the order made; `Reference::flip` decides a recorded call the other way at every run past it, and `flip_routing` now does so at every run rather than the next only. `quantize_activations` and the reference share one quantizer, so with nothing flipped the bits are unchanged (`cuda_lane_lock`).
- **`CudaKnobs::activations`.** A projection's activation precision in the lane (`Q8MatKind`): `f32` with `f64` sums, the two-word int8 that is the default, or llama.cpp's one int8 word; expert mixtures read theirs as the projections do. `Reference::with_activations` and `reference::quantize_activations` compute the `f64` reference at the same precision, quantizing an activation exactly as the kernels do wherever a quantized weight reads it (`cuda_knobs_lock`, `cuda_knob_consumers_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **Every GGUF weight format in the lane.** A matrix in Q2_K, Q3_K, Q4_K, Q5_K, Q6_K, Q4_0, Q4_1, Q5_0, Q5_1, MXFP4, IQ4_NL or IQ4_XS is uploaded as the checkpoint stores its blocks, and `kernels::BLOCKS` decodes a 32-weight sub-block into int8 levels with a scale and an offset per 16, the CPU dequantizer's arithmetic — a word of four levels at a time for the row dots (`lk_sub4`), one level for a lane that takes one weight (`lk_level`), the IQ4 formats' non-linear levels a word at a time by byte permutes of the table (`lk_iq4_word`): one row dot (`lk_row_dot`) serves the block GEMV (`lk_gemv_blk`, a chunk past 16 rows in 16-row slabs), the block gather and the expert kernels under every numerics, the offset term multiplying the activation's block sum (a `__dp4a` against ones). Q8_0 keeps its split upload and its GEMV, and multiplies a wide chunk on the block GEMM every format takes; `plan::Launch` carries each matrix's format, and a mixture's router, experts and down projection each read their own (`lane_compile_lock`, `cuda_lane_lock`).
- **gpt-oss's pieces through the front end and the lane.** `AttentionSpec::sinks`, `ElementwiseSpec::row` and `MoeSpec::{router_bias, biases, act}` (`MoeAct`) carry sinks, a row combined with every row of a block, and a mixture's biases and clamped SwiGLU from their desugars to every consumer; the single-token CUDA forward and the spatial lowering refuse each by name, the single-op CUDA elementwise kernel reads a row operand at its column. In the lane the attention kernel takes each head's sink, the binary kernel a row operand, the expert kernels an `f32` router (`plan::Router`), its bias, the experts' biases and the clamped SwiGLU, and the `gemv_bias` fusion folds a constant bias into the projection it follows; a matrix the checkpoint keeps in a float format is read as values. The author writes biased projections, sinks and gpt-oss's mixture, and refuses them only for tensor parallelism (`lane_compile_lock`).
- **Windows and scales through the front end.** `AttentionSpec::window` carries a sliding window from the desugar to every consumer: the lane's attention kernel attends the window, and the single-token CUDA forward and the spatial lowering, which attend whole prefixes, refuse it by name. `TensorOpSpec::Scale` (`ScaleSpec`, its factor a `tensor_recognize::Factor`) lowers in the spatial IR as one multiply and in the lane as `lk_scale`, or inside the gather that produced its input (`gather_scale`). `model::bind::rotations` and `RotaryTable` name one pair of rotary tables per distinct rotation, so layers turning by different bases read their own (`tensor_attention_window_lock`, `tensor_scale_lock`, `lane_compile_lock`).
- **A norm's epsilon is the program's.** `tensor_recognize::Epsilon` rides on `FusedRowSpec`, `L2NormSpec` and `GatedDeltaSpec` from the statement that adds it, and the CUDA, SOL and spatial emitters compute with it; a spatial lowering refuses an epsilon that is not a whole number of nano-units (`tensor_norm_epsilon_lock`). `ModelSpec`'s authored source states a checkpoint's `rms_eps` when it is not 1e-6.
- **`codegen_c::CRDT_CORE`: the C family's convergent collections.** `crdt_core.c` ports the data crate's observed-remove set (add-wins and remove-wins) and its observed-remove map of last-writer values step for step: entries in `CrdtScalar` order, a context held as its vector clock, state merge in either direction, deep clone. A collection owns its Text cells (`logos_crdt_own`/`logos_crdt_drop`), a map write drops the value it replaces, and `logos_crdt_set_free`/`logos_crdt_map_free` release a collection whole. It is the one implementation the C generators, the stepped tier and RISC-V lower `SharedSet` and `SharedMap` onto; a tier refuses those kinds by name until its lowering lands (`CrdtKind::lowered_by`). `crdt_core_differential_lock`.
- **`optimize::iteration`: how a `Repeat` may iterate, proved once for every backend.** `prove_iteration_facts` marks `Fact::IntRange` on a `Repeat for v from a to b` whose bounds are Ints and `Fact::LentIteration` on a `Repeat for x in xs` over a sequence nothing the loop runs can change (its body, a lend to a `mutable` parameter, a callee's program bindings; `## No borrow` opts out). The VM compiler lowers an Int range to a counted loop, its bounds evaluated once and no list of the range built, and a lent walk to an index walk over the sequence in place, both loops the native tier compiles; the Rust tier reads the sequence through a shared borrow (`iteration_lock`, `seq_iteration_borrow_lock`).
- **The tiny-function inliner takes pure builtins and compound arguments**: a helper whose body is `Return <expr>` over pure builtin calls (`rotr`, `word32Shr`, …) inlines like one over operators, a name the program defines never taken for a builtin; a call whose arguments are not all names or literals inlines where it is a `Let`, `Set` or `Return`'s whole value, each compound argument bound once ahead of the statement, in order (`inline_tiny_lock`).
- **`ui_bridge::{Engine, set_engine}`** choose the engine an in-process run takes (the VM with JIT tier-up, the VM alone, the tree-walker), each over the same optimized program; **`ui_bridge::vm_listing`** prints the bytecode the VM runs, a section per function (`vm_listing_lock`).
- **`vm::perf_map`**: `LOGOS_PERF_MAP` appends each installed native region to `/tmp/perf-<pid>.map` named after the LOGOS source, so `perf` profiles the JIT in the program's terms; `LOGOS_JIT_CODE=<dir>` keeps each region's bytes as `<n>.bin` with an `index.tsv` of start, size and name, for a disassembler (`jit_perf_map_lock`).
- **`semantics::exact`: the exact tower in the value model.** `RuntimeValue::{Algebraic, ExactReal, Hyperreal, Hypernatural, Ordinal, Depends, Converging}` with arithmetic that lifts a standard operand exactly (a float as the dyadic rational it is), lifts every operation over residue branches and provisional evidence, and keeps one representation per value: a hyperreal with no infinitesimal part is its standard value, a polynomial in `1/dt` that is an integer for every admitted `⌈1/dt⌉` is that infinite integer, a dependence read on its classes is flattened and a dependence of integer polynomials is one infinite integer. `floor`, `ceil` and `round` reach every exact type; exact division (`ExactDivide`) stays exact on every residue branch while the integer `/` floors; equality between computable reals is fallible on both engines (`compare::equality`, `eq_op`), and a branch on a residue-dependent or provisional value is refused with the same sentence on both. Builtins `infinitesimal`, `standardPart`, `isInfinitesimal`, `isLimited`, `infinitelyClose`, `generatorOf`, `limitOf`, `provisional`, `atTheLimit`, `everyNatural`.
- **`supertask`: the supertask engine.** Resolves, before any tier runs the program, every loop whose clock steps by a declared infinitesimal (an affine closed form through `affinePower`, whose root-of-unity structure `A^z·(A^p − I)^m = 0` is read off the characteristic polynomial and checked by multiplying it out, or the cycle its state enters through `cycleAt`, bounded by `stateSize`) and every `Repeat for every natural` (`searchCandidates` for a linear condition, a budgeted run with revision counts otherwise), keeping the loop as written for a finite trip count (`tripCount`). `resolve_program` is the one entry every seam calls for division and supertask resolution.
- **Flows.** A loop stepped by `dt` whose update is `x ← A(dt)·x` with `A(0) = I` (an Euler step of a linear differential equation, its terms polynomials in `dt`) ends in a `Germ`: `the standard part of x` after `While t < 1: Set x to x + x * dt.` is `e`, an oscillator's is `cos(2)` and `-sin(2)`, and the value itself prints `e - (1/2)·e·dt + O(dt^2)`. `affinePower` reads the update's dependency closure: a standard characteristic polynomial takes the root-of-unity closed form, an infinitesimal one the flow. Hyperreals carry either a rational function of `dt` or a germ (`semantics::exact::HyperForm`), and arithmetic, comparison, `floor`/`ceil`/`round`, `is limited` and the standard part read both.
- **Nonlinear flows.** The supertask engine reads a body as polynomial forms over its state: an update of degree at most one is the affine closed form, and a polynomial Euler step `x ← x + dt·f(x) + …`, each of its other coefficients reading the clock's step, ends in `polynomialFlow(f, x, N)`. On every residue class of `N = T/dt + δ` the end state is the solution of `x' = f(x)` at `T` from the start's standard part, a germ known by its standard part; a scalar solution certified to blow up before `T` ends unlimited. A clock no update reads advances by `N` steps exactly. Builtin `polynomialFlow`.
- **The exact semantics run on base's core.** `semantics::exact` is `RuntimeValue`'s implementation of `logicaffeine_base::tower::TowerValue` and `ValueAlgebra` (delegating to `semantics::{arith, compare, builtins}`), with the module's functions as concrete wrappers; `supertask::runtime`'s builtins run `logicaffeine_base::supertask`. Same answers, same refusals, one implementation shared with compiled code.
- **`analysis::tower`: where the exact extension reaches.** `TowerTypes::of_program` answers, per scope, which bindings, function returns and parameters may hold a value of the exact extension (the infinitesimal and what is computed from it, a declared exact type, a supertask's state, an item of a reached list), by a fixpoint over the whole program; a program the extension never reaches has no answer and costs nothing. The optimizer's facts carry it: `optimize::fold::program_bool_syms` and `function_bool_syms` build `BoolSyms` with the program's reach, used by `propagate` and `sccp`.
- **AOT-Rust runs the exact extension.** A binding, parameter or result `analysis::tower` reaches is a `logicaffeine_data::LogosValue` in the generated Rust (`codegen::tower`), whatever its declared type, and an expression over one is emitted on that model: arithmetic, relations, `not`, the bit operations, `and`/`or` read by decided truth (the right side only when the left does not settle it), a list's items, length, membership, `Push`, `Set item`, `Pop` and `Repeat`, the lifting builtins, and every builtin of the extension through `logicaffeine_data::exact::logos_extension`; each operation answers through `failure::answered`, which stops with the interpreter's sentence, and a condition on a residue-dependent value stops as the interpreter does. A call's argument converts to a reached parameter, a reached function's `Return` converts its value, and a program the extension never reaches emits exactly what it did before. The declared names `Algebraic`, `ExactReal`, `Hyperreal`, `Hypernatural` and `Ordinal` lower to `LogosValue`. The reach is read once per program and restored when its emission ends (`emitter_statelessness_lock`). `TowerTypes` gains `declares` and `param_at`, reads the extension's builtins from `logicaffeine_base::extension::Builtin::ALL`, and reaches the binding a `Pop` from a reached list fills. Every extension probe of the tier-parity harness runs bit for bit on the tree-walker, the VM, `largo run --interpret` and AOT-Rust debug and release.
- **Recursion over the exact extension's values compiles.** A function the extension reaches keeps tail calls and the accumulator rewrite, so deep recursion runs in constant stack as on the interpreters: a tail call converts each argument to its parameter's value model on the way back round the loop, and the accumulator is a `LogosValue` folded by the model's `+`/`×`. Such a function takes no memo table, no shift-based closed form and no byte-table predicate, and two mutually tail-recursive functions are merged into one loop only when neither is reached. A statement peephole written for machine integers no longer claims a window that names a reached binding (a counter counted up by `+ 1` and later made exact). The conditions inside a tail-recursive or accumulating function go through the one truthiness rule (`truthy_cond_wrap`), so a non-`Bool` condition there compiles.
- **The extension's builtins run through base.** `semantics::builtins::call_builtin` hands every builtin of the exact extension to `logicaffeine_base::extension::call` (`BuiltinId::extension` maps one to the other), the same dispatcher compiled code calls.
- **Marshalling the exact tower.** `WireValue::{Exact, Scaled, Depends, Converging}` and the native wire tags 86–89 carry every exact value in its canonical encoding; a JSON payload is validated before it is rebuilt.
- **Exact values by context (`resolve_division`).** The exact-context resolution `resolve_program` runs first now reads more than `/`: in a context that declares an exact field type `sqrt`, `exp` and `ln` become `exactSqrt`, `exactExp` and `exactLn` (with their argument an exact context), and an unbound `pi`/`π` and `e` become `exactPi()` and `exactE()`; in an `Ordinal` context an unbound `omega`/`ω` becomes `exactOmega()`. A function the program defines itself is never replaced; `exp` and `ln` are replaced only as the library's definitions, which `resolve_program` is told by a new `library` argument every seam passes (`loader::LibraryProvenance::functions`, or `loader::library_owned_functions` of the text it parsed). The contexts are a declared binder, a parameter (also as a call's argument), a function's result and an assigned variable, top-down, and bottom-up an operation, a comparison or a call to `sqrt`/`exp`/`ln` with an exact operand, a call to a function of the program declared to return an exact type, and the base of `**`. `BuiltinId::{ExactSqrt, ExactExp, ExactLn, ExactPi, ExactE, ExactOmega}` hand them to `logicaffeine_base::extension`.
- **The exact extension on both C backends** (`codegen_c::tower`, `codegen_c::from_program::tower`, `codegen_c/tower.c`). The AST generator types a binding, parameter or result the extension reaches (`analysis::tower`), and every value of a declared exact type, as `LTower`, a collected cell around a handle into the value runtime; an expression is exact when an operand is, and a value of another kind that meets one converts on the way in (an exact division in an exact context included). The bytecode walker holds a register exact per web (the definitions that reach one read, with a push, an item store, `+=` and a copy joined to what they continue, and every value a builtin of the extension reads exact, down to the innermost list it is written from), so a register reused for unrelated values keeps its own kind; every definition of an exact web lands as a cell, a parameter of one converts as its function begins, and calls, results, globals and fields convert where they meet one. A change goes through the owned flags (`own_target` copies a shared cell), and no cell is ever freed by hand.
- **`codegen_c::value_runtime`**: `links(code)` (whether emitted C declares `LOGOS_LINKS_VALUE_RUNTIME`), `link_args(code)` (the archive and the system libraries it calls; nothing for any other program) and `archive()`, which builds `logicaffeine-data` with `c-abi` as a static library in a scratch project of its own (this checkout's crate in the monorepo, the crates.io release of this compiler's version elsewhere) under a file lock, keeping each build's archive in a file named by its content.
- **The C collector holds memory outside its heap.** `lc_gc_on_release(object, release)` calls `release` once a collection finds `object` unreached, and at exit for every one left; `lc_gc_count_outside(bytes, report)` lets a linked runtime's bytes count toward the next collection and add to the heap report; `lc_gc_note_outside(grown)` counts their growth. A program that registers nothing collects exactly as before. `LOGOS_HEAP_REPORT` adds `values=` and `values_high=` for a program that holds exact values.
- **The bytecode walker lowers date, instant, time and span constants**, and refuses by name arithmetic on a time value and a comparison of one with a value of another kind, which it computed on the numbers a slot holds them as.
- **`compile_to_cuda` refuses a program the exact extension reaches by name**: its values live in a runtime a GPU thread cannot call.
- **The exact extension on linked WebAssembly** (`vm::wasm` `Kind::Tower`, `module::tower`). A register holding a value of the extension is an i32 handle into the `logos_rt_value_*` runtime, which the linked runtime build now compiles with `logicaffeine-data/c-abi` and links into the module, with the adapters to the module's own representations (`logos_rt_value_checked`, `to_text`, `of_text`/`of_bigint`/`of_rational`/`of_decimal`, `length_i64`, `truth_i32`, `contains_i64`, `to_i64`, `call_list`, `tuple_of`). Which registers hold one is inferred per register after the live-range split: what an op of the shared rule writes, what a builtin of the extension reads and every copy of it in either direction (seeded from the code's shape before the inference, so a list a supertask's cycle search grows is one from the start), and every parameter some call hands one (a fixpoint across functions). A register holding one holds every value as one: an op that computes another kind into it computes into a register of its own kind, its twin, and the value lands converted; a loop of such values over a list of machine values builds its frame of the runtime's values; a value read as a machine integer (an index, a range bound) is narrowed with the interpreter's refusal. Calls, results, globals and fields convert where they meet one, a copy into a second place copies the value, and a record's copy owns its exact fields' values, so a push onto one (`ListPushField`) lands in place in the record's own. A self-contained module refuses a program the extension reaches by name. The C bytecode walker narrows a cell read as a list index or a range bound (`int_operand`) and pushes onto a record's exact field (`push_to_exact_field`), both of which it refused.
- **`vm::tower`: which registers of bytecode hold a value of the exact extension**, the rules the C walker and linked WebAssembly both read: `exact_write` (what an op writes as one, given which registers hold one, text or a map), `demands` (what an op hands the runtime as one), `lifts` and `binary` (the runtime operation of a lifting builtin or an operator), `declared` and `reaches` (whether a declared type, or a program, reaches the extension).
- **The exact extension on RISC-V** (`codegen_riscv` `Kind::Tower`, `emit::tower`, `codegen_riscv/tower.c`). A register holding a value of the extension holds its `LTower` cell; the shim of a program the extension reaches embeds the extension's C core (`codegen_c/tower.c`, now shared, the C backends' own conversions in `tower_of_c.c`) and an adapter whose entry points take each operand with the shape of its slot (form 22 a cell, 23 `nothing`, 24 a span) and convert it, so every operation is one call. Which registers hold one is settled per exact web (`vm::tower::webs`); a definition of an exact web that computes another kind lowers as what it computes and converts, a parameter some call hands one holds one (a fixpoint with the other signatures), and the operands of an op the tier lowers itself are presented in scratch slots in the kinds it reads them as. `codegen_riscv::runtime` answers what a program links (`Runtime { c, link_args }`, with `links_value_runtime` reading the `logos_links_value_runtime` symbol the assembly declares). `codegen_c::value_runtime` builds its archive for a `Target` (`archive_for`, `archive_link_args`): the host, or `riscv64gc-unknown-linux-gnu` linked statically, its system libraries spelled for a static link.
- **Hybrid supertasks** (`supertask::hybrid`). A clock loop whose body has `If guard:` jumps beside Euler steps of a polynomial field, or jumps that read the clock, is encoded for `hybridFlow`: the body in order (each run of flow statements as one polynomial update per slot, each jump as the check of its guard), every guard and condition as comparisons of polynomials in the state under `and`, `or`, `not` and `is infinitesimal`/`is limited`, and every statement a jump runs as a polynomial or a value that reads none of the state. The loop as written stays for a standard step, the clock advances by `N` steps exactly, and a body the encoding does not cover (an `Otherwise:` beside a flow, a jump that does more than set values) stops with its reason. A toggle that neither flows nor reads the clock stays with the cycle strategy. Builtin `hybridFlow` (`BuiltinId::HybridFlow`, appended to the end of the catalog so every wire id stays put).
- **`vm::tower::webs`: the exact webs of a region**, the reaching-definition analysis the C walker ran, over a region the backend describes through `WebFlow`; the walker and RISC-V both read it.
- **A store keeps a map or a record its own kind on every tier that types statically.** `analysis::tower` reached a map or a record an exact value was stored into by key, which the bytecode rule (`exact_write`) never did; a binding only ever made a map or a record now stores under a key. The C walker and RISC-V keep a map of exact values one across later stores and convert a machine value stored into it; linked WebAssembly makes every value stored into such a map, and read from it, exact (`exact_maps`) and gives each a handle of its own; the AST C backend holds one in the generic map families, `Map_str_<V>` and the new `Map_i64_<V>` (`CType::MapI64Value`, `emit_generic_map_family`), and types statements in its tuple, nested-list and map-value collectors with the program's exact reach.
- **A function's store into a root binding reaches the root binding.** `analysis::tower` keyed every name a function assigns to the function; a name the function never binds (no parameter, `Let`, loop variable or `Pop … into` of its own) is the program's root binding, which is what every function reads. The C walker's global kinds take a value of the exact extension from any region's store, whatever the global first held or was inferred as; linked WebAssembly grows its global kinds with the exact stores of every region (`module::tower::exact_globals`, a fixpoint with the planning), converts a machine value stored into an exact global, and lowers `DeepClone` of an exact value as its copy.
- **One refusal for the tiers that hold no exact value** (`compile::exact_refusal`, `refuse_exact`): the Tenstorrent, analog, Plasticine, RDU and CUDA entries, the Silicon ISA, the soft core's encoder (`full_asm::compile_to_full_words_w`) and the fabric's lifter refuse a program whose bytecode the extension reaches (`vm::tower::reaches`) before lowering it, in one sentence naming the tier and what cannot call the runtime.

### Breaking changes
- **`the weekday of` / `weekday_of` answers the ISO 8601 day on every tier: Monday = 1 … Sunday = 7.** v0.10.1 answered 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday. `weekday_iso_convention_lock`. Migration: `(the weekday of d) mod 7` gives the old Sunday-zero numbering; `calendar.lg`'s `dayName`/`dayAbbr` take the ISO number.
- **The checker refuses programs v0.10.1 compiled.** `check_program` no longer ends in a catch-all: it checks what `Show` prints and every argument of a statement-form call, checks arity before argument types, checks a pushed element, a field or index assignment and both sides of a `Swap`, and widens an `Int` argument into a `Real` parameter but never the reverse. A call to a name defined nowhere is refused at check time with `Unknown function: <name>`, even in a branch that never runs; a type nothing declares (`a new Todo` with no `Todo`) is refused at the statement that names it; an `Import` below a module's first `##` header is refused where it stands (v0.10.1 ignored it). `type_checker_rejects_lock`, `unknown_call_lock`, `undeclared_type_refusal_lock`, `imported_record_fetch_lock`. Migration: fix the call, field or argument type the refusal names; declare or `Import` the type; move `Import` lines above the first section header.
- **A multi-file project is scoped per file.** A file sees only the modules it imports directly, so a file that used a type only a transitively imported module declares is refused (v0.10.1 merged every transitive module's types). A module path that resolves outside the project root, the `Largo.toml` root and the declared dependency roots, or that cannot be canonicalized, is refused before it is read. `multimodule_functions::multifile_scope_rejects_transitive_leakage`, `multimodule_edgecases`. Migration: `Import` the module that declares the name in the file that uses it; declare out-of-tree code under `Largo.toml` `[dependencies]`.
- **A compiled binary's runtime error prints the LOGOS message alone on stderr and exits 1.** v0.10.1's generated `main` let the Rust panic report through and exited 101; `Require`/`Assert` now report their own words, never `assertion failed: <expr>`. `semantic_probes_lock`. Migration: a script keyed on exit status 101 keys on non-zero.
- **AOT-Rust `text_from_bytes` fails on bytes that are not UTF-8**, with the tree-walker's and the VM's words (`text_from_bytes(): invalid UTF-8`), where v0.10.1 substituted U+FFFD. Migration: check the bytes with `validUtf8` (`strings.lg`) before converting.
- **`compile_to_c` / `largo emit c` programs print what the interpreter prints.** A record shows as `Point { x: 10, y: 20 }` with fields sorted by name (v0.10.1: `Point(x: 10, y: 20)` in declaration order with `%g` floats); a Real shows as its shortest round-trip decimal, never `%g`; maps, sets and enums show. A read of an absent map key (`missing map key`), an index outside a sequence (`index out of range`; a negative index counts from the end), an unmatched `Inspect` and a zero divisor fail with the interpreter's words and exit 1, where v0.10.1 answered `0` or `""`, read past the buffer, or died on SIGFPE. `struct_show_cross_tier`, `phase_codegen_c`, `ultimate_gauntlet_lock`. Migration: guard a map read with `contains`, and check a divisor or index before using it.
- **A module from `compile_to_wasm` imports the new host functions it uses.** A program that divides, indexes or can fail imports `env.logos_rt_fail` and `env.logos_rt_fail_at`, one that draws a random integer or calls `contains` imports `random_int` or `text_contains`, and a module from `compile_to_wasm_observed` imports `obs`; a host object built for v0.10.1's imports cannot instantiate such a module. `wasm_host_abi_lock`. Migration: regenerate the host with `largo emit wasm` (or `web_host::wasm_host_shim`), or supply every import `vm::wasm::host_import_signatures()` lists.
- **`Option` is a tagged value to an embedder.** On the tree-walker and the VM `some x` is `RuntimeValue::Inductive` of `Option`/`Some` with the payload and `none` is `Option`/`None`, where v0.10.1 used the bare payload and `RuntimeValue::Nothing`; `some 0` differs from `none`, `some none` is present, and a bare record no longer satisfies a `Some` arm. `Show` and `= nothing` are unchanged. `option_tier_lock`, `option_of_record_lock`. Migration: match `Inductive` values of `Option` in host code; wrap a value with `some` where a `Some` arm must see it.
- **`Word16` is its own 16-bit ring on the tree-walker and the VM.** `word16(x)` was a `Word32` in v0.10.1, so `word16(65535) + word16(3)` printed 65538; it is now 2, as in a built binary. Comparing, dividing or combining Words of different widths is an error. `word_narrow_all_tiers`. Migration: convert explicitly, for example `word32(intOfWord16(x))`.
- **`args()` on an in-process engine answers `["logos"]` when no argument vector was supplied**, where the v0.10.1 interpreter answered `[]`; the first element is the program name on every tier. `stdlib_env_args_all_tiers`. Migration: read the arguments from `item 2 of args()` on.
- **Rust API.**
  - `codegen::with_seeded_select` is renamed `codegen::with_deterministic_replay` (no alias). Migration: call `with_deterministic_replay`, or `compile::compile_to_rust_deterministic`.
  - `codegen_c::codegen_program_c(stmts, registry, interner)` takes `policies: &analysis::policy::PolicyRegistry` as a fourth argument. Migration: pass `&PolicyRegistry::new()`, or call `compile::compile_to_c`.
  - `interpreter::needs_async(stmts)` is `needs_async(stmts, interner)`. `analysis::unify::InferType::UserDefined(Symbol)` is `UserDefined(Symbol, Vec<InferType>)`. `vm::NativeTier::compile_function` takes `cow_exempt_regs: &[u16]` after `param_kinds`: the only registers the VM writes a shared list through in place, so a backend storing into a list in place must prove it unshared or in that set. It also takes `enums: &[vm::EnumTypeDef]` after `constants`, the program's declared enum types, which give an `Inspect` arm's payload kinds. `optimize::bta::analyze_function_bt` takes a callee-global write set whose type is crate-private, so it is no longer callable from outside the crate.
  - Public enums gain variants, so an exhaustive `match` needs new arms: `analysis::LogosType` and `analysis::unify::InferType` (`Element`, `Event`, `View`); `analysis::unify::TypeError` (`UnknownFunction`); `interpreter::RuntimeValue` (`ComplexF`, `View`, `Action`); `interpreter::ListRepr` (`Words32`); `semantics::builtins::BuiltinId` (over 120 variants); `semantics::crdt::CrdtValue` (`Counter`, `PnCounter`, `Map`); `vm::Op` (`RevSwap`, `RevXor`, `RevNeg`, `RevAdd`, `RevSub`, `CrdtJoin`, `BigIntWide`, `Obs`, `Tcp`, `Serve`); `vm::BoundaryType` (`Result`, `Set`, `Option`); `vm::{SlotKind, RegBox}` (`Word32`, `Word64`, `CharText`), `vm::ObservedKind` (`Word32`, `Word64`, `Word32List`) and `vm::PinElem` (`Word32`); `vm::wasm::WasmLowerError` (`UnsupportedOwned`); `codegen_sva::sva_to_verify::BoundedExpr` (`BitVecIte`). `Op::MagicDivU` gains `divisor: i64`.
  - Struct literals need the new fields: `vm::CompiledProgram` (`declared_types`, `observation_sites`, `site_at`; `..Default::default()` fills them), `ui_bridge::TheoremCompileResult` (`discharged`) and `ui_bridge::TheoryCompileResult` (`axiom_names`).
  - `CompileError::Parse` displays the parse error's explanation instead of its `Debug` form, and `codegen_sva::hw_pipeline::HwError::ParseError` carries the described diagnostic instead of the `{:?}` dump. Migration: match on the variant, not the text.
  - `codegen_sva::wprog_engine_rtl::{SHOW_INT, SHOW_FLOAT, SHOW_TEXT, SHOW_WORD32, SHOW_BOOL}` are `codegen_sva::chip_protocol::{TAG_INT, TAG_FLOAT, TAG_TEXT, TAG_WORD32, TAG_BOOL}`, with the same values. Migration: import them from `chip_protocol`.
- **`Assert that` is checked in every build on AOT-Rust.** AOT-Rust lowered it to `debug_assert!`, so a release binary ran past a false assertion that the tree-walker, the VM and a debug build stop at; it now stops there in every build, as `Require that` does. `phase50_security`. Migration: a check meant only for development belongs in a `## Test`.

- **`Pop` from an empty list stops the program.** `resolve_program` puts `If length of <list> equals 0: Fail with "Cannot pop from <list>: it is empty".` before every `Pop` that may meet an empty list (`resolve_pop`), the list named as the program writes it (`xs`, `b's items`, `item 2 of xss`), so the tree-walker, the VM, the JIT and every backend stop there with the same sentence; v0.10.1 bound `nothing`. A pop proven to find an item gets no check: the proof keeps a lower bound on the length of every list it can name through literal lists, copies, pushes and pops, conditions on `length of` (a `While length of xs > 0` body, the rest of a block after `If length of xs is 0: Return.`) and loops that change a list only by pushes and pops at their top level, over a trip count a constant range or `1 to length of` the list fixes; whatever a statement or a call may write loses its bound. `pop_empty_list_lock`. Migration: test `length of xs` before popping.
### Changed

- The Caravel tape-out files `codegen_sva::wpe_caravel` writes carry MIT SPDX headers, and `user_defines.v` keeps the
  scaffold's copyright line (Efabless Corporation) for the GPIO mode table it carries from the scaffold.
- Licensed under the MIT License, which replaces the Business Source License 1.1, and the package ships it as `LICENSE.md`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs`: the dual-clock FIFOs take `HOLDS` (a writer that holds a refused word, checked in
  simulation) and `TOKENS` (a writer of pulses, counted on the write side); every FIFO of the shell says one but the
  doorbells' and the status records'.
- `spatial::decode_engine`: `logos_pipe` and `logos_image_stream` clear with their clock; `rtl::column` lays the
  activation copies at a power-of-two pitch of blocks a token-row.
- `model::author`'s tensor-parallel forms split a model over any width: a chip holds ⌊n/tp⌋ or ⌈n/tp⌉ of a layer's
  KV (key) heads with every query (value) head reading them — past one chip a head, a KV (key) head held by a run of
  chips that split its group — and of the FFN's columns; a chip with no share of a layer folds a zero partial, so every
  all-reduce still draws a partial from every chip. At widths every count divides, the programs and manifests are the
  ones written before. `spatial::fit::admits_tp` keeps the serving stacks' rule.
- `tensor_recognize::qwen::AttentionSpec::window` is `Option<Window>` (`Sliding(W)` or `Chunked(C)`), and so is
  `model::spec::Attention::window`: a window's first position is `Window::first`, its widest span `Window::span`.
- The flat mixture of experts moves only its chosen experts' runs of every expert table — the whole tiles around each
  run, a window clamped to end inside the table, read at the run's offset — or each table once where that is fewer
  tiles, where it loaded every expert's tables whole: 64 experts lower flat on the SN50, and Llama 4's small mixture on
  an fp32 twin. Its router decides at the accumulator's width: logits, scores and the copy the arg-max scans at f32,
  as the blocked form's are (the copy was rounded to bf16, which chose other experts among 64).
- A D5005 build's constraints give every path between two of the shell's compute-side registers the compute clock's
  user clocks; the host's wait for a command returns the last progress record of a command it gives up, and the core's
  progress record carries the engine's word; the step program prints each context's word when a step stops moving.
- The D5005 engine's broadcasts leave registers Quartus can copy beside their loads: the column array's activation
  read address a register a copy, the user-clock side's reset two registers on, `maxfan` on the column core's word and
  first flags, the format copies, the channel multiplexer's holder and held write valid, the fast side's class flags
  and the vector side's widest registers. The co-simulation top ends a run that stops moving.
- `rtl::engine::GdnJob` is a job of rows: `ROWS` and the slots `QUERY`, `OUTPUT`, `KEY`, `VALUE`, `LOG_DECAY`, `GATE`
  (5 bits each) and `VALUE_AT` replace `REGION` and the store beats; every field moved (`BITS` 363).
- The spatial pipeline lowers the one IR (`benchmarks/FUSIONS_WE_NEED.md`, MIGRATE-3). `lower_program_stored` iterates
  the optimized graph's ops, and `spatial::link::link` turns each into its section templates (`Form::Alone` for a
  single-op source, `Form::Section` in a composition) from the graph's tensor facts and a `Prelude`: the tables laid
  out dense (`dense_tables`), the operands' proven intervals and each stored product's Q8_0 view, decided once for the
  program, with `Prelude::agree_layouts` bridging a section's layout to the program's. Two sections join through a
  tensor where the graph's `Touches::read_only_by` says only their two ops touch it. `spatial::pipeline::{TensorOp,
  tensor_op_of, lower_tensor_op, lower_section}` and `RecognizedProgram::read_only_by` are gone; a backend reaches a
  source's ops through `TensorGraph::lift_source` (the walk and its graph, `Lifted`), and a single-op source is
  `TensorGraph::of_op`. Every spatial artifact is byte-identical (`one_ir_identity_lock`).
- The older CUDA forward reads the one IR (`benchmarks/FUSIONS_WE_NEED.md`, MIGRATE-2). `qwen_emit::forward_graph`
  lifts a program's walk, refuses it whole where an op has no kernel (`forward_shim::kernel_refusal`, the forward's
  refusals word for word), and fuses it under `ForwardShim`; `emit_forward` writes the kernels of that graph — a sum and
  its norm, a run of elementwise ops, a producer quantizing its result in its epilogue, each as the shared table
  decided it. `Stage`, `cuda_stage`, the forward's own walk and its chain pass are gone, and every CUDA program it
  emits is byte-identical. The numerics fold quantizes the result of any row kernel (`NormQuant`), as the forward always
  has; the lane's shim takes an RMS norm's alone. The SN50 path cross-checks its sentence reader against the IR's lift.
- The D5005 decode engine reads the one IR (`benchmarks/FUSIONS_WE_NEED.md`, MIGRATE-4). `decode_engine::program`
  lifts the decode step's cut (`TensorGraph::of_cut`) and fuses it under the engine's shim
  (`decode_engine::shim::EngineShim`), and `lower_graph` takes each merge the engine makes as the fused symbol the
  shared table built for it: a block's projections of one checkpoint matrix (`merged_qkv`) as one matvec, each
  convolution and its SiLU (`conv_bias_silu`) as a channel of the conv unit, a delta rule's two gates (`gdn_gating`) as
  the gates unit, and an attention's output gate (`glu_act_mul`) as the sigmoid gate. The shim refuses each op the
  engine has no unit for in `capability`'s own words (`Refusal::gap`). Every engine program is byte-identical.
- The shared front end (`spatial::passes::recognize_stages::walk`) is linear in the program's statements: every
  tensor's fill comes from one walk of them (`tensor_recognize::Fills`, which `recognize_fill` now reads), the names a
  `Set` assigns from another, and whether a name is bound or touched from sets kept as the walk goes; the result is the
  same, statement for statement. Qwen3.5-0.8B's batch of 64 (31,382 lines): 41 s to 0.7 s.
- The lane's planner finds a launch's readers, writers and producer through an index kept current with every change
  to the step (`plan::slots::Slots`, reached only through its methods), and asks whether a launch may move past others
  by range over it (`Slots::crosses`); `sequences` checks each set's moves in one sweep over cached spans. Qwen3.5-0.8B's
  batch of 64 compiles in 2 s, of 128 in 5 s.
- `CudaKnobs::recurrent_state` (`StatePrecision::{F32, Bf16}`, default `F32`): the precision the generation lane stores a
  gated-delta layer's recurrent memory in between steps. At `Bf16` every memory a step carries is a `DType::Bf16` buffer
  (`DType::bytes`), `lk_delta_step` and `lk_delta_state` read and write it as their storage type `SS` (`lk_to`,
  `lk_from`, `lk_kept`), the step rounding the memory after every row it walks and the chunked rule at each chunk's
  end; at `F32` the arithmetic is unchanged. `model::reference::StateStorage` (`Reference::with_state_storage`) keeps
  the reference's memories alike, and the residence plan pools every carried array at its element's bytes, so the
  program carves the whole pool it asks for. On the RTX 3060 the delta rule at Qwen3.5-0.8B's 64 sequences moves half
  the bytes.
- A block of `lk_attention` that takes its row whole and holds its chunks' partials weighs its chunks side by side
  (`lk_attn_side`): each step takes tile `t` of `kernels::attn_side_chunks` chunks at once — as many as its threads'
  value slots hold, eight for 64-wide values — each chunk in the operations it takes alone, so a batch's sequence
  attends bit for bit as the decode step's split blocks attend it. `lk_attention`'s `held` argument is that width; its
  shared memory a tile of scores for each chunk side by side (`kernels::attn_side_bytes`).
- `lk_attention` scores a tile through `lk_attn_scores`: a key's dot with a head of `hd` takes a run of the
  power-of-two lanes at least `hd / 4` (so a warp scores two keys a run at once where the head is 64 wide, its lanes
  16 to 31 no longer idle), and each run sums its keys' dots in one transposed reduction (`lk_tree_halves`: a lane
  keeps half its sums and sends the other at each distance) where every (key, head) took its own warp sum — the same
  pairing across 16, 8, 4, 2 and 1 lanes, the lanes past the run added as zeros, so every score is the same bits.
- `CudaKnobs::cache_precision` (`CachePrecision::{F32, F16}`, default `F32`): the precision the generation lane stores
  an attention's key and value cache in. The step graph names its caches (`StepGraph::caches`: every state tensor an
  attention reads, itself or through the head slices cut from it), and at `F16` each is a `DType::F16` buffer in every
  form, every view into it alike. The kernels share one storage vocabulary in the prelude (`lk_to`, `lk_from`,
  `lk_from2`, `lk_from4`): every attention — `lk_attention`, `lk_attention_shared`, `lk_attention_tiled`,
  `lk_attention_flash`, `lk_attention_mma`, `lk_kv_two_words` — and the tiled per-head matmul and the slice read their
  cache through element types the emitter names (`emit::elems`), widening each value exactly (`lk_stage4` stages a
  binary16 row as the `f32` tile a `cp.async` lands); every producer that writes a table's rows writes through
  `lk_out`, a destination taken from an `f32` or binary16 pointer that rounds each value as it is stored. The planner
  refuses a launch that would read or write an array stored in 16 bits through a kernel that takes it only as `f32`
  (`Launch::typed`); a launch over matrices side by side takes each part's destination as an `lk_part_y`, set from an
  `f32` or a binary16 pointer and read by a kernel through `lk_part_out`. `model_lane --cache=f16`; `model::reference::KvStorage::F16` is its reference, which records each
  cached value it rounds within `NEAR_ROUNDING` of a binary16 half-way point, in levels of its row's scale (as an int8
  rounding of the row is measured), as a near call (`RoundingCall` of word `CACHE_WORD`) and keeps the other neighbour
  where the call is flipped, as it does an int8 activation's rounding.
- `model::author` declares a recurrent layer's gate projections (`Wb`, `Wa` — the checkpoint's `ssm_beta`,
  `ssm_alpha`) quantized matrices, in the decode step, the batch and the tensor-parallel slices, as every other
  projection is: a checkpoint that keeps them Q8_0 (the real Qwen3.5 and Qwen3.8 ones) has them read as Q8_0 rows with
  a quantized activation, as the reference and llama.cpp read them; one that keeps them `f32` keeps the `f32` GEMV and
  the fused gates (`gate_projections`). The authored text is unchanged; the weight manifests move. Qwen3.5-0.8B on the
  RTX 3060: the decode step 324.3 to 329.2 tok/s; a batch of 64, 3,954 to 4,315.
- `spatial::passes::from_q8` folds a product over a Q8_0 weight whose width is not whole column tiles — a
  tensor-parallel chip's 24 of a gate's 48 heads — over its column tiles, the last padded with zero columns as every
  tensor's last tile is (`q8_gemv_applies`); the fold's byte rule counts the padded tiles on both sides. The Qwen3.8
  SN50 fit (`benchmarks/silicon/qwen38_sn50.json`): 610 Q8_0 tensors of 28.574 GB, `f32` 10.6 MB.
- `lk_attention` weighs a tile's values over all its threads: thread `t` takes the block's value slots `t + 256·k` of
  its `G` heads' `G·vd/2` pairs (head `s / (vd/2)`, pair `s mod (vd/2)`), so a block of four 64-wide heads weighs on
  four warps where it weighed on one; the split blocks keep and fold the same slots (`lk_attn_keep`,
  `lk_attn_fold_values`). A block that takes every chunk of its row — a batch's sequence, a chunk's row once the rows
  fill the chip — keeps the chunks' maxima, sums and weighed values in its shared memory where they fit
  (`kernels::attn_holds`, `ATTN_HELD_BYTES`, `attn_held_bytes`; `lk_attention`'s `held`), and scores each chunk once
  where it scored every chunk twice. A launch of more blocks than the chip holds at two a multiprocessor — a batch's
  sequences — holds its registers to three a multiprocessor under the `f32` sums (`lk_attention`'s `MB`,
  `kernels::attn_blocks_a_multiprocessor`). Every output is the same, bit for bit.
- A batch's Q8_0 projection of 33 to 64 rows on the tensor cores takes a block of 128 outputs whose warps each take
  two n-tiles over two m-tiles (`kernels::gemv_mma_shape`, `lk_gemv_mma`'s `NW`), each activation and matrix fragment
  a warp loads feeding two products; a narrow projection of long rows takes blocks of 64 outputs
  (`kernels::gemv_mma_launch`), where its blocks of 128 leave more than half the chip idle and its rows are 2048 values
  or longer. Each output sums as before. On the RTX 3060 at 64 rows: Llama-3.2-1B's down projection 117 µs to 102,
  Gemma-3-1B's 94 to 54, Qwen3.5-0.8B's 54 to 31.
- A gated-delta layer's causal conv keeps its ring by position: position `p`'s row in slot `p mod taps`, written once
  where the row `taps` positions before it was, the window read oldest to newest (`lk_conv`, `lk_conv_parts`,
  `lk_conv_rows` each take the 1-based position — a batch's sequences theirs — and `lk_conv_carry` writes only a
  chunk's last rows into their slots). Every output is the same bits as the ring that shifted a row each step, at two
  thirds of its traffic; Qwen3.5-0.8B's batch of 64 decodes 4487 tokens a second, from 4443.
- A launch in parts takes up to six matrices (`kernels::GEMM_PARTS`, the kernels' `LK_PARTS`): a recurrent layer's
  query, key, value and output gate and its two gates project the layer's activation in one launch, at the decode step,
  in a batch and in a prefill chunk, each part summed as its projection alone sums it. Qwen3.5-0.8B's decode step takes
  220 launches where it took 238, and its batch of 64 no longer runs the gates on one block of the chip a layer.
- `lk_gemv_f32` takes a block's activation rows together (`LK_F32_ROWS`, the host's `GEMV_F32_ROWS`): each weight
  value read once for all of them, each row's sums in the order a row alone sums them.
- `lk_delta_step` keeps a lane's values of its memory row in registers (`DW`, the emitter's `⌈D/32⌉`) where it kept
  them in a 128-byte stack frame; the sums are the same.
- No lane kernel keeps an array in local memory. `lk_attn_scores_in` marks which of a run's keys read a row by a
  mask, where it read an array of their rows back at an index known only at run time (a 16-byte local depot in every
  `lk_attention`); `lk_moe_pick_row` keeps each pick's weight in its own lane of the first warp, where it kept the `K`
  picks in an array (128 bytes, 256 under the `f64` sums, in every `lk_moe_route`). The outputs are the same bits;
  Llama-3.2-1B decodes at 220.7 tokens a second after a 512-token prompt, from 219.8.
- Each attention launch compiles only the path it takes past a row of one chunk — its split blocks folded by the last
  to arrive, a row's chunks held side by side, weighed one at a time, or one chunk — and its run width (`lk_attention`'s
  `PATH` and `RW`; `kernels::attn_path`, `kernels::attn_run_width`), where every launch compiled all four paths and both
  widths and took the registers of their mix: the decode attention spilled 240 bytes under two blocks a multiprocessor
  and a batch's 888 under three, where each path alone spills nothing under two and the held path 8 bytes under three.
  A launch and its shared memory's limit take one name (`attention_kernel`, `attention_shared_kernel`); the limit's
  had left out the quantized store's `QO`. The same bits. On the 3060, Llama-3.2-1B decodes at 232.2 tokens a second
  (229.0), 224.7 after a 512-token prompt (220.8) and 7194 in its batch of 64 (6943); Gemma-3-1B at 239.0 (232.9),
  226.1 (219.7) and 7192 (7003); Qwen3.5-0.8B at 323.7 (320.7) and 315.0 (311.6); Qwen3-8B at 40.41 (40.17) and 39.59
  (39.28).
- A one-row projection in a block format and a decode step's mixture norm their row in their last block only where that
  block is at least the norm's width (`kernels::block_holds_its_norm`; the lane shim refuses `gemv_norm_epilogue` and
  the decode step's `moe_deferred_finalize` otherwise): a narrower block played several of the norm's warps a warp, one
  after another, while the step waited on that one block. On DeepSeek-V2-Lite the 320-thread down blocks and the
  128-thread Q4_K projections playing the norm's 1024 decoded at 142.2 tokens a second on the 3060; with the norm's
  tail cut, 146.7; with the fence alone cut, 142.6. Apart, it decodes at 152.9 and gpt-oss-20b at 110.9 (106.1); a Q8_0
  projection's block is its norm's and still folds. `kernels::moe_down_launch` and `kernels::gemv_blk_norm_threads`
  give the emitter and the shim one shape for each launch.
- `lk_gemm_blk` streams a Q8_0 matrix over a `k` of whole 128-value scale groups through a new path
  (`lk_blk_streamed`): four 32-value stages in flight by `cp.async`, each landing on a barrier of its own and freed when
  every warp has read it (no barrier of the whole block in the loop), its rows swizzled so a `ldmatrix` reads every bank
  once, each scale group with its first stage, two held; paired, a warp's 32 outputs are 16 gate rows and the same 16 up
  rows. The plain grid's blocks take a column tile's row tiles one after another, and a line of blocks its whole tiles a
  column tile at a time, so a column tile's weights are read once; two blocks share a multiprocessor in tiles of 64 rows.
  Every output is summed as before, bit for bit, and a last wave cuts the same tiles; `gemm_blk_smem_bytes` sizes the
  new stages. Each `mma`'s int32 sum is read as a float by starting it at the bits of 1.5·2²³ and subtracting
  (`LK_DOT0`, `lk_dot_f`), the same value the conversion gave. `lk_parts` is declared beside `lk_w`. On the RTX 3060,
  Llama-3.2-1B's 512-token prefill: PREFILL_FIGURES.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl`: the fast side's deepest stages split, each latency constant a clock longer:
  `lane::LANE_VALUE_LATENCY` (the units' sums in fours, then their sum), `fp32::F32_TO_F16_LATENCY` (2 → 3: the
  denormal's shift registered before it), `vector::EXP_LATENCY` (its nearest integer on `logos_rne_p`),
  `vector::LN_LATENCY` (the centre's numerator and the exponent on `logos_i2f_p`), `streaming::ACT_CODE_LATENCY`
  (19 → 20: the value's placement registered before its shift); the user clock's vector unit's int8 codes and the
  vector side's memory stores narrow on the pipelined units, the side waiting the later of the two.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::vector_side`: `logos_vector_side` (`FM_ROWS`, default 4) runs a job whose shape is a float
  matrix's (heads first, a tensor row a head and a slot's row, one `Dot`, one scalar put) on its float-matrix path: the
  rows' inputs held a block RAM a row, eight heads' weight rows a line of each in turn, at most 32 lines owed, each
  queued until no line of its head entered the lanes' adds in the last `FADD_LATENCY` clocks, 16·`FM_ROWS` lanes, each
  row's head through `logos_tree16_p` into its element.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine`: a vectored engine whose memories flush lazily (`Streaming::vectors`, every
  Gated-DeltaNet block's log longer than one token) builds its core without the vector unit, the eager and lazy row
  engines, their read port and their pulses: its recurrence header goes to the hand-off (`DL_SG`, `DL_SIDE`) with only
  the job word's registers. `sw/logos_step.c` reads a greedy program's one beat a row back and takes lane 0 as the
  token, and refuses a program for more rows or a larger store than its build's.
- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs`: the shell makes its compute side's step itself, `logos_compute_clock` inside it
  (`COMPUTE_CLOCK_VERILOG`'s reset released in the user clock at the divided rising edges, its divided clock read by
  nothing), and the PIM top hands it the user clock in every build; the compute side steps at `cce` from `crst_n`:
  `logos_bringup_core` and `physical::card_port_verilog`'s `logos_card_port` take `ce`, `logos_d5005_channel` takes
  `uce`, and `logos_dcfifo_mlab`/`logos_dcfifo_m20k` take `wce` and `rce`, every register of a side, its
  synchronizers too, stepping at its side's enable. The SDC generates no clock and holds the engine's held values
  (`held_*`) into the compute side at half a compute cycle less two; the clock-domain map names `pclk`, `uclk` and
  `mem_clk` alone. The co-simulation runs the user clock at its period (`cosim_user_clock_ps`), the shell dividing it
  (`DIVIDE`), and tells the host program the compute clock's period. So the compute side fits one user cycle, the core
  counts a step's progress the step after and ends a table write on compares of its registers.
- `spatial::decode_engine::physical`'s `logos_fast_port` takes each request into two registers of its kind a clock
  before serving it (`writing` holds while a write waits there), and places each line from a register: the line after
  the one last served a clock ahead, any other a clock later, its run held open meanwhile.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::lane`: `logos_lane_value_p` rounds through `logos_round88_p`, `logos_round88` in five
  stages (`ROUND88_P_LATENCY`), so `LANE_VALUE_LATENCY` is 9; `rtl::act_quant`'s `logos_wide_scale` takes its two
  ceilings over 127 as a product and a shift, and `logos_wide_scale_p` is it in three stages (`WIDE_SCALE_P_LATENCY`).
  `rtl::streaming`'s `logos_act_stream_p` finds a block's largest magnitude a clock behind its beats (the tree's last two
  levels after the register) and scales it through `logos_wide_scale_p`, latching the scale and the word count `go`
  brought four clocks after `go`. `rtl::column`'s decoder sums each unit's sixteen codes in a balanced tree of four adds.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::engine_verilog` refuses an engine with the recurrence unit whose heads' binary16
  memory rows are not whole lines (a width not a multiple of 32): the unit walks a head's memory a row of lines at a
  time, and the state format is the run's knob. So a binary16 log entry is always an even number of beats, two a line, and `logos_gdn_side` appends
  it without a padding line.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine`: the core hands the vector unit's read port to the lazy fold in the fold's
  read-out states (`dl_rd` a function of the state); `rtl::column`'s `logos_column_feed_<set>` clears its gather
  register with the reset, and `rtl::vector_side`'s `logos_vector_side` its line-gathering machine and its write
  request. A hybrid engine decodes from any power-up state.
- `sw/logos_step.c` (`spatial::decode_engine::ofs`) decodes up to the program's sequences at once, their prompts apart by
  `--`: each step writes every sequence's row of the row table and its embedding, runs every row, and reads each
  sequence's own logits row; its record gains `sequences`, every sequence's tokens. The `d5005_afu` example builds an
  engine for `--rows <n>` token-rows a step and writes a hybrid program for `--sequences <n>` sequences; so does
  `scripts/d5005/build-afu.sh --rows`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::act_quant::act_quant_verilog` returns a `String` (the quantizers with the memory
  `logos_act_stream` holds its beats in).
- `codegen_c::from_program`: on machine words a sum or difference with a constant (`Op::Add`, `Op::Sub`,
  `Op::AddAssign`) is checked by `__builtin_add_overflow` / `__builtin_sub_overflow` with the constant as an immediate;
  only a product by a constant is guarded by its other operand's range (`product_domain`).
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::vector_side`: a store into the memory streams a beat a clock through a queue of line
  words, its gather handing a full line to the write queue in the clock the next line begins.
- `spatial::decode_engine::vector::{rmsnorm, gdn_out}` take the knob point's `FpUnit` first and hold `1/n` as a constant
  (`recip(fp, n)`), where they took the reciprocal on a function lane each pass.
- `vm::instruction::FnDebugInfo`: debug-only names for each compiled function's frame slots, so the Studio's debugger names function frames and the call stack. A production compile carries none (`production_compile_carries_no_function_debug_info`).
- `Debugger::output` and `Debugger::error`: what the program has printed at the cursor, and the runtime error it stopped on, read from the recorded state without rebuilding a VM — the Studio's Output column follows the debugger through them.
- `analysis::VarState::MaybeMoved` carries a `MaybeGivenIn` (`Branch` or `Loop`), the construct whose paths disagree about the value, and `OwnershipErrorKind::UseAfterMaybeMove` names it in `given_in: MaybeGivenIn` (was `branch: String`). The refusal reads "…might have been given away in a conditional branch" or "…in a pass through the loop", with a tip for each.
- `analysis::LogosType` and `analysis::unify::InferType` gain nominal `Element`, `Event` and `View` variants (each unifies only with itself; runtime and AOT representation `i64`). The VM boundary (`slot_kind_of_type`/`boundary_of_name`), the AOT type maps, the optimizer's parameter tags and the interpreter's struct-field defaults map the three names to the Int handle.
- `analysis::ownership` follows value semantics, as every execution tier does: only `Give`, and `give` as a call argument, move a value (`ownership_follows_value_semantics`).
- `ui_bridge::expr_to_ast_node` gives every logic expression a Syntax Tree node in the notation the Logic Output prints, with an event's thematic roles as branches. It has no fallback arm, so a new variant must be given a node before it builds (`syntax_tree_is_readable`).

- **Comparison narrowing over intervals** (`optimize::abstract_interp::narrow_by_comparison`, behind `narrow_state` and `narrow_state_negated`). `<`, `<=`, `>`, `>=` and `==`, holding or failing, narrow a name on either side by the other side's interval, not only by a constant.
- **Bit operations over unbounded Int chains lower to checked machine-word arithmetic** (`codegen::expr::try_guarded_bitwise`, `checked_chain_parts`). An operand of `&`, `|`, `^`, `<<` or `>>` that may promote is an exact chain computed as an `Option<i64>` of checked steps over its leaves, bound once and typed `i64` (`chain_leaves`, `bind_chain_leaves`); the raw operation runs when every step fits, `logos_band_exact` / `logos_word_operand` over the exact chain otherwise, in `logos_cold_path`. `bitwise_dual_lock`.
- **The guarded dual moves its exact branch out of line and bounds leaves by the Oracle** (`codegen::expr::try_guarded_dual_chain`, `guarded_dual_parts`). The slow branch is a by-value closure in `logos_cold_path` (a borrowed loop counter escaping into an out-of-line call pinned it to memory: matrix_mult ran 5x slower until the closure took its words by value); a leaf with a finite Oracle range costs its own magnitude and takes no guard term, and a leaf the Oracle proves non-negative takes its upper bound alone (`expr_proven_nonneg`). A guard two select arms share is tested once (`codegen::stmt::branchless_select`).
- **`hintversion`: a loop is versioned on its bounds hints** (`codegen::peephole::version_on_bounds_hints`, from the counted-loop and `While` lowerings). The innermost hinted loop, up to 16 KiB of emitted Rust, with an access of a hinted sequence the Oracle does not prove in range (`hints_discharge_a_check`), becomes `if <every __{arr}_bh_ok> { the loop assuming each hint } else { the loop assuming nothing }`. `bounds_hint_version_lock`.
- **`stridefill`: a loop storing one value into a bit-packed sequence at a fixed stride is one strided fill** (`codegen::bits::match_stride_fill`, `codegen::stmt::try_emit_stride_fill`). `While j <= B` (or `< B`) over exactly `Set item (j + e) of bits to v` and `Set j to j + s`, with `bits` a `LogosBits`, `j` an `i64`, `B`, `e`, `v` and `s` expressions the loop does not change, every store `index_provably_in_bounds` with no positivity guard and `s` a literal or an expression the Oracle bounds below by 1, compiles to `if <cond> { fill_stride_through_unchecked(first, through, s, v); j = j + more * s; <the loop's own Set j to j + s> }`. `through` is `first + (B - j)`, an expression of the loop's invariants once `j` cancels, so the enclosing loop holds no strength-reduced arithmetic of `j`. `bits_stride_fill_lock`.
- **`probefuse`: a map's membership test and the reads of the same key under it are one lookup** (`codegen::probe`, `codegen::stmt::probe_fusion`). For `If m contains k:` with `k` a name or a literal of the map's key type, `m` a map whose `get` answers an `Int`, `Real` or `Bool` by value (`LogosI64Map`, `LogosI32Map`, the dense maps, `LogosMap<K, V>`), and a branch that calls nothing and whose `WriteSet` touches neither `m` nor `k`, AOT-Rust emits `if let Some::<V>(__probeN) = m.get(&k)` and, while the branch is emitted, each `item k of m` beneath it as `__probeN`. The program keeps its reads, so the range oracle and the exactness of the arithmetic around them see what they saw; a branch none of whose reads the probe served keeps its plain test. `codegen_if_arms` emits an `If`'s arms under a given opening line and probe; `optimize::writes` is visible to the crate. Each binding is `__probeN`, numbered from 0 in every program (`probe::reset_names` in `begin_program_emission`).
- **The prelude reads a build-time index of the standard library** (`stdlib_text`, `build.rs`). `stdlib_text` holds the pure functions the prelude reads a module with (`module_code`, `strip_note_blocks`, `defined_names`, `references`, `function_header`, `function_section_ranges`, …), the module list `PRELUDE_FILES` and `index`, which the build script runs once and which panics when a module's spliced code is not a sequence of its source's lines or a library section spans bytes its splice strips. The loader embeds `PRELUDE_MODULES` with `PRELUDE_NAMES`, `PRELUDE_NATIVES`, `PRELUDE_PULLS` (the modules a module's code uses a name of), `PRELUDE_MENTIONS_TEXT_FROM_BYTES`, `PRELUDE_SPLICE` (the source runs a splice keeps), `PRELUDE_FUNCTIONS` and `LIBRARY_SECTIONS`. `apply_prelude_mapped` selects modules by lookup, decides a name the program never writes as a whole word without scanning it (`whole_words`, `may_name`) and copies only the runs of the modules it selects; `library_owned_functions` finds each section by binary search; `prune_unreachable_prelude_mapped_with` takes the prelude's function names from the index.
- **`modwrap`: a remainder by a constant `m > 0` of a value bounded to `[0, 2m)` is one conditional subtraction, and below `m` the value itself** (`optimize::modwrap::mod_wrap`, the one rule; the Rust emitter spells it in `try_mod_wrap` and in every raw chain through `raw_literal_rem`, the C emitter beside `bounded_operation`). The C emitter's `int_range` intersects the body's word ranges, the range oracle and the non-negativity facts, and `infer_numeric_type` types a widened item read (`Vec<i32>`, `i32` slices and arrays, byte views; `item_read_widens`, shared with the emitter) and `+ - * / %` of two Int words as `i64`.
- **`branchlessselect`: a two-armed assignment computes both arms and selects** (`codegen::stmt::branchless_select`, from each arm's `codegen::expr::select_arm`). An arm qualifies when its syntax cannot trap (integer names and literals under `+ - * & | ^`, `/` and `%` by a literal that neither divides by zero nor overflows) and its machine word is raw or the fast side of a guarded dual over names; at least one arm must be guarded. The guards, tested on their own, choose between the select and the original branch, which `codegen_if_branches` emits one level in. The guarded dual is built as `GuardedDual { binds, guard, fast, slow }`, which `try_guarded_dual_chain` formats as before. collatz: 1.26x faster.
