- **The range oracle keeps affine equalities** (`optimize::karr`, Karr 1976). `RichAbstractState` carries an `AffineSpace` over prover variables (integer scalars and sequence lengths, `affine::len_var`) in generator form: assignments map it, `Push`/`Pop` move a sequence's length by one, joins take the affine hull, a loop header is the hull of its entry and passes, and a counted loop `While x <= B` that starts at most at `B + 1` meets `x = B + 1` at its exit (`karr_counted_exit`). Lengths are tracked only for sequences proven to be sequences (`seq_vars`), where a store keeps the length; anything a statement writes or lends is forgotten. `length_preserving_functions` proves, by a greatest fixpoint over every `Return`, which functions return a sequence as long as a parameter. The equalities holding at each index occurrence (`OracleFacts::karr_at`) feed a second proof pass after each loop's record pass (`try_prove_affine_karr`), with loop guards that read `length of` a sequence the loop never resizes. That pass substitutes each equality out exactly (`AffineSpace::solved`: a free dim equals an affine expression over the pivots) instead of handing Fourier-Motzkin both halves of it, keeps only the constraints connected to the goal, and proves nothing past 48 constraints over 12 variables, so a large generated program's dozens of equalities at one access cost a few small systems. A non-negative `y / k` is bounded by `y`. mergesort: 1.18x faster.
- **`bitpack`: a local `Seq of Bool` is a `LogosBits`** (`codegen::bits`). A uniquely owned sequence declared `a new Seq of Bool`, every mention of which is an item read, an item store, a push or its length, and that no other representation claimed, is declared, presized and filled as a `LogosBits`; reads compile to `item` or, where the oracle proves the index, `get_unchecked`, stores to `set_item`, `set_unchecked`, or `set_true_unchecked` for a literal `true`. sieve: 2.8x faster.
- **`variantbox`: a variant of a recursive enum that holds the enum is one box around its fields** (`codegen::types::VariantShape`, the one spelling of a variant's declaration, pattern and construction, used by the enum declaration, `Showable`, `Default`, the wire codec, the C-export field accessors, `Inspect` and the constructor). The fields live in a payload struct, `{Enum}__{Variant}__Fields`, with the enum's derives and the type parameters its fields name; an `Inspect` arm binds them from the box (`Variant(ref __fields)`, then `let x = &__fields.x;`), so an arm's bindings are the references they were. A fieldless variant allocates nothing. `collect_boxed_fields` holds only the fields boxed one by one (`field_is_boxed`), none under `variantbox`. binary_trees: 2.11x faster.
- **`foldfuse`: a consumer applied to what a builder builds is one function that never builds it** (`optimize::fold_fusion`, deforestation of a catamorphism over a builder). A builder returns a new variant of an enum, or calls itself, on every path, after `Let`s and conditions that cannot fail; a consumer is one `Inspect` of its one enum parameter whose arms return literals, exact `+ - *`, payload bindings and itself applied to the subtree bindings, every subtree binding used. `C(B(args))`, and `Let x be B(args)` whose one use is `C(x)`, call `{C}_of_{B}`: the builder's body with each construction replaced by the consumer's arm for it, on the flags both functions allow. `## No foldfuse` on either function keeps the pair apart.
- **`sortnest`: a nest of adjacent compare-exchanges that sorts a window is one in-place heap sort** (`optimize::sort_nest`). The outer loop counts its passes, its body is the inner counted loop alone, the inner range starts where every pass starts and ends where the first pass ends or one earlier each pass, every bound is affine over Ints the nest does not write, and the inner body, run symbolically on the two items it touches, exchanges `item (j + c)` and `item (j + c + 1)` of one sequence of Ints exactly when they are out of order (ascending or descending) and writes nothing else. The nest becomes `If P: <heap sort of the window> Otherwise: <the nest>`, with `P` (enough passes, the window inside the sequence) built by `version::total_guard` and the heap sort's indices marked in bounds; a `While` counter leaves the sorted branch at its exit value. `optimize::kinds` learns which names hold a sequence of Ints on every binding.
- **`divisorsieve`: a per-number search for a divisor up to its square root reads one sieve of the range** (`optimize::divisor_sieve`). A counted loop over Ints whose body holds `Let d be D0` (a literal at least 2) followed by `While d * d <= i` (or `d <= i / d`) of `If i % d equals 0: <Sets>. Break.` then `Set d to d + 1`, the Sets writing literals, `i` or names the loop never writes and nothing after reading `d`, runs behind a guard on the range's end (at most 2^28, `version::total_guard`): one pass marks every `k * d` with `k >= d >= D0` up to the end, skipping a `d` already marked, and the search becomes `If i >= D0 * D0 and item (i + 1) of the marks: <Sets>`. `optimize::ast_build` holds the statement builders and the block walker `sort_nest` and `divisor_sieve` share.
- **`tracememo`: a pure iteration run from every start of a range stops where it reaches a start already finished** (`optimize::trace_memo`). A counted loop over Ints whose body holds `Let k be i` directly followed by `While C`, nothing after reading `k`, whose condition and steps read only `k`, the inner loop's own locals and names the range never writes, and whose one other effect is `Set acc to acc + H` (a non-negative literal) on an Int read nowhere else in it, with some step able to shrink `k`, runs behind a guard of at most 2^24 starts: each finished start's sum goes into a table, clamped to 65535 for unknown so that the table narrows to 32 bits, and a step that lands on a finished start with a known sum adds it and breaks.
- **Generated `main` runs `Main` through `logicaffeine_system::stack::run_main`,** on a stack switched to on the starting thread, where it started a 64 MiB thread and joined it: the process stays single-threaded, so `malloc` and `free` keep the C library's unlocked path (binary_trees 1.94x faster).
- **`infer_build_length` counts what a sequence held before its build loop.** The length fact is `length >= trips + seed` from the entry length's lower end, and a literal bound gives the entry interval shifted by the trips, so a seeded DP table is proven in bounds (coins 1.18x faster).
- **`Repeat` over a range walks a half-open Rust range** (`codegen::expr::counted_loop_range`) when its end plus one exists: a constant end below `i64::MAX`, or an end `expr_int_range` bounds below it; other ends keep `..=` (sha256 1.22x faster).
- **A counted loop's arithmetic on the items it grows is raw under one comparison (`optimize::item_growth`).** A count kept in a sequence and a dynamic-programming row rolled from pass to pass grow with the passes of the loop that drives them, so their additions carried an overflow check per item that kept the loop from vectorizing. The analysis runs one pass abstractly, every value an interval whose ends move linearly with the passes made (`c + s·t`) and every carried sequence's items in `[lo, hi + g·t]`, solves the least growth `g` the pass preserves, and finds how many passes keep every such sum within i64; `optimize::version` then versions the loop on its limit (`If n <= C: <the loop, those sums raw> Otherwise: <as written>`), under `## No accumbound`. The versioner's clones keep every fact their originals hold. histogram and knapsack: see the root changelog. `item_growth_lock`.
- **Compiled programs are again at least as fast as 0.10.1 where it was faster.** An A/B against the published 0.10.1 and 0.10.0 compilers on the benchmark programs found three regressions on AOT-Rust; two are closed here. `masknarrow` (`codegen::lowbits`, one classifier both emitters share through a `WordProof`) computes a masked LCG in `u32`/`uint32_t` wrapping arithmetic. An item access whose index the range oracle or the non-negativity proof shows is at least 1 goes through `LogosItems::logos_item_front` (`codegen::expr::item_accessor`, at every emitted read and write), whose miss only fails, so spectral_norm's inner loop vectorizes its divides again: 1.21x slower than 0.10.1, now 0.91x. The range oracle bounds `x & m` by a non-negative `m` (`[0, m]`), `|` and `^` of two non-negative words below the next power of two, and `x << s` for a constant `s` until it would wrap (`OracleFacts::expr_proven_at_least`).
- The Rust compilation API documents its example on the public function and checks generated program emission without depending on an optimized local binding.
- **Theorem certificates and the e-graph's Boolean rule check ask the standard library's one registration** (`StandardLibrary::context()`) instead of registering the prelude per call.
- `compile_to_rust` carries its documentation again (it had moved onto a private helper), its example expects the typed binding `let x: i64 = 5;`, and its errors name the browser-surface refusal.
- **The prelude selects from 46 modules, follows their dependencies, and renames the library's copy of a name a program claims.** v0.10.1 auto-imported 10 modules by prepending each whose names a program referenced and did not define. Now `core.md` (`Result`, `Option`) auto-imports first; selection runs to a fixpoint, so a spliced module brings the modules its bodies call (`statistics.lg` brings `math.lg`); modules are spliced after the program's leading prose; "declarer wins" is per name, so a program's own `## To trim` is called at the program's sites while the rest of `strings.lg` still arrives and the library's `trim` is spliced under a hygienic name; a local value named like a library function neither blocks the module nor is taken over by it; and constructs whose lowering calls library functions the author never typed (`:param` routes and pages, `## Session`, `## Redirect`, typed fetch, `Save`/`Load`, `Solve`/`Minimize`, `text_from_bytes`) pull those functions in. Only builtin-backed types and natives still veto a module. `transitive_prelude_lock`, `stdlib_user_name_hygiene`, `stdlib_no_name_collisions`.
- **SHA-3 and SHAKE are LOGOS source.** `sha3_256`, `sha3_512`, `shake128` and `shake256` were `## To native` bindings to a Rust Keccak; they are now the FIPS-202 sponge over `keccakF` in `crypto.lg`, byte-identical (`sha3_native`), and need no native binding.
- **`compile_to_rust_deterministic` and `compile_program_full_deterministic` replay the interpreter's schedule.** Mode B lowered only how a `Select` picked its arm; it now lowers every task, pipe, timer and select of a scheduler program onto `logicaffeine_system::replay`, so a binary built this way replays the interpreter at `LOGOS_SEED`; a program that uses no scheduler compiles as it does in Mode A. There is no CLI flag for it; it is reached through these two entry points. `replay_executor_port_lock`, `e2e_deterministic_replay`.
- **An unmatched `Inspect` names the value's variant and type and the arm to add**, in the same words on the tree-walker and the VM (`semantics::builtins::inspect_unhandled_message`, `BuiltinId::InspectUnhandledDynamic`), and an exhaustive `Inspect` emits no fallthrough. `inspect_unhandled_message_lock`, `gauntlet_findings_lock`.
- **The optimizer knows more.** `gvn` threads its value table into `If` arms and past loops that do not write the operands; `abstract_interp` narrows after widening, so a modulo-clamped accumulator keeps a finite bound and lowers without an overflow guard, and carries entry-guard lower bounds and monotone-decreasing counters to discharge more index checks; `EffectEnv` classifies builtin calls by `BuiltinEffect`. `gvn_scope_lock`, `e2e_int_range_guard_elimination`.
- **Appending to an unshared Text is in place** on the tree-walker: `Set t to t + a + b` is amortized O(1) per append. `text_append_scaling_lock`.
- **The native tier carries machine words.** `Word32`/`Word64` values cross the JIT boundary unboxed both ways, a `chr` result is carried as a code point, a `Seq of Word32` is pinned, and only a function's `mutable` parameter registers are stored through in place, so loop-carried hash words and sha256 tables tier up. `forge_word_arith_lane`, `forge_chr_lane`, `forge_word_list_pin_lane`.
- **A declared `Word64` parameter or return is a native slot kind**, as `Word32` already was, so a function over `Word64` values can tier (it was refused before compiling).
- **The native tier carries the program's own enum values.** An enum parameter crosses the JIT boundary as `Rc::as_ptr` of the VM's node, lent for the call (`vm::ParamKind::Inductive`); an enum result comes back as an `Rc::into_raw` pointer whose strong count the VM takes over (`vm::NativeRet::Inductive`); and the backend receives the program's `EnumTypeDef`s, which `vm` now re-exports with `EnumVariantDef`. A function with an enum parameter used to be refused before compiling. `jit_enum_lock`.
- **Smaller WebAssembly.** A sequence access, a checked Int `+`/`-`/`*` and a push is a call into one shared runtime helper (`__logos_seq_slot`, `__logos_seq_push_slot`, `__logos_add_checked`, …) outside a loop and keeps its fast path inline inside one; `vm::wasm::stackify` keeps a value read once on the operand stack; a self-contained module writes each Text literal once into its data section. `wasm_site_size_lock`.
- **Generated Cargo projects.** Inside a checkout the staged project gets the workspace `Cargo.lock`, and hot-swap builds pass `--target-dir <proj>/target`, so an inherited `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` no longer leaves the native tier unloaded; `implied_crate_deps` adds `rayon`, `memchr` or `serde` only when the emitted code names them and no `## Requires` declared them, and the `http` feature only when a `Serve` is emitted.
- **`codegen_sva::bitblast` blasts into a shared gate DAG.** A multiply chain costs its gate count instead of an exponential tree, and the materialized formula is byte-identical; `BlastSize`, `BLAST_BUDGET_NODES` (8,000,000 nodes) and `BlastRefusal::{Budget, GateBudget}` refuse an obligation before its tree is built, and `blast_work` counts the walks. `BoundedExpr::BitVecIte` (the fabric's mux) is lowered bit by bit, encoded as an uninterpreted `bv_ite` in `verify_to_kernel`, and fails closed in the Z3 translation. `bitblast_budget_lock`.
- **Dependencies.** The crate depends on `bumpalo` (the optimizer's AST arena) and `fancy-regex` (a form control's `pattern=`, checked with lookaround and backreferences by `semantics::dom`), takes `logicaffeine-kernel` with `serde`, and enables `logicaffeine-system`'s `http` feature, the server `Serve` runs on; the published crate carries only `src/`, `assets/`, `README.md` and `CHANGELOG.md`.
- **The Plasticine report names its generator.** The report's and the calibration's `_generated_by`, and `codegen_sva::plasticine_report`'s module docs, name the private place-and-route engine's emitter, `emit_plasticine_report`, not a command a public checkout cannot run.
- **Memoization declines a function the exact extension reaches** (`optimize::memoize_run`), as the Rust emitter's own cache already did: a cache typed by the function's declaration can neither key nor hold the extension's values.
- **The declared exact types reach the bytecode** as `BoundaryType::Builtin` (`Algebraic`, `ExactReal`, `Hyperreal`, `Hypernatural`, `Ordinal`), so a backend lowering the bytecode types a parameter, result or field of one.
- **`fs2` is a dependency on every native build**, serializing the build-to-snapshot window of both linked runtimes (WebAssembly and the C value runtime).

### Fixed

- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs`: every write run a channel's stream engine finishes reaches its side's done FIFO, each
  side counting the tokens its FIFO has not taken, whatever the user clock's ratio to the memory's.
- `tensor_graph::vjp`: the attention rule takes the attention's scale (it scaled by `1/√hd` whatever the program
  stated), its window (sliding or chunked), its sinks (their gradient written into the sinks' tensor), its value width
  and its soft cap; `Softcap` differentiates as `1 − (y/C)²`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl`: an engine of contexts' vector side reads a ring load's region from the rows table of
  its job's context; the conv path counts every beat of x asked and not yet taken, whatever the store's read latency;
  the vector side's store-load and float-matrix landing valids clear with the reset. `codegen_sva::autopipe::check`
  times the pipelined graph without the fan-out counted on the graph before its registers.
- `spatial::passes::fuse_generic`: a map is folded into the map it follows only across a page that holds every value
  the destination registers do; across a bf16 or fp16 page the two computes stay two, the page rounding the
  intermediate as the program apart rounds it.
- `optimize::sequence`: the Rust pipeline lowers a loan something can see as the C pipeline does, `nothing` in
  the lent place while the call holds it. `LogosType::from_rust_type_str` reads a `LogosOpt<T>` as a `T`. A value
  the Rust tier copies out of a `mutable` list or map parameter lent by sharing is a deep copy. The Rust slice of
  a record field borrows the list it reads.
- `optimize::sequence`: for the C pipeline, a loan something can see is lowered with `nothing` stored into the
  lent place while the call holds it (another argument names its root, or the callee reaches it as a program
  binding), never into the record of program bindings; `analysis::check` takes those stores as fitting the place.
  `optimize::capture::free_names` scopes a body's names block by block. `codegen_c`: collection operations read a
  value-or-nothing operand through to what it holds, `## Main`'s in-place changes count among the changed statics,
  and an item of an item of a list is stored in place.
- The tensor front end tells a mixture's gate bias from its up bias by the weight each accumulator sums: an up bias
  given alone was lifted as the gate's.
- `codegen_c`: a sequence of maps or sets is a sequence of tables (`types::seq_of`); `Eq`/`NotEq` between two maps
  or sets of one C type, and between their items inside a sequence, probes each key of one in the other
  (`equality::value_equal`) instead of comparing slots.
- `optimize::accum_bound`'s intervals saturate at the word's edges; `optimize::abstract_interp` hoists its guards in
  array order. `concurrency::marshal` writes every NaN canonically through `wire::put_f64`/`put_f64s`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::ram`: every memory asks for the block RAM its depth fills (`RAMSTYLE`,
  `MLAB_WORDS`); `logos_delay`'s ring is in MLABs to 32 words whatever its width. `spatial::decode_engine::ofs`: an
  engine's build carries its project settings (`ENGINE_SETTINGS`, `hw/rtl/logos_settings.tcl`).
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine`: the engine is busy until every write a step made is on the card, not only
  until its sequencer is idle.
- `spatial::passes::fuse_generic` leaves unfused a consumer that reads the intermediate in more than one slot; it had
  spliced one read and left the others on a deleted buffer (a joined norm and rotation computed the wrong rotation).
- `codegen_cuda::forward_shim::ForwardShim` runs the shared chain rule before `add_norm` (its own rule, `rules`) and
  adds into a norm only at the next stage; `qwen_emit::emit_forward` rounds a run's product the next step adds to with
  `__fmul_rn`, so nvcc contracts no fused multiply-add across the run.
- `pqAuthHandshake` signs and verifies its transcripts under its `ctx`; `mldsaVerifyLogos` refuses a signature with
  trailing bytes; `mlkem768Encaps` and `mlkem768Decaps` apply FIPS 203 §7's input checks and choose K′ or K̄ without a
  branch; the hashes, seeds, keys and messages of `crypto.lg` must be bytes of the right length.
- `chacha20Encrypt`, `poly1305Mac`, `aeadSeal` and `aeadOpen` take a 32-byte key, a 12-byte nonce, bytes, and a block
  counter the data's blocks stay inside of; `aeadOpen` compares the tag without a branch per byte.
- `concurrency::channel::pq_handshake_responder` and `pq_handshake_finish` return the `KemError` for a malformed peer
  `ek` or ciphertext instead of panicking.
- The C tiers' `randomInt`/`randomFloat` draw from a per-thread ChaCha20 generator keyed by `getentropy`, with fast key
  erasure, where they drew from SplitMix64, whose every output gives its state away (and from the clock without
  `/dev/urandom`); `c_header_names.txt` gains `<sys/random.h>`'s names and the generator's.
- `sealBytes`, `openBytes`, `sealFrame` and `openFrame` encrypt without compressing first, and refuse a key, nonce,
  plaintext or frame item that is not a byte.
- `crypto.lg`'s `sha256`, `hmacSha256` and `pbkdf2Sha256` refuse an item that is not a byte; `pbkdf2Sha256` takes 1 to
  2³² − 1 iterations, and the tree-walker's and the VM's native PBKDF2 read their arguments through one reader
  (`builtins::pbkdf2_arguments`) with the same reports, where they truncated the count to 32 bits.
- `session.lg`'s `verifySession` and `csrfValid` compare a MAC byte for byte, not with early-exit text equality.
- A fueled VM run charges its fuel for every op it runs, so a run that finishes leaves the budget less what it spent;
  `largo run --max-steps --json` had reported `steps_used: 0` for any program that finished on the VM.
- `crypto.lg` marks Rainbow (broken, Beullens 2022) and Ding's key exchange (a reused secret leaks) as study code.
- `codegen_sva::fabric_soc::emit_nexys_fabric_soc` measures the array's settle on the board (a settle sweep under two
  primes, `LMAX` and `REPS` parameters) instead of streaming a constant cycle count.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::vector_side`: a float matrix's held inputs, a row each, are `logos_bram_w512` memories
  (they were memories declared in a generate block, which Quartus built in flip-flops); `rtl::gdn::lanes_verilog`: a
  head lane's copy of the log likewise, the lanes' text carrying the wrapper. `logos_gdn_fold_m` reads its banks, query,
  gates and log decays into registers (its read-back a clock after its address); `logos_gdn_side`'s and
  `logos_gdn_lane`'s line queues read their head's word into a register, a landing line bypassed beside it.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::ram::delay_verilog` (`DELAY_RING_LEAST`, `DELAY_M20K_LEAST`): `logos_delay` is a
  chain of registers for one or two clocks, the word itself at depth 0, and from three a ring of block RAM
  (`logos_delay_m20k` from 40 bits wide, `logos_delay_mlab` below) where it was a chain at every depth, which Quartus
  built in flip-flops; it moved there from `rtl::vector`. A column core's lane fields and `logos_matvec_pass_p`'s unit
  sums go through it; `logos_column_act_stagger` staggers a pass's activations once a column for `logos_pe8_p`, which
  delays its blocks' digits instead of its packed operands and activations; the pass registers a digit's codes a clock
  before the activations and the core no longer copies its held beat. Every latency unchanged.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::column::ColumnSet::with_octet_lanes` and `ColumnSet::lanes`: an octet's blocks
  leave for that many lanes a row a clock, through a chain of in-order adds. `column_array_octet_lane_clocks`, of
  which `column_array_octet_clocks` is the one-lane case. `rtl::lane`'s `logos_lane_slim_p` is
  `logos_lane_value_p` for a block of one scale.
- `rtl::lane`'s `logos_lane_value_p` built for one activation word takes each unit's signed sums in 19 and 13 bits and
  sums a pair of units' products in its first stage, one DSP block's two 18×19 multipliers, where each unit's product
  was a 27×27 multiplier of its own; built for two words each unit's product is as it was. Values and latency unchanged.
- `rtl::ram`'s registered and held memories 40 bits wide or wider, more than one layer of 512 words deep in a number of
  layers that is no power of two, are stacks of powers of two layers: the deepest the memory's own, each other a `<name>_part` (the one
  memory of the depth it is given) beside it, the read the stack its address named. Every other memory is one memory, as
  it was. Ports and parameters unchanged.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::column::ColumnSet::with_pe_pairs` and `ColumnSet::pe_pairs`: an array's cores in pairs
  on `logos_matvec_pass_x2` (the array's `clk2`), each `logos_column_core_bare_<set>`, the core with its processing
  elements outside it.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::column::matvec_pass_x2_verilog`: `logos_matvec_pass_x2`, two cores' passes on one set
  of processing elements in a clock of twice theirs. Core a's codes are taken with the word between the cores' edges and
  core b's at the next edge. Each core's partials are `logos_matvec_pass_p`'s, at its latency.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::EngineParams::pair_rows`: the beats of the vector unit's pair copies, the
  longest operand a Hadamard or rotary job loads (the most of every engine in `EngineParams::union`).
  `logos_simd_p` takes them as `PAIRS` (by default its registers' rows) and `logos_vector_side` as `PROWS`.
- `spatial::decode_engine::numerics::FpUnit` (`Soft`, `Hard`; `FpUnit::mul`, `FpUnit::CARD`) and
  `model::EngineNumerics::fp_unit`: the multiplier a knob point's products come from, the engine's own or the card's
  floating-point DSP block's (`codegen_sva::fp_hypotheses::Semantics::mul` of the member fp-oracle6's receipt names).
  Every numerics function a product reaches takes `fp: FpUnit` first (`exp`, `recip`, `rsqrt`, `ln`, `ln1p`,
  `softplus`, `sigmoid`, `silu`, `rmsnorm`, `l2norm`, `rope`, `softmax`, `fdot`, `attend_vector`, `attend_int8`,
  `weighted_int8`, `conv`, the `gdn_*` steps, `int8_codes`, `cache_row`), as do `vector::run`, `vector::Func::apply`
  and `vector::Bin::apply`; at `FpUnit::Soft` each is the function it was.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::HardFloat::fp_unit` and `HardFloat::product_target`: the arithmetic a hard
  build's products compute, the engine's at `FpUnit::Soft` and the card's at `FpUnit::Hard`. `Streaming::with_hard_float`
  plans the product's wrapper for it (`fp_hard::plan_for`, `hard_verilog_for`).
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::column`: `logos_column_core_<set>` takes `put` and `load` and rings its beats in `RING`
  places, its next beat taken by moving the ring's head. `logos_column_feed_<set>` puts each beat into its core's ring
  (`w_put`) and keeps no queue of its own; `logos_column_array_<set>` takes `w_put` and keeps a copy of its activations
  beside each channel (`ach[k].act_ram`). Every module of the matvec keeps its own copy of the job's format, a clock
  behind it (`fmt_q`).
- `spatial::decode_engine::machine::VectorJob::chunked` and `rtl::engine::EngineParams::side_rows`. A vector job
  whose rows cross nowhere runs a chunk of its rows at a time; its job word carries the flag (bit 53), and the decoder
  refuses a word whose flag disagrees with the job. The vector side's registers are as deep as the deepest job that
  is not chunked. `capability::covers` judges the side's rows.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::Streaming::with_contexts` and `Streaming::contexts`: an engine of two
  sequencer contexts. Its top has a core a context (`core0`, `core1`) and a read arbiter in front of the card port,
  and refuses a core that writes the card. Its fast side (`fast_contexts`) has a store bank a context, matvec and row
  classes granted a context at a time, and the streaming unit on the channel mux through a `logos_read_credit` a
  channel. `logos_vector_side` keeps a rows table a context. `simulation_twin` gates every core instance by one gate.
- `spatial::decode_engine::machine::DeltaNorm`, `Job::Delta`'s `norm`, `gated_norm_job`, `fold_delta_norms` and
  `Machine::delta_normed`: a Gated-DeltaNet block's gated norm folded into its recurrence job, run as the recurrence then
  the norm's own job, so every value the step computes is the one it computed; a normed job's word says so (bit 55, the
  gate's slot in bits 56 to 60) and is followed by one holding the weight's tensor and the norm's epsilon.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::gdn::{gate_verilog, delta_normed_lanes, delta_normed_rows}` and
  `rtl::engine::Streaming::with_delta_norm`: the head lanes make each recurrence job's gated norm as its read-outs are
  made (`logos_gdn_gate_p`: each head's sum of squares in sixteen lanes, its scale through the tree, recip(width),
  epsilon and rsqrt, then each element's product with the weight and the SiLU of its gate), each head's gate read with
  its query, the weight fetched once a job; `GdnJob::{NORM, Z, EPS, W_LINE, NORMED_BITS, bits}`; the core reads a
  normed job's weight line from its tensor table; the build's and its programs' streaming signature name it (`+norm`);
  `d5005_afu --delta-norm` and `build-afu.sh --delta-norm` build it, and `--hybrid-program … --delta-norm` writes its
  programs.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::column::ColumnSet::{with_token_pairs, token_pairs, issue_rows, issues}`: a column
  array that issues its held beat against two token-rows a clock. Each core takes a token pair's two activation words
  from its channel's copy, which holds a pair a word, written a half at a time. Each word goes through processing
  elements and lanes of its own, while the beat's fields and the issue's flags are shared. A sum's place is a pair's,
  and a step issues ⌈tokens / 2⌉ times, so the ring, the core's control and the channel's feed serve twice the
  multiplies. Octet jobs keep a pair's sums a slot and emit a pair a clock. With `with_pe_pairs` the two words run on
  one set of the core's elements in clk2 (`logos_matvec_pass_x2t`), half the processing-element DSP blocks.
  The streaming unit queues a pair's stripe an entry and writes its token-rows one after the other, so folds and the
  greedy head take them as two entries. A set without token pairs builds byte for byte as before.
  `d5005_afu --token-pairs` and `build-afu.sh --token-pairs` build one (a hybrid engine of two or more rows); programs
  are unchanged.
- `spatial::decode_engine::rtl::engine::Streaming::with_conv_path` and `rtl::vector_side::{conv_path_verilog,
  CONV_LATENCY}`: the vector unit's side runs a convolution job on a path of its own (`logos_conv_path`). The side
  recognizes the machine's conv job by its descriptors: four taps, the ring rows whole lines. The kernel's taps are
  read once a job into block RAM. Each row's three ring rows are streamed from the memory and its x from the store,
  `lanes` channels a clock through `logos_conv_lane_p` (the four products, the adds from +0 in tap order, the SiLU, as
  the micro-program computes them). The output goes into its slot and x, in the state's format, into the ring's oldest
  row through the side's line queue. A sequence's next row waits for the card, and any other job runs as before.
  `d5005_afu --conv-path <lanes>` and `build-afu.sh --conv-path <lanes>` build it, the kernel as deep as the models'
  widest convolution.
- `spatial::decode_engine::physical::channel_mux_verilog`: `logos_channel_mux` takes each port's descriptors and its
  write lines through ordered queues of the port's own, one at a time whatever channel each is for, and gives a
  channel's write lines to the port whose next line is the channel's, so no two ports hold channels the other's next
  write waits on; every ready a port sees is a register's. The engine's ports reach it straight.
- `spatial::decode_engine::physical::read_credit_verilog`: `logos_read_credit`, a reader's lines of one channel through
  the channel mux asked only as its buffer of 512 lines can hold them, in runs of at most 32 lines, every line taken as
  it comes, the reader's next line in a register. A reader that takes a channel's lines only as its other channels let
  it (the streaming unit) reaches the mux through one a channel.
- `spatial::decode_engine::ofs::write_contexts_program` and `EngineProgram::contexts`: a hybrid program's sequences
  dealt over the contexts, the store laid for a context's rows. `AfuConfig::contexts`; the bring-up core's
  ENGINE_CONFIG, ENGINE_TABLE and STORE_READ take a context. `sw/logos_step.c` runs `LOGOS_D5005_CONTEXTS` contexts
  and refuses a program written for others.
- `spatial::decode_engine::machine::StoreLayout` lays slots no point of the step holds together over the same beats
  (`StoreLayout::live_together`, `Job::reads`, `Job::writes`), where every slot had beats of its own: the 27B's store
  at sixteen rows from 128,256 beats to 57,344. `StoreLayout::live_out` names the slots live out of each job, and
  `Machine::step_traced` drops a slot from its store once it is dead, as the engine's store gives its beats to another.
- `logos_column_feed_<set>` queues one decoded beat a core for two cores or more (two for one core), where it queued
  two a core at every count.
- `ofs::write_engine_program` and `write_hybrid_program` lay the program's token-row store for each of the machine's
  sequences (they laid it for one, so a program of several sequences wrote sequence 1's rows over the next slot).
- `ofs`'s simulation HAL (`sim/logos_hal_sim.h`) hands the host program every `+arg=` argument it is given (it kept
  sixteen), and its `sim_cmdline` reads the whole of `/proc/self/cmdline` for the DPI backend (it read 64 KB into 256
  arguments at most).
- `ofs`'s simulation memory (`sim/logos_avmm_memory_model.v`) returns a read burst's lines as the memory held them when
  the burst was accepted: a write accepted after a read no longer reaches the read, as on an Avalon-MM memory.
- `optimize::abstract_interp`: a loop's fixpoint is not converged while a list length it widens still changes, and a
  `While` condition's facts are recorded after the body as well as at loop entry (`OracleFacts::expr_int_range` of a
  length a loop grows no longer stops at the first passes' bound).
- `codegen_sva::caravel_project`: a project names its global router's antenna repair: the margin
  (`CaravelProject::macro_grt_antenna_margin_pct`, `CP_GRT_ANT_MARGIN`, `--grt-antenna-margin`) and the passes
  (`macro_grt_antenna_iters`, `CP_GRT_ANT_ITERS`, `--grt-antenna-iters`); its heuristic diode pass's threshold
  (`macro_antenna_threshold_um`, `CP_ANTENNA_THRESHOLD`, `--antenna-threshold`); and the sites a side legalization keeps
  free around every cell (`macro_dpl_padding_per_side`, `CP_DPL_PADDING`, `--dpl-padding-per-side`). The macro harden
  passes each one only when the project names it. At 18.5 ns the whole-program engine repairs to 20% in three passes,
  diodes nets past 700 um, and routes with a gcell adjustment of 0.05 and a met1 adjustment of 0.4. At the default 50%
  its route's first repair pass inserted 5,981 diodes and the reroute stopped at GRT-0232; at 20% it inserts 1,537. At
  300 um its heuristic pass put 57,637 diodes into the design, 4,844 of them in the multiplier patch, where the route
  detailed routing follows overflowed 248 gcells.
- `codegen_sva::flow_config`: `ClosureSettings::dpl_cell_padding_per_side` emits `DPL_CELL_PADDING`, OpenLane's total
  (twice the sites a side), only when chosen; `emit_flow_config --dpl-padding-per-side N` sets it. `emit_flow_config
  --antenna-threshold` refuses a span that is not a positive number of um, where it had built at the default 300.
- `codegen_sva::flow_config`: `ClosureSettings::setup_slack_margin_ns` emits `PL_RESIZER_SETUP_SLACK_MARGIN` and
  `GLB_RESIZER_SETUP_SLACK_MARGIN` only when chosen, for a block or a Caravel macro; `emit_flow_config
  --setup-slack-margin NS` sets it and refuses a value that is not a non-negative time. `CaravelProject::
  macro_setup_margin_ps` (`CP_SETUP_MARGIN`) passes a project's to the macro harden. The repairs judge setup on
  global-route estimates at the nominal RC corner, and signoff on the routed design at the max-RC corner: on the
  whole-program engine at 18.5 ns the repairs closed to +0.03 ns, and the routed design read -0.37 ns extracted at
  the nominal corner and -1.13 ns at max RC. The engine closes 1.25 ns past its estimates.
- `codegen::fast_div`: the loop counter's and the dividend's proofs read the lower bound alone
  (`OracleFacts::expr_proven_nonneg`), as the VM's `magic_div_const` does: a value `>= 0` with no upper bound, a counter
  a runtime `n` bounds, qualifies.
- Escape and ownership are judged on the program as written. `compile_program_full_with_proven`, `compile_to_rust_checked`, `compile_to_rust_verified` and `compile_to_rust_with_registry_full` each run `check_as_written` on the statements as parsed (the pre-elision copy where observation is elided), before division resolution and `optimize_program`.
  - The checked compile had run ownership after the optimizer. Its constant propagation folded a given-away name into its value, so a use after a possible `Give` of a constant compiled, and a possible move in a constant-true branch was reported as certain.
  - The plain compile and `largo`'s path accepted a zone escape inside a branch the optimizer proves dead.
  - Locked by `ownership_judges_the_program_as_written.rs`.
- `analysis::ownership` merges a `Repeat` like a `While`, since a loop may run no pass. It reads an `If`/`While` condition, a `Repeat`'s collection and an `Inspect`'s target. Both checkers read a `During` span's body in place, as observation elision splices it.
- The debugger's heap reference counts are the program's own (`vm::machine::count_handles`), not `Rc::strong_count`, which also counted the rewind history.
- `analysis::unify` names a type mismatch's sides the right way round: what was expected, then what was found (`type_mismatch_names_its_sides`).
- Checker findings (`check`, `ownership`, `escape`, `dimension_check`) carry the trail of statements they came from, so a finding inside a block or a function names the statement at fault.

- `vm::wasm::link` links with `--strip-debug`: a linked module no longer carries the runtime's DWARF, which described the whole runtime rather than the functions `--gc-sections` keeps (113 MB around eight functions; now 272 KB).
- AST C types a read of a name several blocks bind by `Let` by the values those locals agree on (`codegen_c::numbers`): an accumulator each loop binds afresh, read into a running real, made that real refused as holding an exact number, so the tiny Qwen forward no longer lowered to CUDA. `c_ast_vm_parity_lock`, `affine_equality_bounds_lock`.
- The C emitter's walk over a list of rationals, and its comparison of two lists, number their index `__walk{n}`: `__i{n}` was the name a 32-bit build predefines as `__i386` and `__i686`. `c_temp_names_lock`.
- The tree-walker materializes a launched task's arguments as the VM's spawn does (`interpreter::crossing_to_a_task`): each a copy, a value that cannot cross refused in the VM's words.
- **Exact numbers on every compiled backend.** A rational is the VM's exact number on the C walker, AST C, RISC-V, linked WebAssembly and the Rust tier:
  - `vm::numbers` joins a variable's integer and rational definitions over the bytecode's webs (`vm::variables`);
  - `codegen_c::numbers` and `codegen::rational` join them over the AST;
  - each backend's operators take the VM's rules for a whole value.

  `rational_number_lock`.
- `vm::tower::by_constant`: `DivPow2` and `MagicDivU` are the `/` or `%` by a constant the VM computes over any value. Each tower backend lowers them over a value of the exact extension, and the C walker and RISC-V over a joined rational.
- The C walker reads the number a popped value holds wherever a numeric operator takes it, and stops with the VM's sentence over `nothing`. `popped_value_lock`.
- Linked WebAssembly:
  - a big-integer operation takes two integers, so a shown product of reals is a real;
  - `AddAssign` takes `Add`'s kind rule;
  - spawned tasks convert their arguments as calls do;
  - a program holds the value runtime when any region does;
  - integer places narrow a rational (`logos_rt_rational_as_i64`) or a value of the exact extension (`logos_rt_value_integer_at`) in the VM's words for the place.
- `optimize::partial_eval::substitute_block` replaces every read of a substituted name or answers `None` — a read left inside a form the substitution carries through unchanged, or a name the block binds again (`mentions::stmt_touches`) — and reaches into a resolved supertask's `Splice`. Call-site specialization and loop unrolling fold nothing where it answers `None`.
- **The AST C backend lowers an exact quotient of integers as the fraction it is** (`codegen_c` types an `ExactDivide` of integers `Rational` and emits it through `codegen_expr_as_rational`; an `LInt` operand joins exactly through the runtime's new `ldec_from_lint`). It typed one `Int64` and emitted C's integer division, so inside other arithmetic or as a `Rational` argument it computed `0` or did not compile. A list literal bound to, or stored into, a `Seq of Rational` builds its items as fractions (`literal_as`) rather than the machine-integer list its first item typed it as.
- **`min`/`max` of reals decide equal zeros and NaN once.** The interpreter's builtins call `logicaffeine_base::numeric::float_min`/`float_max`; AOT-Rust emits `logicaffeine_data::logos_min_f64`/`logos_max_f64`; both C generators call the runtime's `logos_min_f64`/`logos_max_f64` where they emitted a ternary or `fmin`/`fmax`.
- **A divisibility test on an infinite integer keeps its remainder.** The optimizer reads `x % 2^k = 0` as `x & (2^k - 1) = 0`, sound for every machine integer; for `1 / dt` the remainder is a residue of the generator and `&` has no meaning, so `largo run` stopped with `` `&` requires integer, boolean, or Set operands `` where the tree-walker says the condition depends on the residue of `⌈1/dt⌉`. The rewrite is withheld from a dividend the exact extension reaches. `nonstandard_surface::a_condition_on_the_residue_waits_for_a_fact`, `optimize::fold::tower_guard_tests`.
- **Every seam that runs or emits a program resolves it first.** `compile_project` (`largo build`, `largo run`, `largo emit rust`), the Studio's interpreter (`interpret_streaming`) and Rust view (`generate_rust_code`), the asynchronous interpreter, the VM's IO runner (`run_vm_io_async`), `compile_to_rust_checked`, `compile_to_rust_verified`, the VM's compiled-native functions (`compile_function_to_native_rust`), `with_v2_optimized_program` and the first Futamura projection call `resolve_program` after parsing, as the tree-walker, the VM and both C generators do. On each of them `Let x: Rational be 7 / 2.` printed `3`, and a loop whose clock steps by an infinitesimal never ended.
- **The first projection's residual keeps what the program declared** (`projection1_source`): a `Let`'s declared type (`Let x: Rational be 7 / 2.`), a new value's type arguments (`a new Seq of Text`, `a new Map of Text to Int`, a map's key `to` its value as the language writes it) and the fields it is made with (`a new Point with x 3 and y 4`). One type decompiler, `decompile_type_expr`, serves every site.
- **A counter a peephole lowering declares keeps its Int type** (`codegen::peephole::try_block_peepholes`). Every lowering's counter binder is `let mut c: i64`, and a `Let` the lowering consumed registers its Int type, where the counter was left unregistered and the arithmetic on it emitted raw (the sieve's `i * i` past 2^63, then an out-of-bounds unchecked store). `loop_counter_exit_lock`.
- **A call's Int result is typed by its declaration** (`codegen::expr::declared_numeric_return`, read by `codegen::types::infer_numeric_type`). A call to a program's own `-> Int` function inferred as `unknown`, so `f(x) + big` skipped the exact path and emitted a raw `+`, which wraps in a release build. `bitwise_dual_lock`.
- **A call's argument to an Int parameter narrows into it** (`codegen::expr`, call arguments). An exact (tolerant) context reached into a call's arguments, so an `i64` parameter received a `LogosInt` (`E0308`). `bitwise_dual_lock`.
- **A counter read only through an item's index is not widened to `i128`** (`codegen::stmt::collect_count_accumulators`). The wide-accumulator read check collected names through `collect_expr_symbols`, which looks inside only names, lengths and arithmetic, so `item (v + 1) of arr` hid its read of `v`; once the affine closed form turned that item into `v`, an `i128` `v` met an `i64` total and the generated Rust did not build. The check now walks every expression to its leaves.
- **A counted loop's lowering writes back a counter its block does not declare** (`codegen::detection::value_read_after`). The range loop, the tiled nest, the copy and slice lowering, its continued run, the swap and the substring search each asked whether a later statement of the block mentioned a variable they leave changed; a variable the block does not `Let` belongs to an enclosing scope, read past the block, so it is now always written back. The tiled nest wrote its outer counter back as a new `let`, which left the reused counter unchanged, and as its bound even when it ran no pass; it now assigns `max(0, bound)`. `try_emit_seq_copy_pattern` is gone: `try_emit_seq_from_slice_pattern`, tried first at each of its call sites, takes every loop it took except a sequence copied into itself, for which it emitted `let dst = dst.deep_clone();`.
- **The C backend's counted range stops at its end.** `for (t = lo; t <= hi; t++)` never ended at `hi == INT64_MAX` and let a body's `Set t` move the count; a hidden count now stops at `hi` and binds `t` each pass, and a range used as a value stops at its end too. AOT-Rust binds a `Repeat` variable the body sets as `mut`, where the generated Rust did not build.

- Machine words on bytecode C mean what they mean on the VM. A word sum assigned in place (`Set w to w + word32(1)`, a loop over words) wraps in its ring; a word's remainder is unsigned; `word8(n)` of a big integer takes its low bits (`lc_xword_bits`); a word keys a map and fills a set (`Kind::SetWord`, rendered unsigned by `set_i64_to_string_as`); a word pads and centers under an interpolation width; words of two widths or a word beside a number are refused in the VM's words when ordered, and `"w=" + w` joins as text instead of being taken for a refused pairing, which had left every statement after it unreachable (`refused_for_kinds` now asks `semantics::arith` for any word pairing). On AST C, `"w=" + w` with a word is a text join, not pointer arithmetic. `c_word_lock`.

- A remainder is no longer taken as non-negative. `expr_is_nonnegative` and the per-body non-negativity fixpoint counted `x % 8` as `>= 0` whatever `x`, but the remainder keeps the dividend's sign (`-7 % 8` is `-7`); the power-of-two strength reduction then lowered `r % 4` and `r / 2` of such a remainder to `r & 3` and `r >> 1`, wrong for a negative `r` on AOT-Rust and both C backends. The fixpoint now asks the one rule. `strength_reduce_pow2_lock`.

- Bit operations on an Int that may be a big integer mean the reference's on AOT-Rust: `&` of a non-negative big integer and a low mask takes its low bits and every other big operand of `&`, `|`, `^`, `<<` or `>>` is refused in `semantics::arith`'s words (`logos_band_exact`, `logos_word_operand`); the generated Rust no longer fails to build on `LogosInt & i64` (`((a shifted left by 30) + a) & 4294967295`). `exact_bitwise_lock`, `mask_narrow_lock`.

- The RISC-V runtime collects its garbage. `codegen_riscv::RISCV_RUNTIME_C` embeds `codegen_c::runtime::C_GC_COLLECTOR` and the `lc_gc_*` interface (one source each, through the `lc_gc_collector_source!` and `lc_gc_api_source!` macros, so the C runtime's text is unchanged), allocates every block through `lc_gc_*` (text, digits and counts as atomic blocks the collector never scans), maps the scheduler's `logos_alloc`/`logos_release` to it and hands it every task stack (`LOGOS_STACK_ENTER`/`LOGOS_STACK_LEAVE`), and keeps a sequence's first cells inline after its header until it outgrows them (`logos_seq_new_cap`, `logos_seq_room`).

- The C collector's blocks come from 4 MiB mappings cut to a 64 KiB boundary (`lc_gc_block_take`/`lc_gc_block_give`; an emptied block waits among 64 spares, then goes back to the system) instead of one `posix_memalign` each, and its marking stack is reserved at `lc_gc_start`; `c_header_names.txt` holds the names `<sys/mman.h>` declares.

- A `## To native` declaration reaches its implementation on every engine, or the call is refused by name; no engine runs the empty body and answers `nothing`. env.lg's `get(name)` reads the environment on the tree-walker, the VM, the JIT, both C backends, WebAssembly (the Node host reads `process.env`; a page has none) and RISC-V, as it did on AOT-Rust, so `LOGOS_SESSION_SECRET` signs the sessions of a backend `largo serve` interprets. time.lg's `now()` is the Unix time in milliseconds, an `Int`, on every engine (time.lg declares `now () -> Int` and `sleep (ms: Int)`, and a native's integer answer takes the type its declaration names on AOT-Rust, so `now()` compares with a literal of any size there), and `sleep(ms)` waits as `Sleep ms.` does on the tree-walker and the VM. A native no engine implements is refused in the Rust backends' own words (`Unknown system native function: …`), and a Rust function (`is native "crate::path"`) is refused, as `Escape to Rust:` is, wherever Rust does not link it. The tree-walker and the VM read one table of system natives (`semantics::builtins::system_native`); the VM expands `get` into `EnvHas`, `EnvValue` and an ordinary `Option`, and the WebAssembly host ABI gains `env_len`, `env_into` and `time_now` (the module sizes and allocates the buffer the host writes a variable's text into). The FIPS-202 hashes a program declares native in place of crypto.lg's pure-LOGOS sponge (`sha3_256`, `sha3_512`, `shake128`, `shake256`) are refused by the Rust function they name wherever Rust does not link them (`semantics::builtins::rust_only_native`, the table the Rust backends read).

- A page timer in the browser lands once its time has passed. When the page's wake runs, the browser has run its clock at least the wake's delay, but Chrome coarsens `performance.now()` with jitter and can read a tenth of a millisecond short; the runtime counted whole milliseconds off that reading, so a timer could fall a millisecond short by the page's count and wait for another wake. A wake now passes the page at least the time to the timer it woke for, including a wake `main` asked for before the clock started.

- WASM preserves exact integers in sequences and maps with owned storage, value-based map keys, and copy-on-write extraction. Mutable global loans track absence independently of zero and empty payloads.

- `codegen_c` reads the integers of `SeqI64`, `SetI64`, `MapI64I64`, `MapStrI64` and `MapI64Str` through `Ints` (`Int`, or `Word(bits)`): an element read back, a loop variable and a map entry take the collection's reading, and word collections render through the runtime's `*_to_string_as`. `set_element` types a loop over a set, which walks its insertion `order`.

- `codegen_c` gives machine words their own `CType::Word(bits)`: ring arithmetic masked at the width, unsigned division, comparison and display, wrapped shifts, `intOfWordN` reading the bits signed or unsigned as the VM does, and the VM's refusals for a word beside an `Int` or another width. `map_ctype` is the one table every map annotation, field and construction reads, and holds a word as the `int64_t` of its bits; `min`/`max` over `LInt` lower to exact comparisons, a `%` by an `Int64` divisor narrows to `int64_t`, and the descending counted-loop fast path takes the counter's one unit step anywhere in the body, its guard bounding the offsets read on each side of the step.

- `vm::compiler` compiles an equality against `none` as one against the `Nothing` constant (R2: absence is one value), which every bytecode backend already tests.

- `vm::wasm` batch roots track each register's shadow, so a call or backedge syncs only registers changed since the last sync and only in functions that may reach a safepoint (transitively, by `may_safepoint`); copy-on-write collection operations count as writes. Exact `+ − ×`, tagging, release, narrowing and cloning outside a hot loop call shared `RuntimeFn` helpers, and `ExactAddW`/`SubW`/`MulW` take a machine-word operand and tag it themselves; the inline sequences remain in loops. Variant display reads its payload through the enum layout accessors.

- `codegen_cuda::device_lift` closes reachability over macro bodies (`cshake::macro_references`, which reads a directive past the comments documenting it), so a function named only inside a `#define` is lifted, and `route_alloc_to_shims` steps by character.

- A served program keeps serving when a request fails. The generated failure hook ends the program only when no runtime boundary catches the failure (`logicaffeine_system::failure::is_caught`); a program that serves opens with the unwind header, so its release build takes `panic = "unwind"`; and `codegen::release_panic_strategy` decides the release profile for both `write_cargo_project` and `largo build`.
- The native partial evaluator's cdylib entry `peSpecializeOnce` is `exported for native`, so it keeps the by-value signature its FFI shim calls whatever the emitter lends an internal function; `compile::native_pe_cdylib_lib_rs` returns the library's source.
- Native integer power retains its exact integer return type during static checking; real operands and program-defined power functions retain their own types.
- Optimized VM compilation preserves dynamic return values when static analysis cannot supply a loop-versioning proof; typed bytecode-C compilation retains its static diagnostics.
- Generated C traces only allocated storage, releases completed task frames after their final use, and removes retired scheduler references. Record integer fields preserve exact values across writes, copies and calls.
- Native loop call exits restore the suspended callee frames and borrowed list identities before resuming bytecode, preserving exact overflow results and runtime errors.
- WASM explicit collection copies retain their key and value layouts through subsequent reads and independent mutations.
- WASM kind inference ignores only absence writes proven unread before replacement, including implicit mutable-parameter results at exits. Ownership clears retain the local’s physical type. C exact floor division releases superseded quotient and remainder allocations.
- VM compilation runs the iterative AST depth gate before optimizer analyses, including for callers that supply ASTs directly. The 100,000-level expression regression returns its depth refusal safely.
- WebAssembly stores exact integer record fields in collector-owned immutable cells. Record copies retain their cells, nested records preserve value semantics through copy-on-write, and direct and formatted display render exact field values.
- `optimize::abstract_interp`: after a `While`, the transform walk forgets what the loop writes and narrows by the negated condition only when no `Break` of its own can end the loop; the counter pin from the pre-loop state is gone. The fact walk's three exits (converged, widened, past the iteration cap) skip the negated condition for such a loop, and its converged exit forgets every name the body writes (`RichAbstractState::forget_written`: interval, kind, shape, aliases, length and every affine definition naming or mentioning it), so a value written before the `Break` survives the loop; a counted build's length and the entry-guard exit bound apply only to an exit through the condition. `loop_shape::extract_counted_while` and `extract_counted_repeat` refuse a loop a `Break` of its own can end (`loop_shape::leaves_by_break`, over the exhaustive AST walk, which the oracle asks too), so the Rust for-range lowering, the unroller, loop interchange, integer induction strength reduction and the accumulator bound never treat it as running a fixed number of times. Every benchmark's generated C, bytecode C, Rust and VM bytecode is byte-identical before and after.
- Monomorphization preserves every expression-scoped optimizer fact on the equivalent arena node it rebuilds. The fact registry classifies new fact kinds exhaustively, so a backend cannot silently lose a new proof when generics are lowered.
- Linked WebAssembly programs retain immutable runtime archives across later Cargo cleanup and rebuilds. Checkout-specific native build locks cover runtime compilation and snapshot creation; each link reclaims its temporary inputs on success or failure, and failed builds remain retryable.
- Deterministic native replay lets other tasks finish after a task fails, including generated release builds, and flushes their output before reporting the failed run. Ordinary native programs retain their existing panic profile. [Regression: replay_failure_profile_lock, e2e_deterministic_replay]
- WASM links own separate intermediate directories through output collection and release them on success or failure, so simultaneous callers cannot overwrite one another.
- The VM releases dead unnamed temporaries before record-field mutations and function calls, retaining live arguments and named aliases. Record-field copy counters expose copies and elements copied, so expired reads cannot cause quadratic copying.
- Linked WASM runtime builds select roots and dependencies from the successful Cargo artifact report, including primary library outputs, rather than choosing archives by modification time or directory contents.
- The generated Rust shows through `logicaffeine_system::io::println` (a formatted line as `format_args!`, so no `String` is built), flushes `logicaffeine_system::io::flush_stdout` in the failure hook, before an `Escape to Rust:` block and in the `readWireProgram`/`writeWireResidual` builtins.
- Register promotion refuses a loop whose collection binding can be reassigned, rebound or swapped, preserving the selected array element and saved copies. `jit_scalar_swap_edges_lock` pins the optimized-VM result.
- `web_host::FMT_F64_JS` is the WebAssembly hosts' float display, interpolated into `wasm_host_shim` and carried verbatim by `crates/logicaffeine_compile/assets/web/runtime.js`: `fmt_f64`'s output from ECMAScript's shortest digits, with Rust's tie-up rule over ECMAScript's tie-to-even.
- `codegen_parallel_stmt` lowers a `Simultaneously:` block of any width to one balanced `rayon::join` tree whose result pattern mirrors the tasks: a `Let` binds its value (`mut` when reassigned), any other task `_`, a type-silent literal its `i64`.
- **An `Int` that outgrows a machine word is exact on WebAssembly.** Where the shared decision (`vm::exactness`) says an integer can outgrow i64, the module holds it as a tagged word: the integer itself while it fits beside the tag, a handle to an integer the host holds when it does not (`vm::wasm::module::exact`). Two small words compute inline and checked, with a constant operand tagged at compile time and its check folded to one bound; a held integer goes through the host (the `exact_*` imports, computed by the reference tier's own arithmetic in each host, and by `web_host::EXACT_INT_JS` in both JavaScript hosts). A parameter, result, global or closure boundary is tagged where an exact value crosses it, decided to a fixpoint over the program. A held integer is owned: cloned on copy, given back on overwrite and on every way out of a function. An op the module lowers only on machine words reads an exact register narrowed, failing with the reference tier's sentence for an index past a word (`FailCode::{ListIndexTooWide, TextIndexTooWide, ListStoreIndexTooWide}`) and with `FailCode::IntTooWide` anywhere else. A program none of whose integers can outgrow a word compiles to the module it compiled to before (`exact_int_every_tier_lock`).
- **The exactness decision follows a value through a global and through a swap.** A global's read is any store to it in the region, so a product kept in a program binding a function reads is exact; each side of `Swap a and b` is the other's value, so a variable that takes an exact integer is exact (`vm::exactness`, which bytecode C and WebAssembly share).
- **The range oracle no longer orders two unbounded ends of one sign.** `optimize::abstract_interp`'s `definitely_*` comparisons treated two intervals reaching +∞ (or −∞) as ordered at that end (`Bound::decided`), so a comparison between two exact integers past the word folded to a constant and AST C printed the wrong branch.
- **A linked WebAssembly build's scratch directories belong to their checkout** (`vm::wasm::link`): two checkouts building into one found each other's newest rlibs and linked them as their own.
- A `Let` whose initializer is integer literals declares `: i64`, and a range or counted loop whose bounds are integer literals spells its end bound `_i64`, so rustc never defaults a LOGOS `Int` to `i32` (`codegen::types::type_silent_int`, read by `codegen_let_stmt`, `Expr::Range` and the counted-loop lowering).
- Preserve statement operands across later mutable calls, capture nested mutation keys once in root-to-leaf order, and invalidate propagated constants after calls in bindings, assignments, conditions and iterables. `statement_snapshot_lock` covers source programs and direct VM statement inputs.
- Mutable calls capture each collection key once before the callee frame opens, lend only the selected value, and write back through the original path. Packed lists can hold an empty lent slot; in-out parameters preserve local aliases through copy-on-write. Binary ISA version 2 serializes ownership proofs separately from returned argument slots. Locked by `in_out_storage_lock` and `vm_in_out_wire_lock`. Native calls transfer final mutable argument values and materialize recursive deoptimization frames in the original bytecode argument windows.
- Preserve evaluated VM operands across later function calls, including collection and slice operands, map keys, and named or expression-valued callees; pure expressions continue to reuse local registers.
- **A function that changes a program variable is pure on no path.** `optimize::purity::Purity` is the one definition: a body is admitted by name (its own bindings, program variables nothing changes after binding them, the program's pure functions and pure builtins), as the greatest fixpoint over the call graph. Memoization (`memoize_run`), compile-time evaluation (`supercompile`), the reflection-symmetry rewrite and the Rust tier's `collect_pure_functions` all ask it, so a recursion that counts its calls in a program variable, or calls a function that does, runs every call on every tier (`purity_lock`). The symmetry rewrite also reads the program bindings a callee writes, so a search whose callee writes one is never halved.
- **A `mutable` parameter is the caller's variable on both C tiers.** AST C passes the variable by pointer (`T *name`, read and written as `(*name)`; a lifted closure calls an in-out function the same way), and a text a `mutable` parameter lends is a plain string on both sides of the call (`CContext::plain_texts`), never a builder one side appends to in place. Bytecode C passes the argument register by pointer and the callee stores each `mutable` parameter back before every return (`CompiledFunction::mutable_param_regs`), as the VM leaves it. An in-out `Int` its body makes exact is exact on both sides: `exact::ExactSlots::lent_exact` on AST C, a `LentBack` flow and the parameter's returned kind on bytecode C. `in_out_lock`, `in_out_unrolling_lock` (both C tiers).
- **A `mutable` parameter is the caller's variable on WebAssembly.** A function returns each in-out parameter's final value after its own result (wasm multi-value) and the caller stores it into the argument it lent, where the module passed it by value before. The bytecode peephole's liveness reads a function's `mutable` parameters at every exit, so the last write to one is never deleted as dead (it was on every tier that runs the peephole), and `vm::instruction::visit_regs` counts a call's in-out arguments as read and written (`in_out_lock`).
- **A lent element or field is stored back.** `optimize::sequence::lower_lent_places` lowers `bump(item 2 of xs)` and `bump(p's y)` to a binding the call lends and a store through the same keys after it, each key and each earlier argument evaluated once, in order; the Rust tier and AST C run it.
- **AST C evaluates operands in the order LOGOS does.** `optimize::sequence::sequence_operands` binds the operands of an expression in which one operand writes a binding a sibling reads (a program binding a callee writes, or a variable a call lends to a `mutable` parameter) to fresh names ahead of the statement, in order; `Push`'s value, an `If`'s condition and a `Repeat`'s iterable included (`c_operand_order_lock`).
- **Equality saturation keeps a variable a call lends.** A name a statement's calls write through a `mutable` parameter is a fresh opaque leaf at each of its reads in that statement and a new version after it, so `saturate` no longer folds it to an earlier value.
- **A record or variant stores a variable by a move only at its last use** (`codegen::flow`, from liveness per statement), and copies it otherwise: a list read again after it was stored builds (it was E0382) and a record nothing reads again is no longer copied (`construction_move_lock`).
- **A capacity hint changes nothing but the allocation**: every analysis that decides a sequence's emitted form reaches the same decision with and without `with capacity` (`with_capacity_transparency_lock`). A `Seq` parameter a function writes by item and copies by a push loop indexes its `&mut [T]` slice directly (`mut_borrowed_seq_copy_lock`), and a text accumulator appends a one-character text narrowed to a byte as a character (`string_accum_char_append_lock`).
- **`Swap a and b.` exchanges two variables on every tier**, whatever they hold: `optimize::swap_classes` ties swapped names to one representation, so a fixed list swapped with one of its length stays fixed and anything else falls back together (`swap_every_tier_lock`). `optimize::mentions` gives the analyses every name a statement touches from the one effect walk (`walker_soundness_lock`).
- Apply scalar copying to Word32/Word64 and copy-on-write cloning to their sequences and record fields, including the linked emitter fallback, so writes preserve saved copies and caller values.
- Retain mutable parameters during specialization and refresh lending metadata for generated callees before subsequent constant propagation.
- Keep functions with mutable parameters out of value-only leaf inlining so caller writes survive optimization. Ordinary value-parameter leaves remain eligible.
- AST arenas release their owned allocations after parsing, interpretation and code generation. Division resolution and observation elision borrow temporary statement lists independently of the arena storage they return, so cleanup requires no extra AST copies.
- The loop dependence proof keeps the written order when inner row accesses can report different bounds errors. The facts scope is neither Send nor Sync, so its guard cannot leave a thread-local stack open by moving to another thread. `statement_tier_totality_lock` and `optimize::facts::tests` lock both invariants.
- RISC-V equality carries both value layouts when their kinds differ, compares Int/Float and Rational/Float elements exactly, preserves Rational, Date and Moment sequence element kinds, and limits pointer-identity shortcuts to the collections that use them on the VM. Aliased records, tuples and enums still compare every payload. Runtime whole rationals compare equal to integer sequence elements; rational ordering uses 128-bit cross-products so large numerators do not wrap or allocate temporary rationals. `riscv_equality_lock` covers NaN, numeric precision, temporal elements, empty collections and nested layouts.
- RISC-V retains separate shapes for absent Option and Result constructors, so copying or comparing an untyped `none` or one-sided Result does not require an imaginary payload layout. Present payloads retain their own numeric kinds. Peer sequence elements compare by topic and remain distinct from Text. `riscv_equality_lock`.
- **The C runtime's `lbig_to_f64` rounds as `BigInt::to_f64` does.** It was Horner over base-10^9 limbs and missed the nearest float for 39% of random 64–200-bit integers, so `BigInt + Real` printed differently on C than on every other tier; it re-bases the magnitude to base 2^32 exactly and rounds its top 64 bits with a sticky bit once. `c_bigint_to_f64_lock`.
- **ML-KEM and ML-DSA run on the tree-walker and the VM.** A `## To native` kernel in `crypto.lg` (ML-KEM NTT, sampling and serialization; ML-KEM/ML-DSA keypair, encaps, decaps, sign, verify) was linked only by the compiled tier; v0.10.1's interpreters ran its empty body and answered `Nothing` while the built binary was correct. `semantics::native_kernels::NativeKernel` is now the one table the tree-walker, the VM (`CallBuiltin` with append-only kernel codes that travel in bytecode) and AOT-Rust read, and C-from-bytecode, RISC-V, WebAssembly and the soft core refuse a reachable kernel call by name. `native_kernel_lock`, `native_kernel_reach_lock`.
- **file.lg `read`/`write` and the file statements run on the VM and the tree-walker alike.** `read`/`write` ran their empty native bodies and answered `nothing` on both engines (`BuiltinId::FileRead`/`FileWrite`); `Write … to file`, `Mount … at` and `Read … from file` failed on the VM with "VFS not initialized" and now run against the tree-walker's sandboxed `MemVfs`, content evaluated before path (`BuiltinId::{VfsReadFile, VfsWriteFile, VfsMount}`). `randomInt(lo, hi)` refuses `lo > hi`. `phase_vm_error_parity`, `file_io_cross_tier`.
- **A launched task's failure fails the run on the tree-walker, as on the VM.** Only the main task had an error sink, so a launched task's error vanished; the run's one sink is shared with every task it launches (`Interpreter::install_yield_state`), the first failure is kept, and the other tasks run to quiescence. `launched_task_failure_lock`.
- **Two launches that pass the same pipe build on AOT-Rust.** A launch's arguments were evaluated inside the spawned `async move` block, so the first launch moved the pipe's sender; they are now evaluated where the launch stands, in the interpreter's order, and moved into the task.
- **`largo build` and `largo emit` honor `## No <opt>`.** `compile_to_rust_with_registry_full`, behind the `compile_project` path, used a bare `OptimizationConfig::from_env()` and dropped a file's decorators, and the C, VM, WebAssembly and ISA seams read the all-on default (`## No fastdiv` still produced `MAGICDIVU`); every tier now goes through `optimize::install_decorators`. `decorator_through_largo`.
- **The optimizer no longer keeps a value a later write made stale.** CSE reused a pre-loop `item p of xs` in a loop that advances `p`, and ignored a `Set` inside an `Inspect` arm, a `Swap`, a push through a field and a call that sets a global; LICM hoisted past `Swap` and global-writing calls and hoisted readers of an iteration-local `Let`; BTA and the e-graph kept pre-call values of a global the callee writes; constant propagation and the supercompiler kept a name's first value after a later `Let` rebound it, ignored a `Set` in a match arm and a `Pop … into`, treated a binding lent to a `mutable` parameter as never written, and folded `Let counter be 0. bump(). Show counter.` to `Show 0`; the Oracle folded a collection length a loop grows as empty and deleted the live `Push`. One exhaustive write-effects model now serves CSE/GVN, LICM, loop splitting, bound versioning, deforestation, BTA, SCCP, the e-graph, abstract interpretation, constant propagation and the supercompiler. `optimizer_write_effects_lock`, `cse_loop_soundness_lock`, `optimizer_soundness_lock`, `oracle_loop_length_soundness`, `supercompile_store_lock`, `bootstrap_scope_lock`.
- **The optimizer reads the names the source reads.** An inlined callee's free names were resolved against the call site's frame (`## To peek -> Int: Return x.` inlined into a function with its own `Let x be 7` answered 7), a block spliced by LICM's peel and unswitch could capture the names it landed in front of, and constant propagation kept a constant past a statement that rebinds the name; `inline_tiny`, `inline_leaf`, `licm` and `propagate` now ask one name-capture model, and `inline_leaf` leaves a helper that writes a program binding as a call. `dce` keeps a known branch that declares names as `If true:`, so `If true: Let inner be 5.` no longer leaks `inner` into `## Main`. `optimizer_name_capture_lock`.
- **Smaller optimizer fixes.** `bound_version` hoists a versioned loop's `Let i` above the guard instead of cloning it into one branch (`'i' undeclared` under gcc and nvcc, `c_bound_version_scope_lock`); `fold` and `propagate` no longer drop the `"0"` of `s + 0` on a Text; affine scalarization keeps an array read from the end (`item -1 of arr`) a runtime `Seq`.
- **Generic programs build on C, AOT-Rust and WebAssembly.** C lowered every type parameter to `int64_t`, AOT-Rust emitted generic Rust that did not build, and the WebAssembly backend gave every type parameter an unknown value kind; C and WebAssembly now compile through `monomorphize`, and AOT-Rust keeps Rust generics with the instantiations and bounds a body needs, while partial evaluation never folds a constant into a type-parameter-typed parameter. `generics_all_tiers`, `aot_specialized_recursion_lock`.
- **Programs that ran on the tree-walker build on AOT-Rust.** `For each c in <Text>`; the `contains(h, n)` call; `format(x)`; `readBytes`; a field read that moved out of its record; records named `Box` or `String`; a record field named with a Rust keyword; a tuple of records; a terminal `Inspect` over a call (E0597); Text concatenation of fields, parameters and Text-returning calls; shifts and bit operations on a promoted Int; `sin(x) * 100`; an `Int` into a `Real` parameter; `Show x to f` against a parameter that owns its value; `length(x)` and `copy(x)`; a call statement's arguments, lowered as a call expression's are; `Send m to peer` to a remote peer; a sequence literal that repeats a record; dates moved by calendar spans; a value past 64 bits built inside any statement kind; exact values keeping their type through a binding and printing exactly under a format spec; counters compared by their totals; a `mutable` parameter of any type. A `Merge` folds every field, where plain Int and Text fields were skipped. `aot_text_walk_lock`, `format_builtin_all_tiers`, `aot_record_field_move_lock`, `aot_prelude_name_collision_lock`, `aot_show_lends_lock`, `aot_exact_value_format_lock`, `aot_list_literal_copies_lock`, `keyword_field_names_all_tiers`, `tuple_of_records_all_tiers`, `counter_comparison_all_tiers`, `date_span_all_tiers`, `bignum_reaches_every_statement_lock`, `mvs_record_param_lock`.
- **A function that reads or writes a `## Main` binding builds on AOT-Rust and C.** The bindings functions reach become fields of one record lent to each such function as a `mutable` parameter on AOT-Rust (`program_bindings::{lift_program_bindings, Lifted, PROGRAM_RECORD, PROGRAM_BINDING}`, on every Rust entry), and file-scope statics assigned by their root `Let` on C, with `## Main` lowered first. A call that can reach such a binding before its `Let`, a task launched on a function that reaches one, and an expression that reads one beside a call that writes it (C leaves the operand order open) are refused by name. `aot_module_globals_lock`, `aot_program_record_lock`.
- **Format specs format every kind as the interpreter does** on the tree-walker, the VM, AOT-Rust and C: `{x:$}` gives an Int or a Real two decimals and a big integer its exact digits, and precision, alignment and width apply to any value. The WebAssembly backend lowers precision, alignment and width and still refuses the `$` currency spec. `format_spec_all_tiers`.
- **C computes what v0.10.1's C tier got wrong.**
  - A binding means what it means on the interpreter: a binding that re-types a name in its own block, or shadows one bound outside it, gets a fresh C name (`__logos_b<n>_<name>`), a parameter or program static keeps its spelling, and closing a block restores each name's outer type; a counted `Repeat` reads its bounds once, and the loop variable and the body are blocks of their own (`c_binding_scope_lock`).
  - A `Repeat` over a map walks its insertion order, and one loop name binds the whole entry as a tuple.
  - A date, moment, time, duration or span literal computes and prints, where it lowered to `0` (`c_temporal_lock`, `date_span_all_tiers`).
  - `item 2 of split(s)` shows the text, not an address (`codegen_c_seq_text_index`).
  - `Show x to f` hands `x` to `f` instead of printing it (`show_to_recipient_all_tiers`).
  - A name the C library's headers declare (`rename`, `send`, `read`, `index`, `time`, …, including the `<setjmp.h>` names) is mangled by `escape_c_ident` from `crates/logicaffeine_compile/src/codegen_c/c_header_names.txt` (regenerated with `C_HEADER_NAMES_RECORD=1`) instead of colliding at compile time (`c_header_names_lock`).
- **Nested writes land where the sentence says, and a record copy owns its lists.** `Set a's b's n to …`, `Push v to item i of grid`, `Push v to a's b's c`, `Pop … from <place>` and `Set item k of (<place>) to …` failed on the tree-walker ("Push to nested field access not supported", "Pop collection must be an identifier"); `Set item I of R's F to V` silently changed a clone on the VM; a push to a list field of a copied record reached the other copy. Each write now goes through the place, copying only the shared collections on the path (`Compiler::compile_place_mutation` on the VM); a `mutable` argument naming a place is lent and given back, and a `mutable` parameter is in-out on the VM. `correctness_place_lvalues`, `vm_place_mutation_lock`, `wasm_place_mutation_lock`, `mvs_record_param_lock`.
- **Long programs fit the VM's register frame.** Straight-line code leaked two or three scratch registers per statement and exhausted the 16,384-register frame at a few thousand statements; scratch registers are reclaimed at every statement boundary. `vm_register_recycling_lock`.
- **The VM's strength-reduced division takes a big-integer dividend.** `Op::MagicDivU` panicked ("MagicDivU on a non-Int operand") on a dividend near 10^20; it now falls back to exact `Div`/`Mod`. `bignum_strength_reduction_lock`.
- **A function whose only use of an outer name is `Set x to …` reaches the program's binding on the VM**, where it failed "Undefined variable: x". `vm_global_promotion_lock`.
- **The tree-walker recurses as deep as the VM and resolves a definition written below `## Main`.** It ran on a thread that overflowed near 50 calls; it now runs on a 256 MiB reserved session stack (`interpreter::{on_session_stack, SESSION_STACK_BYTES}`) and reaches `MAX_CALL_DEPTH`, and every `## To`, type and component is registered before the first statement runs. `treewalker_recursion_headroom_lock`.
- **A variant's named fields bind by declaration however the `with` clause orders them**, keyed by enum and variant, on the tree-walker and the VM; an omitted field is `nothing`. `variant_resolution_lock`.
- **The async interpreter path applies the implicit `## Main`, the stdlib prelude and `Alias::fn` resolution**, so a program that sleeps or opens a socket calls prelude functions as the synchronous path does; `Sleep` with no tokio reactor sleeps on the thread. `stdlib_prelude_invisible`.
