- **Wire decoding is bounded.** Every decompressor stops at 256 MiB (`MAX_DECODE_BYTES`; lz4's claimed size, a bounded zstd read), and an untrusted field count no longer preallocates beyond the remaining input. `never_panic_lock`, `oom_injection`.
- **A module outside the project is never read.** The loader read an import before checking its path against the project root, and skipped the check when the path could not be canonicalized; it now canonicalizes first and checks against the project root, the `Largo.toml` root and the declared dependency roots before reading. `multimodule_edgecases`.
- **The WebAssembly backend runs record and collection programs it refused or miscompiled.** A function with more than 64 basic blocks trapped at run time: block indices were written as signed-LEB128 `i32.const` immediates with the unsigned encoder, and `cfg::set_next_block`/`goto_block` are now the one spelling of "go to block k" (`wasm_large_function_lock`). Every value shape displays as the VM displays it; kinds come from `CompiledProgram::declared_types`; records and maps held in module bindings, records read from lists, maps of records, nested collections, `Result` payload records and Shared-struct counters lower; record slots follow the declaration; `Set` and `Map` keep insertion order through a removal; a `Return` inside a loop unwinds only its own function's loops; `For each c in <Text>` walks UTF-8; a function nothing reaches is a trap stub instead of a refusal; a refusal names its function (`WasmLowerError::UnsupportedOwned`). `wasm_display_lock`, `wasm_declared_kind_lock`, `wasm_map_global_lock`, `wasm_record_global_lock`, `wasm_record_list_lock`, `wasm_result_payload_lock`, `wasm_collection_order_lock`, `wasm_module_validity_lock`.
- **`vm::ChanElem::from_type_name` reads `Real` as well as `Float`**, so a `Pipe of Real` is a float channel on the VM.
- **Installed `largo build` works outside a monorepo checkout.** `compile_to_dir` and `write_cargo_project` staged the runtime crates from the monorepo's workspace root (`copy_runtime_crates`) and failed without one. `RuntimeCrateSource::{detect, data_dep, system_dep, linux_engine_section, is_workspace}` decides once: without a workspace root the generated `Cargo.toml` depends on the `logicaffeine-*` runtime crates from crates.io, pinned `=` to this compiler's version, and the source staging is skipped; inside a checkout the staged `./crates` path dependencies are unchanged. Every generated manifest, `largo build`'s included, ends with `linux_engine_section`, so on Linux the runtime carries `io-uring` and a compiled server has the io_uring engine.
- **The AOT-native hot tier hands a failure back to the VM.** An exported shim catches a failure, sets `logos_native_<fn>__failed` and returns a placeholder; the VM re-runs the call on bytecode and raises the exact error. A library without that companion is not loaded, and only replayable functions (`EffectEnv::function_is_replayable`) are exported. `aot_native_tier`.
- **The emitter is deterministic and does not overflow its stack.** `begin_program_emission` resets the per-program counters, so the same source emits identical Rust twice in one process (`emitter_statelessness_lock`); the wide `codegen_stmt` arms each run in their own `#[inline(never)]` frame, so deep nesting compiles on an ordinary thread (`compiler_stack_safety_lock`).
- **`extraction` emits compilable Rust for kernel-minted names and non-dependent matches.** Binder names such as `#fld0`, `✧` and `#eq` are renamed collision-free; a non-dependent match takes its inductive from the discriminant's binder, where it emitted `match n { }` or another type's arms; the prelude's `Int` arithmetic extracts as Rust operators; a user definition sharing a prelude name is kept unless type and body match. `phase84_extraction_e2e`.
- `codegen_c` keeps an exact integer exact in every slot it reaches. `codegen_c::exact::exact_slots` decides, once per program and to a fixpoint, which `Int` returns, parameters, struct fields and variant fields hold the tagged `LInt`; `codegen_function` and the closure and collector contexts declare each body's `LInt` locals from the same analysis, and a call promotes a machine word passed to an `LInt` parameter. `types::seq_fn` names every sequence family's helpers from its C type, so an empty `Seq` of a user enum is built by `seq_<Enum>_new` and `Set item … of` reaches a sequence of structs or a field's sequence. `json.lg` compiles and runs on C.
- `optimize::partial_eval` classifies a call's arguments in the caller's scope. The specializer read them against the callee's parameter bindings, so a caller variable spelled like a callee parameter that the same call passes a literal was specialized to that literal (`randBeta`'s second gamma draw ran at shape `1.0`).
- **An index names the same item on every tier, and so does every slice and every loop the optimizer copies in one step.** One rule, `logicaffeine_data::logos_offset`: 1 is the first item, a negative index counts from the back, and 0 or anything past either end names nothing. AOT-Rust's fast paths for copy, fill, drain, reverse and swap loops, fixed-size arrays and tiled nests indexed around it, so an index the interpreter refused could read another item, stop with Rust's own message, or leave a loop counter where the loop never leaves it. They now go through the rule, and an index the compiler proves names an item still compiles to an unchecked or plain indexed access. `statement_tier_totality_lock`.
- **A failure reads the same on every tier.** `logicaffeine_data::failure` holds the sentence for every runtime failure the engines report (division and modulo by zero, an index or key that names nothing, quantities, money, modular arithmetic, UUIDs, timestamps, time zones, `decimal(…)`), and the tree-walker, the VM, generated Rust and the WebAssembly Node host all report it. A compiled program stopped by a failure prints its reason alone and exits 1 under either panic strategy. `statement_tier_totality_lock`.
- **`decimal(…)` means one thing everywhere.** It reads Text, widens an Int or a big integer exactly, keeps a Decimal, and refuses a Real, a Bool or a Char in one sentence. AOT-Rust read any argument through its text, so `decimal(2.5)` succeeded compiled and failed interpreted, and a big integer did the reverse. `statement_tier_totality_lock`.
- **A sequence stored in 32-bit slots keeps every value the program puts in it.** AOT-Rust narrows a `Seq of Int` to `i32` slots when it proves every stored value fits. A sequence exchanged by `Swap` with one holding wider values, or written inside a zone or another statement the proof did not classify, kept the narrow slots and lost the high bits; sequences a `Swap` exchanges now narrow together or not at all, and every statement that names the sequence is judged. `statement_tier_totality_lock`.
- **Two specializations of a function never share a name.** The partial evaluator named a clone by its constant arguments, so `f(15.0)` and `f(15)`, `f(-0.0)` and `f(0.0)`, or a clone and a function the program declares could collide and run one body for both calls; every name is now distinct. `statement_tier_totality_lock`.
- **`compile_to_c` matches the VM on every row of `c_ast_vm_parity_lock`.**
  - A program the VM refuses before it runs lowers to a C program that fails the same way, before any output, in the VM's words.
  - Value semantics without per-call copies (`codegen_c::sharing`): a binding copies only when a change to the shared buffer can be seen through another name read afterwards; an argument is copied only for a parameter the callee changes while the caller still reads it; a function's result is taken as sharing only the arguments some `Return` of it can reach (narrowed call shapes).
  - Texts: a text appended in place is a builder (`text_builder.c`: length and ASCII-ness in a header, geometric growth, overlap-safe append); a joining is one allocation; `item k of` reads by UTF-8 character, negative from the end, from a table of one-byte texts.
  - Exact integers: `exact` widens `Int` components of tuple and named-record returns and the locals bound from them; function values and closures lifted by defunctionalization (`optimize::is_lifted_closure`) are exact; `x & c` and `x % c` bound their result, so an accumulator masked or reduced stays a machine word, its step checked (`checked_int.c`) with the exact path on a cold branch unless `word_ranges` or the range oracle proves it; `LInt` arithmetic takes an inline machine-word path.
  - Maps and sets: power-of-two capacities probed by mask; removal by backward shift keeps every other key findable and the insertion order; `Remove` from maps and `Add`/`Remove` on record fields; exact keys narrowed on every key arm; a record constructed without a text, set or map field holds an empty one.
  - `Swap` declares its temporary with the operands' C type for every kind; an `Inspect` with no arm for the value fails with `semantics::builtins::inspect_unhandled_message`; a list a counted loop fills is reserved before the loop (`codegen::peephole::counted_push_loop`, now shared with the Rust tier's with-capacity pattern); calls to functions that neither fail nor act are left unordered among themselves.
  - The stepped tier (`compile_to_c_stepped`, the one a GPU thread runs): a `Break` leaves a loop whose condition waits, a `Return` inside a zone leaves the zone, a Text parameter a function that waits grows is its own copy, and a waiting condition is true or false as the VM reads it.
  - The C unit splitter (`codegen_c::cshake`) ends a preprocessor directive at the first newline no backslash escapes, so a macro continued over lines reaches the CUDA device-lift whole.
- **A loop nest runs in tiles only where no run can tell the order apart, and a loop's index checks are lifted only under a guard every tier evaluates alike.** AOT-Rust tiled every triple nest shaped like a matrix product, so a nest that folded into one variable or stored a running count printed a different answer than every other tier; and a loop over a row of a flattened matrix (`item (i * n + j + 1) of c`) asserted its index range in Rust of its own, failing with a sentence no other tier prints. The optimizer now decides whether a reorder is observable (Fourier–Motzkin over the nest's accesses, the Wolf–Lam condition) and runs such a loop as `If P: <the fast clone> Otherwise: <the loop as written>`, where P bounds every name it reads first, then checks every access's corner indices (and, for a reorder, every divisor) once, and cannot itself fail. An index that leaves its sequence fails where the VM fails; the tiled matrix product and the unchecked row loops keep their speed; a Real product stays bit for bit, since each item's own updates keep their order. `## No tile` turns tiling off and `## No oraclehints` the bounds versioning. `statement_tier_totality_lock`.
- Bytecode C says why it indexes a Text without the VM's unchecked selection: a Text is read through its bounds-checked character accessor, and the backend trace records that decline (`optimizer_stage_parity`) instead of leaning on a precedence no backend exhibits.
- **A record field of an exact type compiles.** The checker typed a field declared `Hyperreal` (or `Algebraic`, `ExactReal`, `Hypernatural`, `Ordinal`) as an undeclared user type and refused `a new Box with v 3` on every compiled backend while the interpreter ran it; such a field is now typed as a binding declared one is. The Rust emitter reads a field of `LogosValue` type as an exact operand and fills one with the value model's value of what is given; both C generators start a field left unset at nothing, as the interpreter does. `nonstandard/a_field_of_an_exact_type` (every tier), `exact_on_c_lock`.
- **A list literal whose items differ in kind is refused by both C backends, never misread.** A C sequence holds one element type, taken from the first item, while the VM keeps each item the value it is: the bytecode walker pushed an integer's bits as a real (`[1.4142135623730951, 0.0000…005]`), a text's pointer as an integer, and crashed on an integer read as text; the AST generator widened the integers (`item 3 of [sqrt(2.0), 1, 7] / 2` was 3.5, the VM's is 3) and read `[true, 1]` as `[true, true]`. Both now name such a list at generation; the forms of one integer (a machine word and a big integer) are one kind, and a list holding exact values is the value runtime's list. `c_mixed_list_lock`.
- **Linked WebAssembly orders rationals and adds onto one in place.** A comparison with a `Rational` side was refused by the linked emitter ("comparison of rational values"); it now orders both sides through `logos_rt_rational_cmp`, an `Int` or `BigInt` side widened. `Set q to q + …` on a number the linked runtime holds (a rational, a big integer, a complex number, a residue, a decimal, money, a quantity) was refused as "arithmetic on a non-numeric value"; the in-place `AddAssign` now lowers as the `Add` it names. `wasm_linked_rational_lock`.
- **The C bytecode walker copies a lent value before it changes once it is shared.** `own_target` exempted every `mutable` parameter from copy-on-write, so a lent list stored into a global, a local, a field or a list and then changed in place changed every holder. A `mutable` parameter now starts owned (`own[p] = 1`, the call site's `EnsureOwned` having isolated it), loses that like any owned register when another place reads it in, and copies on its next change; `store_inout_params` hands back whichever list it then holds. `lent_value_lock`.
- **RISC-V hands a `mutable` parameter back.** A function returns each `mutable` parameter's final value in `a2`… beside its own, and the call stores it into the argument register it lent (`inout_params`, `for_each_written`), where the bytecode's copy-out reads it. `lent_value_lock`.
- **RISC-V shows a value of any shape its shim models** (`logos_shaped_str`, `logos_show_shaped`: nested lists, tuples, maps, by their shape tables) and lowers date, moment and span literals (`Kind::Span`, months and days packed in one word).
- **A generic C map family stops on a missing key** (`logos_map_miss`), as the fixed families do; it answered a zeroed value. `map_missing_key_traps_lock`.

### Security

- `concurrency::pnp` keys each cover's MAC from a key region at the top of its half and its keystream from the bottom,
  so no MAC key is ever keystream a known plaintext exposed (a forged cover had authenticated); its ledger reserves
  atomically under a file lock, records before a frame exists, syncs its directory, refuses a record it cannot read,
  and keeps the receive half's high-water key slot; `Connect`/`Listen … with pad` keep that ledger beside the pad, where
  each run had started the pad over; the receive window checks and records a cover under one lock. Affected 0.10.0
  and 0.10.1. Found in an internal audit.
- `concurrency::channel::PqSuite` opens each sequence number once within a 1,024-frame window and refuses a 2^64th seal.
  Affected 0.10.0 and 0.10.1. Found in an internal audit.

## [0.10.0] - 2026-07-08


### Added
- `repl` module: `ReplSession`, the replay-based interactive session behind `largo repl` — accumulates definitions + Main statements, re-runs the composed program through `interpret_for_ui_with_args` (the exact `run --interpret` engine), surfaces output past a high-water mark, and rolls back failing inputs. `source()` is always a valid runnable program. Plus `Interpreter::global_bindings` and `ui_bridge::repl_global_bindings` for `:vars` inspection.
- Register bytecode VM (`vm/`): bytecode compiler, dispatch machine, shared semantics-kernel delegation, and `Int` fast paths bit-identical to the kernel by the wrapping-`i64` spec (pinned by an edge-grid differential).
- Tier-up seam to `logicaffeine-jit`: hot functions compile per call with argument guards and kind-inference; hot Main loops region-tier (OSR-lite) with incoming-dead analysis and per-entry guards. The integer/float subset gates tier-up; anything else fails closed to bytecode.
- **The rustc→LOGOS sourcemap is live.** `codegen_program_mapped` records, for every generated line, the top-level LOGOS statement span that produced it (post-hoc newline counting — no cursor threading through emission; function bodies map to their `## To` span; peephole-fused statements map to the merged span), and walks the AST recording variable origins with ownership roles (`GiveObject` beats `ZoneLocal` beats `ShowObject`… — the move is what a borrow error is about). Previously `SourceMapBuilder` was never called: every translated rustc error had `logos_span: None`.
- `rustc_check(source, cache_dir)` — the flycheck engine: an optimizer-OFF compile front (spans stay 1:1; a check build has no use for optimized output) with prelude-offset translation (stdlib prepend never shifts user spans), `cargo check --message-format=json` in a persistent cache dir, and `translate_diagnostics_all` through the REAL interner and populated map. Fail-loud: a cargo failure that translates to nothing is a `Build` error, never a silent all-clear. Plus `rustc_check_artifacts` (the cargo-free substrate for tests) and `write_cargo_project` (extracted from `compile_to_dir`, now shared).
- `translate_diagnostics_all` — every translatable rustc diagnostic, not just the first (`translate_diagnostics` now delegates).
- `OwnershipChecker::check_program_collect` (+ `OwnershipFinding`) and `EscapeChecker::check_program_collect` — every finding reports with its top-level statement index, and ownership findings carry the index of the statement that MOVED the variable (tracked at the four move sites via a central `mark_moved`); after each finding the variable resets to owned so errors don't cascade. Strict `check_program` on both checkers is unchanged.
- `analysis::check_program_collect` + `IndexedTypeError` — collect EVERY failing top-level statement (with its statement index, mapping 1:1 onto `Parser::stmt_spans()`), instead of bailing at the first; inference continues best-effort so one bad statement does not cascade. The strict fail-fast `check_program` contract is unchanged and compile paths keep using it — this is the IDE substrate.
- **The stdlib is literate.** Every definition across the 12 embedded stdlib modules (158 in all — the crypto suite included) carries a `## Note` doc line above its header; `loader::prelude_module_sources()` exposes the RAW sources so the LSP reads the prose, while `prelude()`/`apply_prelude` strip Note blocks byte-exactly — the runtime prelude is identical to the pre-documentation join, and documentation prose can never mint an auto-import trigger (`module_names` derives from the stripped code). En route, the root `assets/std` copies of `env`/`random`/`time` had silently drifted from the canonical compile-tree copies (`## To native args` vs `## To native args ()`); they're re-synced and a byte-parity lock in `logicaffeine_tests` (`stdlib_asset_sync.rs`) ends that drift class.

### Fixed
- `compile_to_dir` appended `## Requires` dependencies after the `[target.'cfg(linux)'.dependencies]` section of the generated Cargo.toml, silently scoping user crates to Linux; they now land inside `[dependencies]`.

### Changed
- Run-path optimizer: magic-reciprocal division/modulo, run-path recursion inlining, and loop-invariant pointer/length plus constant hoisting on the interpreted execution path.

See the root CHANGELOG for the cross-crate history.

## [0.8.18] - 2026-02-15

### Fixed
- Constant propagation no longer substitutes `Literal::Text` (String) values — `is_literal` → `is_propagatable_literal` excludes non-Copy types to preserve use-after-move detection (E0382)
- Zone-scoped `Let` bindings no longer leak into propagation environment — new `propagate_zone_block` processes zone bodies without registering their bindings, preserving escape analysis (E0597)

## [0.8.17] - 2026-02-15

### Added
- C codegen backend (`codegen_c.rs`) — `compile_to_c()` produces self-contained C files with embedded runtime (Seq, Map, Set, string helpers, IO)
- C runtime: `Seq_i64`, `Seq_bool`, `Seq_str`, `Map_i64_i64` (open-addressing), string helpers, IO
- C keyword escaping: `is_c_reserved()` + `escape_c_ident()` → `logos_` prefix for reserved words
- Constant propagation optimizer pass (`optimize/propagate.rs`) — forward substitution of immutable constants
- Pipeline order: fold → propagate → dce
- `is_simple_expr()` guard for for-range pattern — prevents codegen of complex limit expressions

### Fixed
- For-range guard for complex expressions — `While i is at most length of items` no longer produces `_` in generated Rust
- For-range post-loop value for empty loops — empty loops keep counter at start value using `max(start, limit)`
- Vec-fill pattern relaxed mutability — `Let items be a new Seq of Bool` now matches without explicit `mutable`
- C codegen missing `SetI64`/`SetStr` in `c_type_str()` match
- Interpreter `apply_comparison` now handles Float-Float, Int-Float, Float-Int comparisons

## [0.8.16] - 2026-02-15

### Fixed
- For-range loop emission: switched from `RangeInclusive` (`..=`) to exclusive `Range` (`..`) with `limit + 1`. `RangeInclusive` has per-iteration overhead that caused 41.4% regression in bubble sort's O(n^2) inner loop. Literal limits compute `limit + 1` at compile time.

## [0.8.15] - 2026-02-15

### Added
- For-range loop emission: `Let counter + While counter <= limit + Set counter to counter + 1` → `for counter in start..(limit + 1)` (and `start..limit` for exclusive `<` bounds)
- Post-loop counter value restoration for correct semantics after for-range transformation
- `body_modifies_var` helper for detecting counter modification inside loop bodies (prevents invalid for-range optimization)
- Iterator-based loops: `.iter().copied()` for Copy-type `Vec` iteration instead of `.clone()` when body doesn't mutate the collection
- `body_mutates_collection` helper for recursive mutation detection across nested If/While/Repeat/Zone blocks
- List literal element type inference: `[10, 20, 30]` registers as `Vec<i64>` for direct indexing and Copy-type elision
- Vec fill pattern: `BinaryOpKind::Lt` (exclusive bound) support alongside existing `LtEq`
- Swap pattern: `BinaryOpKind::Eq` and `BinaryOpKind::NotEq` support alongside existing comparison operators

## [0.8.14] - 2026-02-15

### Added
- Deep expression recursion in constant folder — all 26 Expr variants now get sub-expressions folded
- Unreachable-after-return DCE — statements after `Return` truncated from blocks
- Algebraic simplification — identity/annihilator rules for int and float (`x + 0`, `x * 1`, `x * 0`, etc.)
- Maybe type support in codegen — `Maybe` handled as alias for `Option` in all 7 codegen paths

## [0.8.13] - 2026-02-14

### Added
- Accumulator introduction optimization for single non-tail recursive calls with `+` or `*`
- Automatic memoization for pure multi-branch recursive functions with hashable parameters
- Mutual tail call optimization merging paired mutually-recursive functions into a single loop
- Purity analysis pass (`collect_pure_functions`) using two-pass fixed-point propagation
- Helper functions: `count_self_calls`, `is_hashable_type`, `detect_mutual_tce_pairs`, `find_tail_call_targets`

## [0.8.12] - 2026-02-14

Synced to workspace version 0.8.12. See root CHANGELOG for full history.

## [0.6.0] - 2026-01-17

Initial crates.io release.

### Added

- LOGOS compilation pipeline
- Code generation from AST to target output
- Tree-walking interpreter (optional `interpreter-only` feature)
- Refinement type syntax with `where` clauses
- DRS (Discourse Representation Structures) for donkey anaphora
- Event adjective analysis
- Escape analysis for memory safety
- Diagnostic system with source mapping
- Optional `codegen` feature for full compilation
- Optional `verification` feature for Z3 integration
